- [The Role of GitHub Copilot on Software Development: A Perspec-tive on Productivity, Security, Best Practices and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.13199): Surveys Copilot productivity, security, intellectual-property risk, best practices, and adoption guidance, so it is better treated as Survey material.
- [CODEPROMPTZIP: Code-specific Prompt Compression for Retrieval-Augmented Generation in Coding Tasks with LMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14925): Trains a small code compressor with type-aware, priority-driven samples from program-analysis token ablations and a copy mechanism for compressed RAG prompts in assertion generation, repair, and suggestion tasks.
- [Learning to Solve and Verify: A Self-Play Framework for Code and Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.14948): Uses self-play for code and test generation so synthetic coding data are filtered by executable verification.
- [GNN-Coder: Boosting Semantic Code Retrieval with Combined GNNs and Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15202): Combines AST graph neural networks with Transformers for semantic code retrieval, including AST pooling and a mean angular margin measure for embedding separability.
- [DeepRTL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15832): Builds a unified representation model for Verilog understanding and generation, extending code-model coverage to hardware description languages.
- [Improving Deep Assertion Generation via Fine-Tuning Retrieval-Augmented Pre-Trained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16071): Pairs a hybrid lexical and embedding assertion retriever with a pretrained generator to produce unit-test assertions, outperforming prior deep assertion generation baselines.
- [Beyond Trusting Trust: Multi-Model Validation for Robust Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16279): A multi-model validation approach for robust code generation. Core idea: compare independent model outputs to detect anomalous or potentially compromised generated code.
- [Code Summarization Beyond Function Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16704): Studies class-level and repository-level context for code summarization, revising benchmarks and showing how retrieved code chunks can improve summary quality.
- [Renaissance of Literate Programming in the Era of LLMs: Enhancing LLM-Based Code Generation in Large-Scale Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17441): Uses interoperable literate-programming documents and prompt templates to help LLMs generate Scheme and Python across RepoBench-scale project contexts.
- [Thinking Before Running! Efficient Code Generation with Thorough Exploration and Optimal Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17442): Explores code solutions before execution, refines the best candidates, and uses ReST preference optimization on exploration trajectories to lower test-time code-generation cost.
- [SWE-RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18449): Applies reinforcement learning to open software-evolution tasks so coding agents learn from verifiable repository changes.
- [SOK: Exploring Hallucinations and Security Risks in AI-Assisted Software Development with Insights for LLM Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18468): Synthesizes hallucination, insecure-code, bias, data-leak, and intellectual-property risks across Copilot, ChatGPT, Cursor, and Codeium, so it is a Survey candidate.
- [Automated Code Generation and Validation for Software Components of Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18905): Generates STM32F407 hardware-abstraction-layer code with AST analysis and retrieval-augmented generation, then validates integration into embedded applications.
- [Efficient and Universal Neural-Network Decoder for Stabilizer-Based Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.19971): Introduces GraphQEC, a graph-based neural decoder for stabilizer quantum error correction, which is off-axis for software-development code models.
- [UniCodec: Unified Audio Codec with Single Domain-Adaptive Codebook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20067): Off-axis for software-development code models; it builds a unified audio codec with a partitioned domain-adaptive codebook, domain mixture-of-experts, and mask-prediction modeling for speech, music, and sound.
- [muCode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.20380): Trains generators and verifiers for multi-turn code generation from execution feedback using single-step rewards.
- [Discrete Codebook World Models for Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00653): Builds a self-supervised discrete-codebook world model plus model-predictive control for continuous-control RL, which is off-axis unless cross-listed under general agent model learning.
- [ABFS: Natural Robustness Testing for LLM-based NLP Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01319): Formulates prompt-and-example robustness testing as best-first search over natural perturbations, producing deployment tests for LLM-based NLP software.
- [V2 Dial: Unification of Video and Visual Dialog via Multimodal Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02063): Off-axis for Software Development Model; it routes image and video dialog inputs through multimodal experts and contrastive alignment for AVSD and VisDial rather than training a code model.
- [Which Code Statements Implement Privacy Behaviors in Android Applications?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.02091): Fine-tunes LLMs to detect statement-level Android privacy behaviors from programmer-labeled data, moving privacy notice generation below method or file granularity.
- [Unmasking the Genuine Type Inference Capabilities of LLMs for Java Code Snippets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04076): Builds the unreleased ThaliaType benchmark and detects StatType-SO leakage in StarCoder2 training data, making this mainly a Bench or evaluation concern for Java snippet type inference.
- [Multi-modal Summarization in Model-Based Engineering: Automotive Software Development Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04506): Evaluates multimodal LLMs on summarizing UML and EMF diagrams in automotive model-based engineering, focusing on diagram relationships and functionality.
- [LoRACode: LoRA Adapters for Code Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.05315): Fine-tunes task-specific LoRA adapters for CodeBERT and UniXcoder embeddings, using less than two percent trainable parameters for multilingual Code2Code and Text2Code retrieval.
- [SQLCritic: Correcting Text-to-SQL Generation via Clause-wise Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07996): Trains a clause-wise critique model with adaptive-DPO and automatically curated critique data to localize and correct syntax and semantic errors in Text-to-SQL.
- [Investigating Execution-Aware Language Models for Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08228): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [DIV-FF: Dynamic Image-Video Feature Fields For Environment Understanding in Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08344): Off-axis for software development; it decomposes egocentric scenes into persistent, dynamic, and actor components using image-video feature fields for long-term scene understanding.
- [GraphSense: Graph Embedding Based Code Suggestion Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.08648): Uses graph embeddings for lightweight local IDE next-line code suggestions, targeting real-time assistance where GPT-style models are too slow or resource-intensive.
- [Commenting Higher-level Code Unit: Full Code, Reduced Code, or Hierarchical Code Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10737): Compares full-code, reduced-code, and hierarchical-code inputs for higher-level code-unit commenting, informing code summarization models.
- [Tikzero: Zero-Shot Text-Guided Graphics Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11509): Synthesizes editable TikZ-style graphics programs from text through image-mediated program generation.
- [Your Text Encoder Can Be an Object-Level Watermarking Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.11945): Off-axis image-generation watermarking work; it fine-tunes only text-token embeddings to control object-level invisible watermarks in latent diffusion models.
- [PredicateFix: Repairing Static Analysis Alerts with Bridging Predicates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12205): Retrieves clean-code examples via static-analysis rule predicates and feeds them to an RAG repair pipeline for CodeQL and GoInsight alerts.
- [LLM Test Generation via Iterative Hybrid Program Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13580): Panta combines static control-flow analysis, dynamic coverage feedback, and iterative LLM prompting to target uncovered execution paths in unit-test generation.
- [Inference-Time Intervention in Large Language Models for Reliable Requirement Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14130): Steers requirement-verification outputs by intervening on a few specialized attention heads during inference over graph-encoded MBSE models, improving precision over fine-tuning.
- [UTFix: Change Aware Unit Test Repairing using LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14924): Repairs stale unit tests by giving LMs static slices, dynamic slices, and failure messages after focal-method changes, covering assertion failures and coverage loss.
- [aiXcoder-7B-v2: Training LLMs to Fully Utilize the Long Context in Repository-level Code Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15301): Trains aiXcoder-style models on COLA-132K long cross-file completion examples so repository-level completion learns to attend to APIs and similar spans across 128K context.
- [LLM-Aided Customizable Profiling of Code Data Based On Programming Language Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15571): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [Human or LLM? A Comparative Study on Accessible Code Generation Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15885): Compares GPT-4o and Qwen2.5-Coder web-code accessibility, then uses feedback-driven ReAct repair to improve ARIA, color contrast, and alt-text issues.
- [HDLCoRe: A Training-Free Framework for Mitigating Hallucinations in LLM-Generated HDL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16528): Mitigates HDL hallucinations without fine-tuning by combining HDL-aware chain-of-thought self-verification with heterogeneous RAG over extracted components and reranked examples.
- [Code Evolution Graphs: Understanding Large Language Model Driven Design of Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16668): Uses code evolution graphs to analyze how LLM-generated algorithms change across iterative optimization.
- [Enabling Global, Human-Centered Explanations for LLMs:From Tokens to Interpretable Code and Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.16771): Introduces CodeQ, mapping token-level rationales to programming categories and aggregating them into global code explanations that expose shallow syntactic cues in code LMs.
- [A Study on the Improvement of Code Generation Quality Using Large Language Models Leveraging Product Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.17837): Generates end-to-end test code from product manuals, FAQs, and tutorials with tailored prompts that first infer documentation intent and then emit executable tests.
- [SEAlign: Alignment Training for Software Engineering Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18455): Aligns code models for real SWE tasks by using high-quality workflow steps, Monte Carlo tree search over multi-step decisions, and preference optimization on critical actions.
- [Coding Malware in Fancy Programming Languages for Fun and Profit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.19058): Shows static malware detectors can miss shellcode after translation into less-used programming languages, making this a security-evasion robustness study rather than code-model training.
- [GenEdit: Compounding Operators and Continuous Improvement to Tackle Text-to-SQL in the Enterprise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.21602): Describes an enterprise Text-to-SQL system with compounding generation operators, a company-specific knowledge set, feedback-driven edits, and regression-tested knowledge merges.
- [RocketPPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.21971): Uses an LLM plus mixture-of-experts regressors for code-level power, performance, and area estimation in HDL, supporting hardware-code design feedback.
- [Code Structural Knowledge for Code Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22776): Injects code-structure knowledge into LLM code translation through in-context learning, adding a structure-aware code-model adaptation route.
- [CCCI: Code Completion with Contextual Information for Complex Data Transfer Tasks Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.23231): Incorporates contextual information such as variable scope, object models, APIs, and database relations to improve LLM code completion for complex data-transfer tasks.
- [Enhancing LLMs in Long Code Translation through Instrumentation and Program State Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02017): Introduces LongTrans and PAST, using instrumentation and program-state alignment so translated C++, Java, and Python programs preserve execution behavior across long code.
- [VoiceCraft-Dub: Automated Video Dubbing with Neural Codec Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.02386): Off-axis for Software Development Model; it extends neural codec language models with facial-feature adapters, audio-visual fusion, and CelebV-Dub for synchronized video dubbing.
- [Expectations vs Reality - A Secondary Study on AI Adoption in Software Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04921): Survey candidate; systematically maps 2020+ industry studies on AI adoption in software testing, extracting use cases, benefits, and adoption barriers.
- [BRIDGES: Bridging Graph Modality and Large Language Models within EDA Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05180): Generates RTL and netlist graph data, then uses a lightweight cross-modal projector to feed graph representations into LLM prompts for EDA tasks without changing the base LLM.
- [Identifying and Replicating Code Patterns Driving Performance Regressions in Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05851): Mines real performance-regression code changes to extend mutation operators for performance testing and evaluate whether those mutants degrade runtime or memory on real systems.
- [ARLO: A Tailorable Approach for Transforming Natural Language Software Requirements into Architecture using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.06143): Maps natural-language requirements to architecture choices by extracting architecturally relevant requirements with an LLM and solving the resulting choice matrix with integer linear programming.
- [R2E-Gym](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.07164): Uses SYNGEN to synthesize issue-like executable environments and hybrid verifiers for training and test-time scaling of open-weight SWE agents.
- [From Token to Line: Enhancing Code Generation with a Long-Term Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.07433): Uses LSR-MCTS to search line by line rather than token by token, adding self-refinement at tree nodes to correct errors and diversify generated programs.
- [Program Skeletons for Automated Program Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.07483): Uses high-level program skeletons to preserve source behavior while abstracting away language-specific details during automated translation.
- [Zero-Shot Cross-Domain Code Search without Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.07740): Decomposes query-code matching to improve cross-domain code search without task-specific fine-tuning, adding a model-side code-retrieval route.
- [Towards an Understanding of Context Utilization in Code Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08734): Survey candidate; reviews 146 studies to taxonomize context sources, integration strategies, tasks, and evaluation practices in code intelligence.
- [RTLRepoCoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08862): Combines fine-tuning and retrieval augmentation for repository-level RTL code completion, adding a hardware-code model line to software development.
- [NetTAG: A Multimodal RTL-and-Layout-Aligned Netlist Foundation Model via Text-Attributed Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09260): Pretrains a netlist foundation model by combining LLM-encoded gate semantics with graph transformers, plus RTL and layout alignment for functional and physical EDA tasks.
- [The Code Barrier: What LLMs Actually Understand?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10557): Uses controlled Java code obfuscation over CodeNet-derived problems to test whether LLMs can describe and deobfuscate code beyond token-pattern recognition.
- [QualiTagger: Automating software quality detection in issue trackers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11053): Trains Transformer taggers on a large GitHub issue dataset to detect software quality attributes and security labels in issue-tracker text.
- [Proof-Carrying Neuro-Symbolic Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12031): Connects code generation with proof-carrying neuro-symbolic verification, adding a model-side route where generated programs carry formal evidence rather than only passing tests.
- [Automated Generation of Commit Messages in Software Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12998): Trains and compares TF-IDF, Word2Vec, logistic-regression, and LSTM models on code-change and commit-message pairs to generate concise repository commit messages.
- [ApiRAT: Integrating Multi-source API Knowledge for Enhanced Code Translation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14852): Integrates API sequence retrieval, back-translation, and API mapping to improve LLM-based code translation when cross-language library calls differ.
- [StyleMe3D: Stylization with Disentangled Priors by Multiple Encoders on 3D Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.15281): Off-axis 3D generation work; uses Dynamic Style Score Distillation plus CLIP and VGG alignment to stylize 3D Gaussians while preserving geometry.
- [Optimizing Token Consumption in LLMs: A Nano Surge Approach for Code Reasoning Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.15989): Reduces chain-of-thought token cost in automated code repair with context awareness, responsibility tuning, and cost-sensitive inference, preserving repair quality while suppressing unnecessary reasoning tokens.
- [Neuro-symbolic Static Analysis with LLM-generated Vulnerability Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.16057): Generates vulnerability-detection patterns with LLMs, then iteratively corrects them using trace-driven symbolic validation over pattern DSLs for C, Java, PHP, and JavaScript.
- [GENCNIPPET: Automated Generation of Code Snippets for Supporting Programming Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.16292): Fine-tunes code models on Stack Overflow question and snippet pairs so missing Python and Java examples can be generated during question submission.
- [High-Fidelity And Complex Test Data Generation For Google SQL Code Generation Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.17203): Uses LLM-assisted generation plus pre- and post-processing to create syntactically valid, semantically coherent mock data for nested Google SQL workloads and NL2SQL testing.
- [ThinkFL: Self-Refining Failure Localization for Microservice Systems via Reinforcement Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.18776): Applies progressive multi-stage GRPO fine-tuning so lightweight LLMs learn reasoning and self-refinement trajectories for microservice failure localization.
- [Can We Enhance Bug Report Quality Using LLMs?: An Empirical Study of LLM-Based Bug Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.18804): Instruction-tunes Qwen, Mistral, and Llama models to convert informal bug reports into structured templates and evaluates missing-field detection and cross-project generalization.
- [Large Language Models Are Qualified Benchmark Builders: Rebuilding Pre-Training Datasets for Advancing Code Intelligence Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19444): Rebuilds code pretraining corpora by replacing stale human comments with LLM-generated code comments, then re-pretrains CodeT5 for summarization, generation, and translation.
- [Evaluate-and-Purify: Fortifying Code Language Models Against Adversarial Attacks Using LLM-as-a-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19730): Uses LLM-as-a-judge to detect and purify adversarial identifier-substitution examples for code language models, adding a model-side robustness defense for AI-for-code systems.
- [Using LLMs in Generating Design Rationale for Software Architecture Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20781): Evaluates five LLMs on 100 architecture-decision problems from Stack Overflow, GitHub issues, and GitHub discussions, comparing zero-shot, chain-of-thought, and agent prompting against expert design-rationale ground truth.
- [Secure coding with AI - from detection to repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20814): Analyzes DevGPT C, C++, and C# snippets for vulnerabilities, then evaluates GPT-4.1, GPT-5, and Claude Opus 4.1 on CWE identification and repair.
- [SWE-smith](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.21798) ([open-source code](https://github.com/SWE-bench/SWE-smith)): Scales synthetic SWE training data and trains SWE-agent-LM-32B, complementing its environment-setup role with a direct model-training contribution.
- [MCMComm: Hardware-Software Co-Optimization for End-to-End Communication in Multi-Chip-Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.00041): Hardware-software co-design work for chiplet communication, combining congestion-aware analysis, diagonal links, workload partitioning, genetic algorithms, and MIQP.
- [CoRAC: Integrating Selective API Document Retrieval with Question Semantic Intent for Code Question Answering](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.naacl-long.628): Integrates selective API-document retrieval with question intent modeling for code question answering.
- [Identifying Root Cause of Bugs by Capturing Changed Code Lines With Relational Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.00990): Uses relational graph convolution over changed lines in bug-fixing commits to detect root-cause deleted lines, evaluated across 87 open-source projects.
- [Detecting the Root Cause Code Lines in Bug-Fixing Commits by Heterogeneous Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01022): Builds heterogeneous bug-fixing graphs from syntax and dependency relations, then uses attention and gated cross-line semantics to identify root-cause code lines.
- [DRAFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01307): Uses document retrieval-augmented fine-tuning for safety-critical software assessment, connecting regulatory evidence retrieval with model adaptation.
- [Automatic Generation of PLC Code Based on Finetuned Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/ieeeconf65522.2025.11136984): Centers on fine-tuned language models for PLC code generation, treating industrial control logic synthesis as a software-model adaptation problem.
- [CodeSSM: Towards State Space Models for Code Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01475): Trains a state-space model on code corpora for retrieval, classification, and clone detection, testing longer-context extrapolation and memory savings versus Transformers.
- [CircuitFusion: Multimodal Circuit Representation Learning for Agile Chip Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.02168): Fuses hardware code, structural graphs, and functionality summaries with self-supervised circuit tasks and retrieval-augmented inference for chip-design prediction tasks.
- [LAMeD: LLM-generated Annotations for Memory Leak Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.02376): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for software-engineering agents and code models. Core idea: focus on llm-generated annotations for memory leak detection as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [Can Large Language Models Predict Parallel Code Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.03988): Frames GPU-kernel performance prediction as roofline classification and tests zero-shot, few-shot, profiling-aware, and fine-tuned LLM settings on CUDA and OpenMP kernels.
- [Identification and Optimization of Redundant Code Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04040): Agent Harness proposal; uses LLMs to detect and optimize redundant code in AI projects while studying recurring redundancy patterns and developer causes.
- [Chain-of-Thought Tokens are Computer Program Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.04955): Off-axis model-interpretability study showing CoT tokens can act like variable-like intermediate state in multiplication and dynamic-programming tasks.
- [Towards Mitigating API Hallucination in Code Generated by LLMs with Hierarchical Dependency Aware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.05057): Introduces MARIN, which mines local and global project dependencies and applies dependency-constrained decoding so generated APIs are checked against project-specific availability rather than retrieved as flat documentation snippets.
- [Spec2Assertion: Automatic Pre-RTL Assertion Generation using Large Language Models with Progressive Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.07995): Generates pre-RTL SystemVerilog assertions from design specs using LLM progressive regularization and chain-of-thought prompting, with a broad assertion-quality evaluation.
- [LEGO: A Layout Expression Language for Code Generation of Hierarchical Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08091): Defines layout-independent computation plus layout expressions and integrates with Triton, MLIR, and CUDA to generate hierarchical GPU indexing and data-movement code.
- [Enhancing Code Quality with Generative AI: Boosting Developer Warning Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11677): Uses LLMs to simplify static-analysis warnings, explain severity and vulnerability implications, and suggest fixes so developers are more likely to act on warnings.
- [JOLT-SQL: Joint Loss Tuning of Text-to-SQL with Confusion-aware Noisy Schema Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.14305): Jointly optimizes schema linking and SQL generation in one SFT objective with local bidirectional attention and confusion-aware noisy schema sampling.
- [Success is in the Details: Evaluate and Enhance Details Sensitivity of Code LLMs through Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.14597): Builds counterfactual code-sensitivity data and CTF-Instruct, an incremental fine-tuning framework that selects examples by difficulty, diversity, and sensitivity.
- [Code Graph Model (CGM): A Graph-Integrated Large Language Model for Repository-Level Software Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16901): Graph-integrated LLM for repository-level software engineering tasks.
- [Style2Code: A Style-Controllable Code Generation Framework with Dual-Modal Contrastive Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.19442): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for software-development capability. Core idea: Controllable code generation, the ability to synthesize code that follows a specified style while maintaining functionality, remains a challenging task.
- [An Empirical Study on Strong-Weak Model Collaboration for Repo-level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.20182): Studies strong-weak model collaboration for repository-level code generation, relevant to model-side coding-agent composition.
- [iDSE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22086): Uses LLMs for design-space exploration in high-level synthesis, extending software-development model coverage into hardware-code optimization workflows.
- [Training Language Models to Generate Quality Code with Program Analysis Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22704): Uses program-analysis findings and unit tests as scalable reinforcement signals for production-quality code generation.
- [Infinite-Instruct: Synthesizing Scaling Code instruction Data with Bidirectional Synthesis and Static Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23177): Synthesizes scalable code-instruction data with bidirectional construction and static verification, adding a data-generation recipe for stronger code LMs.
- [Satori-SWE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23604) ([code](https://github.com/satori-reasoning/Satori-SWE)): Combines evolutionary test-time scaling and RL-based self-evolution so smaller SWE models can improve repairs with fewer samples.
- [Linear Layouts: Robust Code Generation of Efficient Tensor Computation Using F_2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23819): Models tensor layouts as binary matrices over F2, integrates the abstraction with Triton, and avoids the layout-conversion explosion in efficient tensor code generation.
- [Fine-Tune an SLM or Prompt an LLM? The Case of Generating Low-Code Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24189): Compares fine-tuned small language models with prompted large models for generating low-code workflows, clarifying when workflow synthesis needs model adaptation versus prompting.
- [A Reward-driven Automated Webshell Malicious-code Generator for Red-teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24252): Builds RAWG, a reward-driven webshell generator that categorizes obfuscation types, normalizes malicious-code tokens, and uses SFT plus PPO for diverse red-team payloads.
- [CoRet: Improved Retriever for Code Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24715): Trains a dense retriever for code editing with repository structure and call-graph dependencies, improving SWE-bench and Long Code Arena bug-localization recall.
- [Veritas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00005): Converts LLM-generated conjunctive-normal-form specifications into deterministic Verilog synthesis, extending hardware-code generation beyond free-form HDL prompting.
- [CRScore++: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Tool and AI Feedback for Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00296): Combines verifiable tool feedback and AI feedback for reinforcement learning of code-review comment generation.
- [How Programming Concepts and Neurons Are Shared in Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01074): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for software-engineering agents and code models. Core idea: focus on how programming concepts and neurons are shared in code language models as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [AdaRewriter: Unleashing the Power of Prompting-based Conversational Query Reformulation via Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01381): Off-axis for software development unless routed to search; trains a lightweight outcome-supervised reward model with contrastive ranking for test-time query reformulation.
- [Exploring Prompt Patterns in AI-Assisted Code Generation: Towards Faster and More Effective Developer-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01604): Developer-workflow study mining DevGPT for seven prompt patterns and measuring which patterns reduce interaction rounds in AI-assisted code generation.
- [Backdoors in Code Summarizers: How Bad Is It?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.01825): Systematically studies data, model, and inference factors for poisoning code summarizers, showing even tiny absolute numbers of poisoned samples can implant backdoors.
- [Co-Evolving LLM Coder and Unit Tester via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03136) ([code](https://github.com/Gen-Verse/CURE)): Co-trains coder and unit-tester capabilities from interaction outcomes, making the tester a supervision source for agentic coding.
- [Seed-Coder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03524) ([code](https://github.com/ByteDance-Seed/Seed-Coder)): Builds an 8B code-model family with model-centric code-data filtering, SFT, preference optimization, and Long-CoT RL for code reasoning and software tasks.
- [Auto prompt sql: a resource-efficient architecture for text-to-sql translation in constrained environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03598): Builds AP-SQL with schema filtering, retrieval of in-context examples, prompt-driven schema linking, and CoT or GoT templates for resource-constrained Text-to-SQL.
- [VisCoder: Fine-Tuning LLMs for Executable Python Visualization Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.03930): Fine-tunes LLMs for executable Python visualization code generation.
- [Leveraging Reward Models for Guiding Code Review Comment Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04464): Uses reinforcement learning for code-review comment generation, rewarding comments for semantic match and for usefulness to a downstream code-refinement model.
- [Cracking the Code: Enhancing Implicit Hate Speech Detection through Coding Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04693): Off-axis NLP classification work that uses coding-style labels to improve implicit hate-speech detection, not a software-development model contribution.
- [Zero-Shot Detection of LLM-Generated Code via Approximated Task Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06069): Detects LLM-generated code zero-shot by approximating the original task prompt and measuring token entropy under task-conditioned distributions across languages.
- [SWE-Dev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.07636): Builds SWE agents through supervised data construction, reinforcement learning, and inference scaling.
- [CUDA-LLM: LLMs Can Write Efficient CUDA Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09092): Introduces Feature Search and Reinforcement for CUDA generation, jointly optimizing compilation, functional correctness, and actual GPU kernel latency through iterative LLM refinement.
- [Taming SQL Complexity: LLM-Based Equivalence Evaluation for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09359): Uses LLMs to judge weak and semantic SQL equivalence for Text-to-SQL outputs, covering cases where exact string or execution matching misses valid answers.
- [Expert-in-the-Loop Systems with Cross-Domain and in-Domain Few-Shot Learning for Software Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10104): Human-in-the-loop vulnerability workflow using zero- and few-shot CWE classification with confidence-based routing to experts rather than pure model training.
- [D-LiFT: Improving LLM-based Decompiler Backend via Code Quality-driven Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10125): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for software-engineering agents and code models. Core idea: focus on improving llm-based decompiler backend via code quality-driven fine-tuning as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [Augmenting Large Language Models with Static Code Analysis for Automated Code Quality Improvements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10330): Combines static-analysis findings, retrieval over supporting evidence, and a code-comparison interface to guide LLM fixes for code-quality problems.
- [MLLM-Based UI2Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10376): Uses UI layout information to guide multimodal models in generating interface code.
- [Not One to Rule Them All: Mining Meaningful Code Review Orders From GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10654): Empirical study of 23,241 pull requests that mines useful review file orders such as largest-diff-first, semantic-to-title, and tests-first.
- [Formalising Software Requirements using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10704): VERIFAI project note using NLP, ontologies, similarity-based artifact reuse, and LLMs to generate formal specifications and trace requirements into verification.
- [DCE-LLM: Dead Code Elimination with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.11076): Applies large language models to dead-code elimination, adding a code-optimization task beyond generation.
- [SAGE:Specification-Aware Grammar Extraction for Automated Test Case Generation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.11081): Fine-tunes open LLMs to translate specifications into counter-augmented grammars, then applies GRPO with verifiable rewards and iterative feedback for test-case generation.
- [Agent-RLVR: Training Software Engineering Agents via Guidance and Environment Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.11425): A harness for environment-reward reinforcement learning for software-engineering agents. Core idea: expose workflow logic outside the base model so planning, tool use, memory, verification, recovery, or orchestration can be studied and reused.
- [An Empirical study on LLM-based Log Retrieval for Software Engineering Metadata Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.11659): Agent Harness candidate; evaluates LLM-assisted natural-language retrieval over software-engineering metadata and logs rather than training a code model.
- [Mastering Da Vinci Code: A Comparative Study of Transformer, LLM, and PPO-based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.12801): Off-axis game-reasoning study comparing Transformer, LLM, and PPO agents for the Da Vinci Code board game.
- [From Empirical Evaluation to Context-Aware Enhancement: Repairing Regression Errors with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13182): Builds RegMiner4APR for Java regression bugs and shows LLM-based repair improves when bug-inducing change context is included.
- [DesignCoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13663): Generates UI code with hierarchy-aware and self-correcting LLM mechanisms, extending visual-to-code and frontend code-model coverage.
- [Issue Retrieval and Verification Enhanced Supplementary Code Comment Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14649): Combines issue retrieval and verification for supplementary code-comment generation.
- [Uncovering Intention Through LLM-Driven Code Snippet Description Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.15453): Uses LLM-driven code-snippet description generation to uncover developer intention.
- [Reassessing Code Authorship Attribution in the Era of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17120): Empirical code-stylometry study reassessing authorship attribution when LLM-generated code changes the distribution of human coding style.
- [Large Language Model Unlearning for Source Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17125): A source-code unlearning method for large language models. Core idea: remove memorized or risky code snippets while reducing spillover damage to general programming ability.
- [I Know Which LLM Wrote Your Code Last Summer: LLM generated Code Stylometry for Authorship Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17323): Releases CodeT5-Authorship and LLM-AuthorBench, using an encoder-only CodeT5 classifier over 32,000 compilable C programs to attribute generated code to source LLMs.
- [Context-Aware CodeLLM Eviction for AI-assisted Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18796): Proposes CACE for self-hosted CodeLLM serving, evicting models with context signals such as load time, latency sensitivity, expected output length, recent use, and sliding-window demand instead of simple recency.
- [Mix-of-Language-Experts Architecture for Multilingual Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18923): Mix-of-Language-Experts for multilingual programming is a code-model architecture contribution.
- [From Codicology to Code: A Comparative Study of Transformer and YOLO-based Detectors for Layout Analysis in Historical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20326): Off-axis computer-vision study comparing Transformer and YOLO detectors for historical-document layout analysis; "code" refers to codicology rather than programming.
- [DiffuCoder: Understanding and Improving Masked Diffusion Models for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20639): Masked diffusion language-model method specialized for code generation.
- [XY-Tokenizer: Mitigating the Semantic-Acoustic Conflict in Low-Bitrate Speech Codecs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23325): Off-axis speech-codec model; it uses multi-stage, multi-task learning to balance semantic text alignment with acoustic reconstruction at low bitrates.
- [AlphaVerus](https://proceedings.mlr.press/v267/aggarwal25a.html): Bootstraps formally verified code generation through self-improving translation and tree refinement, making proof-oriented feedback part of code-model improvement.
- [NextCoder](https://proceedings.mlr.press/v267/aggarwal25b.html): Adapts code language models to diverse code edits, strengthening the edit-distribution coverage needed by patching and coding-assistant agents.
- [Reasoning Through Execution](https://proceedings.mlr.press/v267/yu25f.html): Unifies process and outcome rewards for code generation by grounding intermediate reasoning and final success in execution feedback.
- [Revisiting Chain-of-Thought in Code Generation](https://proceedings.mlr.press/v267/liu25ah.html): Studies whether code models need explicit reasoning learning before generation, informing model-side code-reasoning training choices.
- [Synthesizing Software Engineering Data in a Test-Driven Manner](https://proceedings.mlr.press/v267/zhang25cn.html): Generates SWE training data around tests so synthetic examples carry executable validation signals rather than only natural-language issue descriptions.
- [Generating OpenAPI Specifications from Online API Documentation with Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.acl-industry.18): Agent Harness candidate; combines LLM extraction with rule-based validation and repair to convert online API documentation into valid OpenAPI specs for tool use.
- [CodePRM: Execution Feedback-enhanced Process Reward Model for Code Generation](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.findings-acl.428): Builds a code-specific process reward model from thought traces labeled by derived code pass rates, code snippets, and execution feedback, then uses it as a verifier inside a Generate-Verify-Refine code-search pipeline.
- [Structural Code Search using Natural Language Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.02107): Agent Harness and tool candidate; translates natural-language developer queries into Semgrep or GQL structural queries and evaluates 400 queries across ten Java projects.
- [Model Collapse Is Not a Bug but a Feature in Machine Unlearning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.04219): Off-axis general unlearning work that reframes post-unlearning model collapse as useful for removal and privacy rather than code-specific modeling.
- [OASBuilder: Generating OpenAPI Specifications from Online API Documentation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05316): Agent Harness candidate; turns online API documentation into valid OpenAPI specs through LLM extraction, rules, and validity-oriented post-processing.
- [AutoTriton: Automatic Triton Programming with Reinforcement Learning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05687): Turns software development capability into an executable or orchestrated workflow rather than a single prompt.
- [SQLBarber: A System Leveraging Large Language Models to Generate Customized and Realistic SQL Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.06192): Generates realistic SQL workloads by deriving template specs and cost distributions from Redshift and Snowflake statistics, then self-correcting and pruning templates by execution cost.
- [Iceberg: Enhancing HLS Modeling with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.09948): Uses synthetic data to improve high-level-synthesis modeling, making hardware-code performance prediction and optimization less dependent on scarce labeled HLS examples.
- [Compute Requirements for Algorithmic Innovation in Frontier AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.10618): Model-scaling and policy analysis estimating development compute for 36 pretraining innovations in Llama 3 and DeepSeek-V3, including compute-cap counterfactuals.
- [Function-to-Style Guidance of LLMs for Code Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11083): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for software-engineering agents and code models. Core idea: focus on function-to-style guidance of llms for code translation as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [Modeling Code: Is Text All You Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11467): Combines text-based code LLMs with structured program representations so models can reason over control-flow and data-flow properties that plain token sequences miss.
- [MetaLint: Easy-to-Hard Generalization for Code Linting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11687): Frames code linting as instruction following over natural-language best practices, enabling generalization to unseen or evolving rules.
- [CRABS: A syntactic-semantic pincer strategy for bounding LLM interpretation of Python notebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11742): Bounds LLM notebook interpretation with AST-derived lower and upper I/O estimates, then resolves ambiguities to recover cell data-flow and execution-dependency graphs.
- [Extremal Testing for Network Software using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11898): Agent Harness and testing candidate; asks LLMs for protocol constraints and violating inputs for HTTP, BGP, and DNS, uncovering network-software bugs.
- [Chain-of-Descriptions: Improving Code LLMs for VHDL Code Generation and Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12308): Improves VHDL code generation and summarization by inserting intermediate descriptions of problem statements or code before the final LLM output.
- [LLM-Powered Quantum Code Transpilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.12480): Uses LLMs as rule-free transpilers between quantum SDKs, aiming to preserve functional equivalence across Qiskit, Cirq, PennyLane, and related platforms.
- [VerilogDB: The Largest, Highest-Quality Dataset with a Preprocessing Framework for LLM-based RTL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13369): Builds VerilogDB and preprocessing infrastructure for LLM-based RTL generation.
- [Demystifying Feature Requests: Leveraging LLMs to Refine Feature Requests in Open-Source Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13555): Uses LLMs to detect ambiguity and incompleteness in open-source feature requests and generate clarification questions, then compares the refinements with human annotations on real GitHub requests.
- [CUDA-L1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.14111): Applies contrastive reinforcement learning to CUDA optimization, representing a specialized execution-feedback training line for performance-oriented coding agents.
- [On the Effect of Token Merging on Pre-trained Models for Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.14423): Merges hidden representations of identifier subtokens in pretrained code models to reduce FLOPs, testing CodeBERT, GraphCodeBERT, UniXCoder, and CodeT5 variants across SE tasks.
- [Enhancing Repository-Level Code Generation with Call Chain-Aware Multi-View Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.14791): Uses call-chain-aware multi-view context for repository-level code generation, improving how retrieved code structure is represented in prompts.
- [Re:Form - Reducing Human Priors in Scalable Formal Software Verification with RL in LLMs: A Preliminary Study on Dafny](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.16331): Uses reinforcement learning with LLMs to reduce human priors in scalable Dafny formal verification, adding a proof-oriented software model-training line.
- [ACT: Bridging the Gap in Code Translation through Synthetic Data Generation & Adaptive Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.16478): Synthetic data generation and adaptive training for code translation fits software-development Model.
- [Can LLMs Write CI? a Study on Automatic Generation of GitHub Actions Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.17165): Empirically studies whether LLMs can generate correct GitHub Actions workflow YAML, making it a CI-generation evaluation rather than new model training.
- [CodeReasoner: Enhancing the Code Reasoning Ability with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.17548): Contributes a code-reasoning training method; core idea: improve software-development reasoning through reinforcement or verifier-style optimization.
- [GenAI for Automotive Software Development: From Requirements to Wheels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.18223): Agent Harness candidate; converts automotive requirements into metamodel/OCL artifacts, RAG-supported test scenarios, Python simulation code, and C++ target-platform code.
- [Scenethesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.18625): Guides 3D software synthesis with a constraint-expressive intermediate representation, enabling traceable requirements-to-executable-scene generation.
- [SelfRACG: Enabling LLMs to Self-Express and Retrieve for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19033): Lets LLMs express their own information needs for retrieval-augmented code generation, narrowing the gap between retrieved snippets and the next code fragment.
- [Mut4All: Fuzzing Compilers via LLM-Synthesized Mutators Learned from Bug Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19275): Compiler-fuzzing harness that learns LLM-synthesized mutators from bug reports to generate compiler stress tests.
- [Learning to Align Human Code Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.20109): Trains models to align with human code preferences, complementing functional correctness with developer preference signals.
- [CodeNER: Code Prompting for Named Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.20423): Off-axis NLP work that encodes BIO labeling rules as code-style prompts to improve multilingual named-entity recognition with LLMs.
- [Kimi K2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.20534): Reports an open MoE model with large-scale agentic data synthesis and joint reinforcement learning, including strong SWE-bench and agentic task results.
- [Generating Project-Specific Test Cases with Requirement Validation Intention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.20619): IntentionTest retrieves reusable project tests and edits them with an LLM according to validation intentions, improving mutant killing, shared coverage, and passing tests.
- [HLSDebugger: Identification and Correction of Logic Bugs in HLS Code with LLM Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.21485): Identifies and repairs logic bugs in high-level-synthesis code with LLM-generated fixes, extending repair workflows into hardware-design code.
- [Fine-Tuning Code Language Models to Detect Cross-Language Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.21954): Builds cross-language bug data and fine-tunes code models to detect interactions across Python, C/C++, and Java components.
- [SparseRecon: Neural Implicit Surface Reconstruction from Sparse Views with Feature and Depth Consistencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.00366): Off-axis 3D reconstruction method using volume-rendered feature consistency and uncertainty-guided depth constraints for sparse-view surface recovery.
- [SPENCER: Self-Adaptive Model Distillation for Efficient Code Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.00546): Code-retrieval model that pairs dual-encoder candidate narrowing with cross-encoder reranking and adaptive distillation to cut inference time while preserving accuracy.
- [HyClone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.01357): combines LLM semantic screening with execution validation for clone detection, making code semantics and runtime behavior jointly modelled.
- [Seed Diffusion: A Large-Scale Diffusion Language Model with High-Speed Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02193): Introduces a high-speed discrete-diffusion language model with competitive code-evaluation performance.
- [TreeRanker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02455): Ranks IDE code suggestions in a fast, model-agnostic way, making code-completion selection a software-assistant model component.
- [AnalogCoder-Pro: Unifying Analog Circuit Generation and Optimization via Multi-modal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02518): Uses multimodal LLMs for analog-circuit code generation and optimization, extending code-model coverage to EDA artifacts with circuit-specific feedback.
- [Automated Code Repair for C/C++ Static Analysis Alerts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02820): Engineering experience report on an APR tool that triages and repairs true-positive C/C++ static-analysis alerts, reducing manual review of security-relevant diagnostics.
- [Tool-integrated Reinforcement Learning for Repo Deep Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03012): A tool-integrated harness for repository issue localization. Core idea: train agents to use repository retrieval tools through multi-step reasoning and navigation.
- [Training Long-Context, Multi-Turn Software Engineering Agents with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03501): Applies rejection fine-tuning and DAPO-style RL to Qwen2.5-72B for long-context, stateful SWE environments.
- [SAGE-HLS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03558): Uses syntax-aware AST guidance for LLM-based high-level synthesis code generation, adding a hardware-code model route beyond ordinary software snippets.
- [LaTCoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03560): Uses Layout-as-Thought to make webpage design-to-code generation preserve layout structure rather than translating screenshots to code in one opaque step.
- [EVOC2RUST: A Skeleton-guided Framework for Project-Level C-to-Rust Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04295): Uses a skeleton-guided framework for project-level C-to-Rust translation, extending model-side code-migration methods.
- [Agnostics: Learning to Code in Any Programming Language via Reinforcement with a Universal Learning Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.04865): Uses a language-agnostic verifier and RLVR pipeline to train code models across low-resource programming languages through observable I/O behavior.
- [ReCode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05170): Trains reasoning-process reward models from synthesized reasoning variants and gates RL rewards with execution correctness to reduce reward hacking in code generation.
- [Impact-driven Context Filtering For Cross-file Code Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05970): Selects cross-file context by estimated impact for code completion, targeting repository-scale context efficiency.
- [GLM-4.5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06471): Presents open agentic, reasoning, and coding foundation models trained with multi-stage post-training and reinforcement learning for software-engineering and agent tasks.
- [Integrating Rules and Semantics for LLM-Based C-to-Rust Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.06926): Improves C-to-Rust translation with rule-augmented retrieval and semantic analysis.
- [Is GAN Necessary for Mel-Spectrogram-Based Neural Vocoder?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.07711): Off-axis speech-generation work; FreeGAN replaces adversarial vocoder training with amplitude-phase serial prediction, SNAKE-ConvNeXt, and frequency-weighted phase loss.
- [SaraCoder: Orchestrating Semantic and Structural Cues for Profit-Oriented Repository-Level Code Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10068): Orchestrates semantic and structural cues for resource-optimized repository-level code completion, improving retrieval diversity under limited context windows.
- [From Intent to Execution: Multimodal Chain-of-Thought Reinforcement Learning for Precise CAD Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10118): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for software-engineering agents and code models. Core idea: focus on multimodal chain-of-thought reinforcement learning for precise cad code generation as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [On the need to perform comprehensive evaluations of automated program repair benchmarks: Sorald case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.15135): Bench and evaluation candidate; uses Sorald to show APR evaluation should measure newly introduced violations, functional changes, and structure degradation, not just cleared static-analysis alerts.
- [Correctness-Guaranteed Code Generation via Constrained Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.15866): Uses constrained decoding to enforce correctness properties during code generation rather than relying only on post-hoc filtering.
- [Cognitive Agents Powered by Large Language Models for Agile Software Project Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.16678): Agent Harness candidate; uses CogniSim and SAFe-style simulations with LLM roles for task delegation, inter-agent communication, and project lifecycle management.
- [EyeMulator: Improving Code Language Models by Mimicking Human Visual Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.16771): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for software-engineering agents and code models. Core idea: focus on improving code language models by mimicking human visual attention as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [Exploring the Impact of Generative Artificial Intelligence on Software Development in the IT Sector: Preliminary Findings on Productivity, Efficiency and Job Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.16811): Survey and adoption candidate; mixed-method IT-sector study reports 97% GenAI use plus correlations with productivity, efficiency, and job-security concerns.
- [Pandora: Leveraging Code-driven Knowledge Transfer for Unified Structured Knowledge Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.17905): Uses Pandas code as a unified representation for tables, databases, and knowledge graphs, with execution feedback and cross-task memory for structured reasoning.
- [LLM-Guided Genetic Improvement: Envisioning Semantic Aware Automated Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.18089): Proposes PatchCat, clustering LLM-suggested genetic-improvement edits into 18 patch categories to guide semantic-aware software evolution and skip no-op test runs.
- [How do Humans and LLMs Process Confusing Code?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.18547): Empirically compares human and LLM comprehension on clean versus confusing code, using the mismatch as evidence for where code models may need better comprehension signals.
- [LLM as an Execution Estimator: Recovering Missing Dependency for Practical Time-travelling Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.18721): Debugging-harness work that uses an LLM as an execution estimator to recover missing dependencies for practical time-travel debugging.
- [Leveraging LLMs for Automated Translation of Legacy Code: A Case Study on PL/SQL to Java Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.19663): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for software-engineering agents and code models. Core idea: focus on a case study on pl/sql to java transformation as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [Bootstrapping Learned Cost Models with Synthetic SQL Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.19807): Synthesizes SQL queries to bootstrap learned database cost models when workload traces are scarce, making it SQL systems data synthesis rather than code-model training.
- [Towards Better Correctness and Efficiency in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.20124): Targets both functional correctness and efficiency in generated code, complementing code-model work that optimizes only pass rates.
- [Detecting Stealthy Data Poisoning Attacks in AI Code Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.21636): Studies triggerless poisoning against CodeBERT, CodeT5+, and AST-T5 code generators and finds spectral, activation, and static-analysis defenses unreliable.
- [LLM-based Zero-shot Triple Extraction for Automated Ontology Generation from Software Engineering Standards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.00140): Builds an ontology-generation workflow for software-engineering standards with document segmentation, term mining, LLM relation inference, normalization, and cross-section alignment.
- [Dream-Coder 7B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.01142): Introduces an open diffusion language model for code, adding a non-autoregressive code-model branch to the software-generation landscape.
- [Reinforcement Learning for Machine Learning Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.01684): Targets model training for machine-learning engineering or software-development agents.
- [Spectrogram Patch Codec: A 2D Block-Quantized VQ-VAE and HiFi-GAN for Neural Speech Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.02244): Off-axis neural speech-coding work using a two-dimensional block-quantized VQ-VAE and HiFi-GAN decoder.
- [Code Review Without Borders: Evaluating Synthetic vs. Real Data for Review Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.04810): Uses LLM translation of code changes to create synthetic training data for review recommendation in underrepresented languages and frameworks.
- [Natural Language-Programming Language Software Traceability Link Recovery Needs More than Textual Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.05585): Improves requirements-to-code traceability by adding domain strategies to HGT edge types and Gemini 2.5 Pro prompts, outperforming HGNNLink across twelve projects.
- [ChatGPT for Code Refactoring: Analyzing Topics, Interaction, and Effective Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.08090): Empirical developer-interaction study mining 715 refactoring conversations from DevGPT to identify refactoring topics, intentions, and effective prompt patterns.
- [Development of Automated Software Design Document Review Methods Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.09975): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [EfficientUICoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.12159): Improves multimodal UI-to-code generation by compressing redundant visual input tokens and generated code tokens, making visual front-end coding more efficient.
- [Towards the Next Generation of Software: Insights from Grey Literature on AI-Native Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13144): Survey candidate; grey-literature review defining AI-native applications, quality attributes, architecture patterns, technology stacks, opportunities, and risks.
- [Interleaving Natural Language Prompting with Code Editing for Solving Programming Tasks with Generative AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14088): Studies a developer workflow that alternates natural-language prompts with direct code edits for programming tasks, making it an interaction harness rather than model training.
- [Automated and Context-Aware Code Documentation Leveraging Advanced LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14273): Generates code documentation with context-aware LLM methods, extending coding models toward maintainability support.
- [Brevity is the Soul of Wit: Condensing Code Changes to Improve Commit Message Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.15567): Condenses code diffs before commit-message generation, treating change representation as the model-side lever for producing shorter and more informative maintenance summaries.
- [GraphMend: Code Transformations for Fixing Graph Breaks in PyTorch 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16248): Transforms PyTorch source before execution to eliminate TorchDynamo FX graph breaks from dynamic control flow and Python side effects, improving latency and throughput.
- [LLaVul: A Multimodal LLM for Interpretable Vulnerability Reasoning about Source Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17337): Trains a multimodal LLM on source code paired with security QA so vulnerability detection includes fine-grained, interpretable reasoning.
- [SLICET5: Static Program Slicing using Language Models with Copy Mechanism and Constrained Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17338): Uses a language model with copy mechanism and constrained decoding to predict static slices from incomplete or unparsable snippets while avoiding hallucinated slice tokens.
- [Detection of security smells in IaC scripts through semantics-aware code and language processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18790): Combines CodeBERT semantic code-text representations with LongFormer long-script modeling to detect Infrastructure-as-Code security misconfigurations.
- [Reverse Engineering User Stories from Code using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19587): Requirements-recovery workflow using LLMs to infer user stories from existing code, closer to Agent Harness or requirements engineering than model training.
- [Assertion Messages with Large Language Models (LLMs) for Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19673): Uses LLMs to generate assertion messages, extending code models toward test readability and maintenance support.
- [Synergistic enhancement of requirement-to-code traceability: A framework combining large language model based data augmentation and an advanced encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20149): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [The Cream Rises to the Top: Efficient Reranking Method for Verilog Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20215): Uses efficient reranking to improve Verilog code generation, adding a test-time selection route for HDL coding models.
- [PseudoBridge: Pseudo Code as the Bridge for Better Semantic and Logic Alignment in Code Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20881): Uses LLM-generated pseudo-code as an intermediate modality to align natural-language intent with programming-language logic for code retrieval.
- [An Improved Quantum Software Challenges Classification Approach using Transfer Learning and Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21068): Classifies Stack Overflow quantum-software challenges using transfer learning and explainable AI over a grounded-theory-labeled dataset.
- [Maximum-Entropy Long-CoT Code Review Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21170): Fine-tunes LLMs with regulated long chain-of-thought to analyze multiple dimensions of code review.
- [Code once, Run Green: Automated Green Code Translation in Serverless Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22068): Translates legacy serverless code toward lower-energy implementations, treating energy debt as a code-generation and modernization target rather than only a data-center operations issue.
- [Kimi-Dev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23045): Turns Agentless-style localization, editing, and self-reflection training into transferable skill priors for SWE-Agent adaptation.
- [Beyond Embeddings: Interpretable Feature Extraction for Binary Code Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23449): Binary-analysis model work that replaces opaque embeddings with interpretable, auditable multi-view features for code similarity.
- [AssertFix: Empowering Automated Assertion Fix via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23972): Assertion-repair method using LLMs to diagnose and fix faulty test assertions, extending automated program repair beyond production code changes.
- [TENET: Leveraging Tests Beyond Validation for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24148): Leverages tests beyond final validation to improve code generation.
- [Devstral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25193): Fine-tunes lightweight open models specifically for coding-agent applications with OpenHands-style software-engineering evaluation.
- [Reinforcement Learning-Guided Chain-of-Draft for Token-Efficient Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25243): The paper applies reinforcement learning to chain-of-draft prompting for token-efficient code generation.
- [API misuse detection and repair for data-science libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25378): Uses LLMs to detect and fix API misuses in data-science code, adding a concrete library-aware repair task.
- [DeepCodeSeek: Real-Time API Retrieval for Context-Aware Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25716): Agent Harness and RAG candidate; retrieves relevant APIs in real time and feeds them into code generation for context-aware library use.
- [Regression Language Models for Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26476): Trains a frozen-encoder regression language model to predict numeric execution outcomes such as memory footprint, Triton latency, and ONNX model accuracy directly from code text.
- [Which Programming Language and Model Work Best With LLM-as-a-judge for Code Retrieval?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00324): Studies LLM-as-a-judge for code-retrieval annotation, comparing sparse versus semantic retrievers, programming languages, and judge models against human labels.
- [Analyzing Latent Concepts in Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00476): Introduces CoCoA, a post-hoc interpretability pipeline that clusters contextualized code-token embeddings and labels latent lexical, syntactic, and semantic concepts with static analysis and LLM annotations.
- [QUASAR: Quantum Assembly Code Generation Using Tool-Augmented LLMs via Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00967): Uses tool-augmented agentic reinforcement learning for quantum assembly code generation, combining circuit execution feedback with LLM code synthesis to improve quantum-program correctness and optimization.
- [Clarifying Semantics of In-Context Examples for Unit Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01994): CLAST decomposes and rewrites unit tests with program analysis and LLM rewriting so in-context examples are semantically clearer for test generation.
- [SIEVE: Towards Verifiable Certification for Code-datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02166): Data-verification candidate; aims to certify code datasets with inspectable correctness and provenance evidence for downstream training or evaluation.
- [AP2O-Coder: Adaptively Progressive Preference Optimization for Reducing Compilation and Runtime Errors in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02393): Uses adaptively progressive preference optimization to reduce compilation and runtime errors in LLM-generated code.
- [Mechanistic Interpretability of Code Correctness in LLMs via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02917): Uses sparse autoencoders to identify code-correctness directions in LLM activations, then tests them through steering, attention analysis, and orthogonalization.
- [The Valley of Code Reasoning: Scaling Knowledge Distillation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06101): Studies distilling reasoning traces into small code models, finding a performance valley where more data first hurts and then improves competitive-coding skill.
- [Beyond More Context: How Granularity and Order Drive Code Completion Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06606): Studies how context granularity and ordering affect code-completion quality, refining repository-context use for code models.
- [LLM Company Policies and Policy Implications in Software Organizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.06718): Survey and governance candidate; examines how 11 companies write LLM chatbot policies for safe integration into software-development workflows.
- [Relative Positioning Based Code Chunking Method For Rich Context Retrieval In Repository Level Code Completion Task With Code Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08610): Agent Harness and retrieval candidate; chunks repositories and uses syntactic or semantic similarity plus relative positioning to build code-completion context.
- [Faver: Boosting LLM-based RTL Generation with Function Abstracted Verifiable Middleware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08664): Introduces a function-abstracted verifiable middleware that mixes LLM-friendly code structures with rule-based templates so RTL generators can verify behavior without directly producing scarce testbench artifacts.
- [Vector Graph-Based Repository Understanding for Issue-Driven File Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08876): Agent Harness candidate; represents repository files in a vector graph for issue-driven file retrieval before repair or localization.
- [A Human Behavioral Baseline for Collective Governance in Software Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08956): Survey and empirical-governance candidate; parses governance documents into actors, rules, actions, and objects, then tracks entropy, richness, and drift across 710 open-source projects.
- [TIT: A Tree-Structured Instruction Tuning Approach for LLM-Based Code Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09400): Uses tree-structured instruction tuning to decompose and supervise LLM-based code translation rather than treating a whole translation request as one flat instruction.
- [Learning Bug Context for PyTorch-to-JAX Translation with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09898): Code-migration model work that learns bug context for PyTorch-to-JAX translation so LLM fixes target framework-specific failure modes.
- [IntrinTrans: LLM-based Intrinsic Code Translator for RISC-V Vector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10119): Translates architecture-specific intrinsic code to RISC-V Vector intrinsics, extending code-translation coverage to low-level vectorization.
- [Learning to Guarantee Type Correctness in Code Generation through Type-Guided Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10216): A type-guided program-synthesis route for code generation. Core idea: align model generation with type-derivation structure so type correctness is learned internally rather than only filtered after decoding.
- [CLASP: Training-Free LLM-Assisted Source Code Watermarking via Semantic-Preserving Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11251): A training-free LLM-assisted source-code watermarking framework. Core idea: apply semantic-preserving transformations so generated code can carry more robust ownership signals.
- [ReLook: Vision-Grounded RL with a Multimodal LLM Critic for Agentic Web Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11498): Trains agentic web-coding behavior with vision-grounded reinforcement learning and a multimodal LLM critic, so the model-side contribution is the critic and RL signal.
- [Enhancing Neural Code Representation with Additional Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12082): Improves neural code representations by injecting additional program context for downstream code-intelligence tasks.
- [On Pretraining for Project-Level Code Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13697): Extends OpenCoder from 4k to 16k context with 1B curated repository-level tokens, finding that RoPE-scaling adaptation drives most project-level completion gains while simpler file-level training remains competitive under limited compute.
- [PathFix: Automated Program Repair with Expected Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14341): Guides automated program repair with expected execution-path information, making the patch model reason about intended control flow.
- [TokDrift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14972): Analyzes the mismatch between LLM subword tokenization and code grammar, informing tokenizer-aware robustness work for code models.
- [Selecting and Combining Large Language Models for Scalable Code Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15480): Studies model selection and ensemble combination for scalable LLM-based code clone detection.
- [Enhancing Code Review through Fuzzing and Likely Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15512): Agent Harness and verifier candidate; combines fuzzing with likely invariant mining to surface review evidence around candidate code changes.
- [LLM-VeriPPA: Power, Performance, and Area Optimization aware Verilog Code Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15899): Adds power, performance, and area constraints to LLM-based Verilog generation, keeping HDL synthesis tied to hardware quality goals.
- [VeriGRAG: Enhancing LLM-Based Verilog Code Generation with Structure-Aware Soft Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15914): Retrieves structural graph embeddings from Verilog code and turns them into soft prompts for more correct specification-to-Verilog generation.
- [SemOpt: LLM-Driven Code Optimization via Rule-Based Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16384): Couples LLM code optimization with rule-based analysis so candidate rewrites are steered by explicit optimization rules.
- [Contrasting the Hyperparameter Tuning Impact Across Software Defect Prediction Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16665): Compares hyperparameter tuning across inner-version and cross-version defect prediction using 28 algorithms, 53 post-release datasets, two tuners, and five optimization metrics.
- [HGAdapter: Hypergraph-based Adapters in Language Models for Code Summarization and Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.17591): Inserts hypergraph adapters into code PLMs to capture AST-family, lexical, and line-level high-order token correlations for summarization and clone detection.
- [Can Image-To-Video Models Simulate Pedestrian Dynamics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.17731): Off-axis video-model candidate; evaluates whether image-to-video generators reproduce pedestrian dynamics rather than contributing a software-development model.
- [This is Going to Sound Crazy, But What If We Used Large Language Models to Boost Automatic Database Tuning Algorithms By Leveraging Prior History? We Will Find Better Configurations More Quickly Than Retraining From Scratch!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.17748): Booster structures query-configuration history for LLM recommendations and beam-search composition, helping DBMS tuners adapt after workload drift or schema transfer.
- [TritonRL: Training LLMs to Think and Code Triton Without Cheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.17891): Trains an 8B Triton programming model with reinforcement learning and safeguards against reward hacking.
- [KAT-Coder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.18779): Reports an agentic coding model trained with mid-training, SFT, reinforcement fine-tuning, and deployment adaptation for IDE-style software workflows.
- [SBAN: A Framework & Multi-Dimensional Dataset for Large Language Model Pre-Training and Software Code Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.18936): Provides a framework and multidimensional dataset for LLM pretraining and software-code mining, strengthening code-model data coverage.
- [That's Deprecated! Understanding, Detecting, and Steering Knowledge Conflicts in Language Models for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.19116): Constructs code-generation knowledge-conflict scenarios and uses detected conflict representations to steer LLMs away from deprecated or prompt-conflicting API behavior.
- [R\documentclass(12pt){minimal} \usepackage{amsmath} \usepackage{wasysym} \usepackage{amsfonts} \usepackage{amssymb} \usepackage{amsbsy} \usepackage{mathrsfs} \usepackage{upgreek} \setlength{\oddsidemargin}{-69pt} \begin{document}$$^2$$\end{document}ComSync: improving code-comment synchronization wit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.21106): Improves code-comment synchronization with hybrid retrieval and reranking, targeting maintenance cases where comments lag behind code changes.
- [Agentic Reinforcement Learning for Real-World Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22075): Trains code-fixing agents on reproducible real issues with SFT and RL pipelines, exposing environment-generalization limits in repository repair.
- [Collaborative LLM Agents for C4 Software Architecture Design Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22787): Agent Harness candidate; coordinates multiple LLM agents to automate C4 software-architecture design rather than introducing a new code model.
- [Code Aesthetics with Agentic Reward Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23272): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [DCMM-SQL: Automated Data-Centric Pipeline and Multi-Model Collaboration Training for Text-to-SQL Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23284): DCMM-SQL automates text-to-SQL data repair and error-data augmentation, then uses multi-model collaboration to train stronger SQL generation models from cleaner and harder supervision.
- [Agentsway - Software Development Methodology for AI Agents-based Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23664): Agent Harness and methodology candidate; defines a software-development process for teams of AI agents rather than a model training recipe.
