- [RefleXGen:The unexamined code is not worth using](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23674): Improves secure code generation through RAG-guided self-reflection, iteratively accumulating security knowledge and revising outputs without fine-tuning or a specialized secure-code dataset.
- [Squrve: A Unified and Modular Framework for Complex Real-World Text-to-SQL Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24102): Squrve is primarily a deployment framework for real-world Text-to-SQL, standardizing invocation interfaces and modular components rather than introducing a new code-model training recipe.
- [MAGNET: A Multi-Graph Attentional Network for Code Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.24241): Fuses AST, control-flow, and data-flow graphs with residual GNNs and attention so clone detection can capture multiple syntactic and semantic views of code.
- [Dissect-and-Restore: AI-based Code Verification with Transient Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.25406): Prometheus uses transient refactoring to break verification targets into AI-manageable units, then restores the original structure after generating specifications and proof annotations.
- [Empirical Studies on Quantum Optimization for Software Engineering: A Systematic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.27113): Survey candidate; systematically analyzes how quantum optimization has been applied to software-engineering problems.
- [Divide, Link, and Conquer: Recall-oriented Schema Linking for NL-to-SQL via Question Decomposition](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.emnlp-industry.122): Decomposes complex questions before schema linking so Text-to-SQL models recover more relevant tables and columns under recall-oriented evaluation.
- [SQLWOZ: A Realistic Task-Oriented Dialogue Dataset with SQL-Based Dialogue State Representation for Complex User Requirements](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.emnlp-main.383): Releases a task-oriented dialogue dataset whose SQL-form dialogue state represents complex user constraints, making database interaction a training and evaluation target.
- [SynC-LLM: Generation of Large-Scale Synthetic Circuit Code with Hierarchical Language Models](https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.877/): Generates large-scale synthetic circuit code with hierarchical language models, extending code-model data synthesis into electronic-design automation.
- [Hierarchical Reward Modeling for Fault Localization in Large Code Repositories](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.findings-emnlp.966): Trains reward signals at multiple repository granularities so fault localization can rank files, functions, and finer code regions instead of relying on a flat suspiciousness score.
- [ORANGE: An Online Reflection ANd GEneration framework with Domain Knowledge for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.00985): ORANGE builds database-specific knowledge bases from historical SQL translation logs, then reflects over that accumulated domain knowledge to improve future Text-to-SQL generation.
- [QiMeng-NeuComBack: Self-Evolving Translation from IR to Assembly Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.01183): Introduces NeuComBack for IR-to-assembly neural compilation and a self-evolving prompt-optimization method that mines prior self-debugging traces to improve generated assembly.
- [Open the Oyster: Empirical Evaluation and Improvement of Code Reasoning Confidence in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.02197): Evaluates and improves code-reasoning confidence in LLMs, targeting calibration of software reasoning outputs.
- [Who’s Who? LLM-assisted Software Traceability with Architecture Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.02434): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [From Code Changes to Quality Gains: An Empirical Study in Python ML Systems with PyQu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.02827): Empirical software-quality candidate; studies how code changes in Python ML systems relate to quality gains using PyQu rather than proposing a new code model.
- [Automated Prompt Generation for Code Intelligence: An Empirical study and Experience in WeChat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.03136): Studies instruction generation and multi-step reasoning prompts across code translation, summarization, and API recommendation, making prompt construction a model-side coding variable.
- [OMPILOT: Harnessing Transformer Models for Auto Parallelization to Shared Memory Computing Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.03866): Trains a transformer for translating C++ into OpenMP code, extending code-generation coverage to shared-memory auto-parallelization.
- [Generating Software Architecture Description from Source Code using Reverse Engineering and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05165): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [An Empirical Study of Java Code Improvements Based on Stack Overflow Answer Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05813): Mines Stack Overflow answer edits to characterize recurring Java improvement patterns, providing empirical repair and refactoring signals rather than a new code model.
- [Self-Abstraction from Grounded Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05931): Uses rollout-derived plan abstractions to refine SWE-agent policies across Mini-SWE-Agent and OpenHands, giving coding agents a reusable self-improvement signal without changing the base model.
- [Leveraging Large Language Models for Use Case Model Generation from Software Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.09231): This requirements-engineering study prompts an open-weight LLM to extract actors and use cases from textual requirements, targeting automated use-case model generation rather than code synthesis.
- [Architecting software monitors for control-flow anomaly detection through large language models and conformance checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.10876): Combines LLM-generated software monitors with conformance checking to detect control-flow anomalies.
- [Prompt Engineering vs. Fine-Tuning for LLM-Based Vulnerability Detection in Solana and Algorand Smart Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.11250): Compares prompting and fine-tuning for LLM vulnerability detection on Solana and Algorand smart contracts.
- [EARL: Entropy-Aware RL Alignment of LLMs for Reliable RTL Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.12033): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for software-development capability. Core idea: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated significant potential in hardware design automation, particularly in using natural language to synthesize Register-Transfer Level (RTL) code.
- [Towards Requirements Engineering for GenAI-Enabled Software: Bridging Responsibility Gaps through Human Oversight Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.13069): Requirements-engineering governance candidate; formulates human oversight requirements for GenAI-enabled software rather than a code-model training method.
- [Pre-Filtering Code Suggestions using Developer Behavioral Telemetry to Optimize LLM-Assisted Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18849): Trains a lightweight telemetry-based acceptance predictor that decides whether to invoke an LLM code suggestion, nearly doubling VS Code plugin acceptance rates while suppressing unused calls.
- [Robust Long-term Test-Time Adaptation for 3D Human Pose Estimation through Motion Discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18851): Off-axis vision-model candidate; uses motion discretization for long-term test-time adaptation in 3D human pose estimation.
- [TreeCoder: Systematic Exploration and Optimisation of Decoding and Constraints for LLM Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22277): Explores decoding and constraint strategies for stronger LLM code generation.
- [Chart2Code-MoLA: Efficient Multi-Modal Code Generation via Adaptive Expert Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.23321): Uses adaptive expert routing for multimodal chart-to-code generation.
- [Progressive Code Integration for Abstractive Bug Report Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00325): Adds source-code context progressively to abstractive bug-report summarization so generated summaries stay tied to relevant program artifacts.
- [Knowledge-Graph-Driven Data Synthesis for Low-Resource Software Development: A HarmonyOS Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00380): Knowledge-graph-driven data synthesis for low-resource software development is a software-model data generation candidate, albeit case-study-shaped.
- [The AI Attribution Paradox: Transparency as Social Strategy in Open-Source Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00867): Survey and empirical adoption candidate; studies how open-source developers use AI attribution as a social strategy around transparency.
- [LAURA: Enhancing Code Review Generation with Context-Enriched Retrieval-Augmented LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.01356): Enhances code-review generation with context-enriched retrieval-augmented LLMs, improving review grounding in surrounding code.
- [Flowchart2Mermaid: A Vision-Language Model Powered System for Converting Flowcharts into Editable Diagram Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02170): Converts flowchart images into editable Mermaid.js diagram code with a VLM prompt and mixed-initiative editing, making it a visual-to-code workflow rather than model training.
- [Bin2Vec: Interpretable and Auditable Multi-View Binary Analysis for Code Plagiarism Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02197): Compares binaries with inspectable views over imports, exports, instructions, and memory behavior, then fuses those views into an auditable plagiarism-similarity score.
- [CUDA-L2: Surpassing cuBLAS Performance for Matrix Multiplication through Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02551): Trains LLM-generated HGEMM CUDA kernels with execution speed as the RL reward across 1,000 configurations, surpassing torch.matmul, cuBLAS, and cuBLASLt baselines in offline and server-mode tests.
- [DeepSeek-V3.2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.02556): Adds large-scale agentic task synthesis and scalable reinforcement learning to a frontier open model, with coding and tool-use implications for software agents.
- [Beyond Code Pairs: Dialogue-Based Data Generation for LLM Code Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03086): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for software-engineering agents and code models. Core idea: focus on dialogue-based data generation for llm code translation as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [Completion by Comprehension: Guiding Code Generation with Multi-Granularity Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.04538): Guides code generation with multi-granularity understanding, linking comprehension signals to completion quality.
- [When Forgetting Builds Reliability: LLM Unlearning for Reliable Hardware Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05341): Uses syntax-preserving unlearning and selective loss to remove problematic hardware-code knowledge while preserving HDL generation quality.
- [A Hybrid Approach for EMF Code Generation:Code Templates Meet Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05498): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for software-development capability. Core idea: Template-based and LLM-based code generation are both key enablers of automated software development.
- [Multi-Repository Bug Localization by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05908): Uses natural-language summarization to support LLM bug localization across microservice repositories, extending repository-level diagnosis coverage.
- [Translating PL/I Macro Procedures into Java Using Automatic Templatization and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.06448): Uses automatic templatization and large language models to translate PL/I macro procedures into Java.
- [Automatic Syntax Error Repair for Discrete Controller Synthesis using Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07261): Repairs syntax errors for discrete-controller synthesis with large language models.
- [Understanding Privacy Risks in Code Models Through Training Dynamics: A Causal Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07814): Studies how different PII types are learned and leaked during code-model fine-tuning, using causal interventions to separate privacy-risk mechanisms across PII categories.
- [LaMoSys3.5D: Enabling 3.5D-IC-Based Large Language Model Inference Serving Systems via Hardware/Software Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.08731): Systems candidate; applies hardware and software co-design for LLM inference serving on 3.5D integrated circuits rather than improving code-generation capability.
- [Llama-Based Source Code Vulnerability Detection: Prompt Engineering vs Fine Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.09006): Compares prompt engineering and fine-tuning for Llama-based source-code vulnerability detection.
- [Training Versatile Coding Agents in Synthetic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12216): Synthesizes diverse coding-agent environments to train agents beyond fixed GitHub issue distributions.
- [Sharpen the Spec, Cut the Code: A Case for Generative File System with SYSSPEC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13047): SYSSPEC turns ambiguous file-system prompts into sharper specifications so LLMs can generate and evolve file-system code with fewer underspecified design failures.
- [Fine-tuned LLM-based Code Migration Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13515): Fine-tunes an LLM inside an iterative SQL codebase migration framework, focusing on Oracle-to-SQL syntax mapping and discrepancy resolution during database modernization.
- [The Double Life of Code World Models: Provably Unmasking Malicious Behavior Through Execution Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13821): Introduces CTVP, a verifier framework that probes untrusted code-generating models through predicted execution traces over semantically equivalent program transformations.
- [DreamPRM-Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15000): Trains a function-as-step process reward model with label correction for LLM coding.
- [An Exploratory Study of Bayesian Prompt Optimization for Test-Driven Code Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15076): Uses Bayesian optimization to tune prompts for test-driven LLM code generation, treating test outcomes as the search signal.
- [Knowledge Distillation with Structured Chain-of-Thought for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.17053): Distills Text-to-SQL reasoning into small language models using formal structured chain-of-thought traces, aiming to reduce enterprise reliance on expensive proprietary LLMs.
- [Self-Play SWE-RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.18552): Trains software agents through self-play with generated software-evolution challenges rather than only human GitHub issues.
- [Modular Layout Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.18996): Normalizes visual structure and constrains generation to produce modular frontend code for modern UI frameworks.
- [Beyond Language Boundaries: Uncovering Programming Language Families for Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19509): Analyzes programming-language families for code language models, clarifying cross-language structure beyond surface syntax.
- [LASER: Language Model Regression for Semi-Structured Workflow Resource and Runtime Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19701): Fine-tunes LLMs on serialized workflow job configurations for multi-target resource and runtime regression, preserving shell commands, dependency graphs, and hierarchical metadata.
- [Widget2Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.19918): Uses multimodal LLMs to turn visual widgets into UI code, adding a model-side visual-to-code generation route.
- [VSA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.20034): Aligns visual and structural signals so UI-to-code generation produces component-oriented frontend assets rather than flat code.
- [Fairness Is Not Just Ethical: Performance Trade-Off via Data Correlation Tuning to Mitigate Bias in ML Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.21348): Tunes data correlations in ML software to study fairness and performance trade-offs, making it a software-quality model candidate.
- [SWE-RM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.21919): Trains execution-free reward models for SWE agents, targeting both test-time scaling and reinforcement-learning feedback.
- [AGRO-SQL: Agentic Group-Relative Optimization with High-Fidelity Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23366): Combines high-fidelity synthetic data with agentic group-relative optimization for text-to-SQL model training.
- [Rug](https://doi.org/10.1109/icse55347.2025.00097): Targets Rust unit-test generation with an LLM-based code-generation system.
- [Comprehend, Imitate, and then Update](https://doi.org/10.1109/ASE63991.2025.00046): Maintains test suites by first understanding existing tests, imitating project testing style, and then updating tests as production code changes.
- [Context-aware code summary generation](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jss.2025.112580): Generates code summaries with broader program context, adding a model-side code-understanding method for software-development assistance.
- [CSMS](https://doi.org/10.1109/APSEC66846.2025.00105): Refines class-level code summarization by combining Transformer-based summary generation with fused method summaries for repository documentation.
- [LLM-Based Code Clone Detection with AST Graphs](https://doi.org/10.1109/ACCESS.2025.3628856): Combines LLM-based code revision with AST graph modeling for nuanced clone detection, extending model-side code understanding beyond patch generation.
- [DEGAN-CS: An efficient code search model based on dataenhanced optimization of generative adversarial networks](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patcog.2025.112709): Uses data-enhanced GAN optimization for code search, adding a neural code-retrieval method to software-development model coverage.
- [GenC2Rust: Towards Generating Generic Rust Code from C](https://doi.org/10.1109/icse55347.2025.00127): Generates generic Rust code from C, adding an ICSE code-translation method line for memory-safe software modernization.
- [Llm-Assisted Crossover in Genetic Improvement of Software](https://doi.org/10.1109/gi66624.2025.00012): Uses large language models as crossover operators in genetic improvement, adding a search-based software-engineering method for recombining and improving code variants.
- [RaxCS: Towards cross-language code summarization with contrastive pre-training and retrieval augmentation](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.infsof.2025.107741): Combines contrastive pre-training with retrieval augmentation for cross-language code summarization, targeting summaries that transfer across programming languages.
- [HyRACC: A Hybrid Retrieval-Augmented Framework for More Efficient Code Completion](https://doi.org/10.1109/forge66646.2025.00013): Uses a hybrid retrieval-augmented framework for efficient code completion, adding a repository-context retrieval route for coding models.
- [DLCoG: A Novel Framework for Dual-Level Code Comment Generation Based on Semantic Segmentation and In-Context Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/icpc66645.2025.00036): Segments code semantically and uses in-context examples to generate both local and broader comments, separating comment granularity instead of emitting one flat summary.
- [Mixture-of-Experts Low-Rank Adaptation for Multilingual Code Summarization](https://doi.org/10.1109/ase63991.2025.00186): Applies mixture-of-experts low-rank adaptation to multilingual code summarization, adding a model-side adaptation method for code intelligence.
- [Comprehensive Fine-Tuning Large Language Models of Code for Automated Program Repair](https://doi.org/10.1109/tse.2025.3532759): Studies comprehensive fine-tuning of code LLMs for automated program repair, treating repair performance as the adaptation target rather than only prompting base models.
- [The Design Space of LLM-Based AI Coding Assistants: An Analysis of 90 Systems in Academia and Industry](https://doi.org/10.1109/vl-hcc65237.2025.00041): Survey and parent-review candidate; analyzes 90 academic and industry coding assistants, so the contribution is a design-space map rather than a model recipe.
- [Comparative Analysis of Styles in LLM-Generated Code for LeetCode Problems: A Preliminary Study](https://doi.org/10.1109/compsac65507.2025.00219): Empirically compares stylistic properties of LLM-generated LeetCode solutions, providing behavior evidence for code generators rather than a new model.
- [C2RustTV: An LLM-based Framework for C to Rust Translation and Validation](https://doi.org/10.1109/compsac65507.2025.00158): Translates C to Rust with an LLM-backed framework that includes validation, keeping migration tied to semantic equivalence.
- [Extracting the Essence and Discarding the Dross: Enhancing Code Generation with Contrastive Execution Feedback](https://aclanthology.org/2025.coling-main.704/): Uses contrastive execution feedback to separate useful and misleading execution signals for stronger code generation.
- [Breaking Task Isolation: Enhancing Code Review Automation with Mixture-of-Experts Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/issre66568.2025.00033): Applies a mixture-of-experts LLM design to code-review automation so review subtasks can share information instead of being handled in isolation.
- [Spec2Doc2RTL: RTL Generation from Specification with Natural Language Representation](https://doi.org/10.1109/iseda65950.2025.11101140): Generates RTL from specifications through a natural-language intermediate representation, bridging requirements text and hardware code.
- [RoSum-Mcts: Monte Carlo Tree Search-Inspired HDL Code Summarization with Structural Rewards](https://doi.org/10.1109/iclad65226.2025.00037): Uses MCTS-style search with structural rewards for HDL code summarization.
- [Automated AADL Architecture Modeling: Leveraging Large Language Models for Safety-Critical Software](https://doi.org/10.1109/models-c68889.2025.00050): Uses LLMs to generate AADL architecture models for safety-critical software, making the target artifact an analyzable architecture model rather than executable code.
- [FuzzCoder: Code Large Language Model-Based Fuzz Testing for Industrial IoT Programs](https://doi.org/10.1109/jiot.2025.3577602): Agent Harness and testing candidate; uses a code LLM to generate or guide fuzz testing for industrial IoT programs.
- [The icing on the cake: Enhancing Code Pre-trained models-based program repair with multi-source inputs and fix templates](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jss.2025.112590): Improves code-pretrained-model program repair by feeding multi-source context and fix templates into the repair process.
- [Boosting unit test generation via structure-aware fine-tuning of pre-trained model](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.infsof.2025.107948): Fine-tunes a pretrained model with structure-aware signals to improve unit-test generation.
- [Automatic SystemVerilog Assertion Generation: Challenges and Opportunities](https://doi.org/10.1109/ccece64018.2025.11364438): Analyzes how LLMs can generate SystemVerilog assertions and where assertion correctness, design-context grounding, and verification coverage still fail.
- [From Prototype Interface to Test Case Generation with Large Language Model](https://doi.org/10.1109/qrs-c65679.2025.00037): Converts prototype interface descriptions into test cases with an LLM, keeping the contribution on test-generation artifacts rather than general code synthesis.
- [Coding With AI: From a Reflection on Industrial Practices to Future Computer Science and Software Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23982): Survey and education candidate; reflects on industrial AI coding practices and implications for CS and software-engineering education.
- [When to Ponder: Adaptive Compute Allocation for Code Generation via Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.00894): Allocates code-generation compute adaptively through test-time training, deciding when extra pondering is likely to improve generated programs.
- [Avaliação de Qualidade de Código Java gerado por Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.5753/eres.2025.16845): Evaluates the quality of Java code generated by LLMs, making the row empirical evidence about generated-code behavior rather than a training recipe.
- [ChatGPT Choreography: Discovering Developer Dialogues and Potential Software Development Lifecycle Applications](https://doi.org/10.1109/mc.2025.3532347): Studies developer dialogue patterns with ChatGPT across software lifecycle tasks, making it a workflow and adoption signal rather than a pure model entry.
- [LiSSA: Toward Generic Traceability Link Recovery Through Retrieval- Augmented Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/icse55347.2025.00186): Uses retrieval-augmented generation for generic traceability-link recovery across software artifacts.
- [SecuGuard: Leveraging pattern-exploiting training in language models for advanced software vulnerability detection](https://doi.org/10.2478/ijmce-2025-0005): Uses pattern-exploiting training to adapt language models for software vulnerability detection.
- [Analysis of ChatGPT-Generated Codes Across Multiple Programming Languages](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2025.3538050): Empirical evaluation candidate; analyzes ChatGPT-generated code quality across multiple programming languages rather than adding a new model.
- [Analyzing the dependability of Large Language Models for code clone generation](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jss.2025.112548): Empirically evaluates whether LLM-generated code clones are dependable, providing model-risk evidence for reuse and clone-generation workflows.
- [Planning a Large Language Model for Static Detection of Runtime Errors in Code Snippets](https://doi.org/10.1109/icse55347.2025.00102): Uses planning with an LLM to detect runtime errors in short code snippets before execution, positioning runtime-error detection as a model-assisted static-analysis task.
- [An empirical study of best practices for code pre-trained models on software engineering classification tasks](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2025.126762): Benchmarks code-pretrained-model choices and tuning practices across SE classification tasks, giving adaptation guidance rather than a new architecture.
- [Integrating Pull Request Comment Analysis and Developer Profiles for Expertise-Based Recommendations in Global Software Development](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2025.3532386): Combines pull-request comments with developer profile features to recommend expertise in distributed software projects.
- [Using pseudo-AI submissions for detecting AI-generated code](https://doi.org/10.3389/fcomp.2025.1549761): Uses pseudo-AI programming submissions as supervision for identifying AI-generated code in educational or repository settings.
- [Detecting code paraphrased by large language models using coding style features](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.engappai.2025.112454): Detects LLM-paraphrased code through coding-style features, complementing authorship and generated-code detectors with paraphrase-specific signals.
- [Can AI Fix Buggy Code? Exploring the Use of Large Language Models in Automated Program Repair](https://doi.org/10.1109/mc.2025.3527407): Evaluates LLMs for automated program repair, separating practical bug-fixing capability from claims about training a repair-specialized model.
- [Why Do Developers Engage with ChatGPT in Issue-Tracker? Investigating Usage and Reliance on ChatGPT-Generated Code](https://doi.org/10.1109/saner64311.2025.00015): Studies why developers use ChatGPT in issue trackers and how much they rely on generated code, making it an adoption and reliance study.
- [Cross Domain Few-Shot Line-Level Defect Prediction in Open Software Development via Meta Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/tce.2025.3572334): Uses meta-learning for cross-domain, few-shot line-level defect prediction when target projects have limited labeled defects.
- [Multimodal Classification of Software Issue Reports Using CLIP: A Fine-Tuned Approach for Bug and Feature Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/ecce64574.2025.11012920): Fine-tunes CLIP-style multimodal representations to classify issue reports as bugs or feature requests when reports include textual and visual evidence.
- [SEOCD: Detecting obsolete code comments by fusing semantic features and expert features](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2025.127470): Detects obsolete comments by fusing learned semantic representations with expert-crafted code-comment consistency features.
- [A Test Oracle for Reinforcement Learning Software Based on Lyapunov Stability Control Theory](https://doi.org/10.1109/icse55347.2025.00074): Defines a Lyapunov-stability-based oracle for testing reinforcement-learning software rather than training a coding model.
- [Who's Pushing the Code? An Exploration of GitHub Impersonation](https://doi.org/10.1109/icse55347.2025.00065): Empirically studies GitHub impersonation in code contributions, making the row software-supply-chain evidence rather than a model method.
- [Malo in the Code Jungle: Explainable Fault Localization for Decentralized Applications](https://doi.org/10.1109/tse.2025.3578816): Provides explainable fault localization for decentralized applications, adapting localization evidence to smart-contract and blockchain code.
- [Can We Translate Code Better with LLMs and Call Graph Analysis?](https://doi.org/10.24963/ijcai.2025/848): Adds call-graph analysis to LLM code translation so cross-language outputs preserve interprocedural dependencies.
- [RTLExplain: A Structured Approach to RTL Code Summarization and Question Answering for Medium-to-Large Designs Using LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1109/mlcad65511.2025.11189167): Structures RTL summarization and question answering for medium-to-large hardware designs instead of treating HDL as isolated snippets.
- [Automated robustness testing for LLM-based natural language processing software](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2025.130642): Tests robustness of LLM-based NLP software through automated input variation, making the row testing infrastructure adjacent to model reliability.
- [Scalable Large-Scale Multi-Granularity Code Clone Detection via Clustering Search and Pre-Trained Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/tse.2025.3635158): Combines clustering search with pretrained models to scale clone detection across multiple code granularities.
- [Promises and Perils of LLM- and Agent-Generated Code](https://doi.org/10.1109/mc.2025.3627694): Survey and risk-analysis candidate; discusses benefits and failure modes of code produced by LLMs and coding agents.
- [The True Cost of AI Assistance to Programming of Software](https://doi.org/10.1109/ms.2025.3646081): Empirical adoption and productivity candidate; examines hidden costs of AI programming assistance rather than a model-side method.
- [Gated transformer network for multivariate security patch identification with mixture-of-experts](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.infsof.2025.108006): Uses a gated Transformer and mixture-of-experts design for multivariate security-patch identification.
- [OpenFix: leveraging graph representation and LLM knowledge augmentation for prompt-guided vulnerability repair in smart infrastructures](https://doi.org/10.1108/ijpcc-11-2025-0490): Combines graph representations with LLM knowledge augmentation to guide vulnerability repair prompts for smart-infrastructure code.
- [Machine Learning Solutions to Automated Governance and Code Analysis to Compliance](https://doi.org/10.1109/icaaic64647.2025.11331106): Compliance-analysis candidate; applies machine learning to governance and code analysis for compliance rather than training a reusable coding model.
- [Bibliometric and Content Analysis of Large Language Models Research in Software Engineering: The Potential and Limitation in Software Engineering](https://doi.org/10.14569/ijacsa.2025.0160436): Survey candidate; uses bibliometric and content analysis to map LLM research in software engineering.
- [Catching Common Vulnerabilities with Code Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/secdev66745.2025.00016): Evaluates code language models as detectors for common software vulnerabilities.
- [HectorIDE: A Document Centric Platform for AI Powered Software Development](https://doi.org/10.1109/roedunet68395.2025.11208510): Agent Harness candidate; provides a document-centric IDE platform for AI-powered software development.
- [Software Defects Prediction: Source Code Is All You Need](https://doi.org/10.1109/qrs-c65679.2025.00029): Studies source-code-only representations for software defect prediction.
- [Effective Code Generation Using Program Synthesis And CodeLLM](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccies63851.2025.11032972): Combines program-synthesis techniques with CodeLLM generation for code-generation tasks.
- [Iterative program synthesis with code knowledge](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ins.2025.122156): Injects code knowledge into an iterative program-synthesis loop so candidate programs can be refined with domain and implementation constraints.
- [Integrating Attention Mechanism with Code Structural Affinity and Execution Context Correlation for Automated Bug Repair](https://doi.org/10.32604/cmc.2025.071733): Repairs bugs by combining attention with code-structure affinity and execution-context correlation, giving the repair model structural and runtime cues.
- [Code Auto-generation System based on Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/bmsb65076.2025.11165667): Describes an LLM-based code autogeneration system, useful as applied evidence for generation pipelines rather than a new foundation-model recipe.
- [From Kotlin to Swift and Back: Toward Fully Automated Cross-Language Code Transpilation](https://doi.org/10.1109/asew67777.2025.00030): Automates bidirectional Kotlin and Swift transpilation, testing whether LLM-based migration can preserve mobile-app semantics across ecosystems.
- [KG-Code: A Knowledge Graph Guided Pre-Training Approach for Code Representation](https://doi.org/10.1109/apsec66846.2025.00095): Guides code representation pretraining with a knowledge graph so learned embeddings encode relationships beyond token co-occurrence.
- [A Study on Metric-Human Alignment in Code Summarization by LLMs through Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://doi.org/10.1109/sped67700.2025.11252151): Uses Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks to study how automatic code-summary metrics align with human judgments.
- [DSBox: A Data Selection Framework for Efficient Deep Code Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/ase63991.2025.00375): Selects training data for deep code learning so code models can improve efficiency without consuming every available sample.
- [Generating Cost Optimized Smart Contracts from Deep Learning Code using Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/bcca66705.2025.11229467): Translates deep-learning code into smart contracts while optimizing cost, tying LLM generation to blockchain deployment constraints.
- [Exploring continual learning in code intelligence with domain-wise distilled prompts](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.infsof.2025.107775): Uses domain-wise distilled prompts to study continual learning for code-intelligence tasks without forgetting prior domains.
- [KNSE-Code: A Knowledge-Aware, Safety-Enhanced, Rule-Driven Framework for Aviation Safety-Critical Software Code Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccbdai66607.2025.11388648): Combines aviation-domain knowledge, safety rules, and LLM generation for safety-critical code artifacts.
- [LLM-Powered Code Generation Using RAG Framework with LLaMA 3](https://doi.org/10.1109/csitss67709.2025.11294234): Grounds LLaMA 3 code generation with retrieval-augmented context so generated code can use external project or domain knowledge.
- [SageJavon: A scalable AI tutor for personalized programming learning](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ipm.2025.104605): Programming-education system that personalizes tutoring at scale, better treated as education workflow evidence than code-model training.
- [Discarding the Crutches: Adaptive Parameter-Efficient Expert Meta-Learning for Continual Semantic Parsing](https://aclanthology.org/2025.coling-main.240/): Off-axis semantic-parsing adaptation work using parameter-efficient expert meta-learning; it is not specific to software-development coding models.
- [VeRPO: Verifiable Dense Reward Policy Optimization for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03525): Optimizes code generation with verifiable dense rewards.
- [AID-SQL: Adaptive In-Context Learning of Text-to-SQL with Difficulty-Aware Instruction and Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/icde65448.2025.00294): Selects instructions, examples, and retrieval evidence according to query difficulty so Text-to-SQL prompting can adapt between simple and hard database questions.
- [A Comprehensive Study on Code Completion for Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/icaace65325.2025.11020128): Surveys LLM-based code completion and evaluates multi-granularity Python, Java, and C++ completion samples, finding weaker behavior on long dependencies and complex nested or asynchronous code.
- [Designing an LLM-Based Copilot for Manufacturing Equipment Selection](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.mfglet.2025.10.017): Off-axis copilot candidate; designs an LLM assistant for manufacturing equipment selection rather than software development.
- [CM-SQL: A cross-model consistency framework for text-to-SQL](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2025.131708): Introduces cross-model consistency checking for text-to-SQL generation so multiple models can expose unreliable SQL outputs.
- [Automated Extraction of Conditional Causal Rules from Control Narratives Using Logic Programming and Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/etfa65518.2025.11205764): Combines LLM parsing with logic-programming constraints to extract conditional causal rules from industrial control narratives.
- [MaxCode: A Max-Reward Reinforcement Learning Framework for Automated Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05475): Frames automated code optimization as max-reward reinforcement learning, extending coding models from correctness toward performance improvement.
- [An Empirical Study of Policy-as-Code Adoption in Open-Source Software Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05555): Empirical adoption candidate; analyzes how open-source projects use policy-as-code and should be treated as process or governance evidence rather than model training.
- [Enhancing Algorithm Comprehension for Visually Impaired Individuals With LLM-Based Programming Code Segmentation](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2025.3638804): Segments programming code into LLM-generated explanatory units so visually impaired learners can follow algorithm structure more easily.
- [Enhancing Industrial Data Access with Text-to-SQL using Portuguese LLMs and LangGraph](https://doi.org/10.1109/iscmi67495.2025.11358511): Uses Portuguese LLMs with a LangGraph Text-to-SQL workflow for industrial data access, making it a workflow-backed SQL generation entry.
- [Enhancing Text-to-SQL generation with language sequential consistency](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2025.131721): Text-to-SQL model entry; uses language sequential consistency as a generation constraint or validation signal for SQL outputs.
- [Root Cause Analysis of RISC-V Build Failures via LLM and MCTS Reasoning](https://doi.org/10.1109/ase63991.2025.00227): Combines template-based log compression, phase-aware few-shot LLM anomaly detection, and MCTS over domain knowledge to diagnose 117 real RISC-V build failures.
- [Coding-Fuse: Efficient Fusion of Code Pre-Trained Models for Classification Tasks](https://doi.org/10.1109/ase63991.2025.00157): Selects adaptable layers from multiple code pretrained models with evidence theory and fuses them by soft voting for greener software-engineering classification.
- [Peeking inside the black box: Training data exposure in code language models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jss.2025.112729): Parent-review candidate; studies memorization and training-data exposure risks in code language models rather than adding a new coding model recipe.
- [Spec2Code: Mapping Protocol Specification to Function-Level Code Implementation](https://doi.org/10.1109/ase63991.2025.00174): Maps RFC requirements to function-level protocol implementations with LLM matching, relevance filtering, and clustering, then uses the mappings to find consistency bugs.
- [Harnessing code domain insights: Enhancing programming Knowledge Tracing with Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.knosys.2025.113396): Uses code-domain signals to adapt LLM-based programming knowledge tracing for learning-state prediction.
- [Combining Code Generating Large Language Models and Self-Play to Iteratively Refine Strategies in Games](https://doi.org/10.24963/ijcai.2025/1249): Off-axis games candidate; uses code-generating LLMs and self-play to iteratively refine game strategies.
- [An Elastic Coding and Decoding Method for Satellite Remote Sensing Image Semantic Transmission](https://doi.org/10.1109/tnse.2025.3632547): Off-axis communications candidate; proposes elastic coding and decoding for semantic transmission of satellite remote-sensing images.
- [MDVul: A semantic-based complex dependency code vulnerability detection using fusion path](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.inffus.2025.103475): Detects code vulnerabilities by fusing semantic and complex dependency paths.
- [A Retrospective of ChangeDistiller: Tree Differencing for Fine-Grained Source Code Change Extraction](https://doi.org/10.1109/tse.2025.3538326): Survey and historical-method candidate; revisits ChangeDistiller's tree differencing for fine-grained source-code change extraction.
- [Transforming Code Development with Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccns66249.2025.11428578): Survey or adoption candidate; discusses how LLMs change code-development practice rather than a specific model recipe.
- [LLM-Driven Fortran-to-C/C++ Portability for Parallel Scientific Codes](https://doi.org/10.1109/escience65000.2025.00083): Uses LLMs to port parallel scientific Fortran code to C or C++.
- [Harnessing the Power of LLMs for Code Smell Detection in Terraform Infrastructure as Code](https://doi.org/10.1109/compsac65507.2025.00075): Applies LLMs to detect code smells in Terraform infrastructure-as-code.
- [An Empirical Study of Exploring the Capabilities of Large Language Models in Code Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/tse.2025.3609876): Empirical education candidate; evaluates LLM capabilities for code-learning scenarios.
- [A Metaprotocol For a Family of Rapid Multivocal Reviews of Generative AI in the Software Industry](https://doi.org/10.5753/sbes.2025.11577): Survey candidate; defines a metaprotocol for rapid multivocal reviews of generative AI in software-industry practice.
- [BinEGA: Enhancing DNN-based Binary Code Similarity Detection through Efficient Graph Alignment](https://doi.org/10.1109/saner64311.2025.00052): Improves DNN-based binary code similarity detection with efficient graph alignment.
- [A Novel Approach to Generative AI-based Optimized Code Generation for Semiconductor Equipment Interfaces](https://doi.org/10.23919/icact63878.2025.10936654): Uses generative AI to produce optimized interface code for semiconductor equipment.
- [Self-Adaptive Quantum and Classical Software Systems](https://doi.org/10.1109/qce65121.2025.00089): Quantum-software candidate; discusses self-adaptive software systems spanning quantum and classical components.
- [Locus: A Proposal for Quantum Software Composition](https://doi.org/10.4230/oasics.programming.2025.17): Quantum-software candidate; proposes a composition approach for quantum software rather than a code LLM.
- [Examining the Performance of Mamba Tab for Software Defect Prediction](https://doi.org/10.1109/bigcomp64353.2025.00010): Evaluates the MambaTab architecture on software defect prediction.
- [Fedvuln: Scalable and privacy-preserving federated graph learning for smart contract vulnerability detection on parallel systems](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.future.2025.108264): Uses federated graph learning for privacy-preserving smart-contract vulnerability detection at scale.
- [Enhancing Python Code Security: A Comparison of Machine Learning, ChatGPT, and Static Analysis Methods](https://doi.org/10.1109/icecer65523.2025.11401125): Compares machine learning, ChatGPT, and static analysis for Python code-security detection.
- [Adversarial Training for Robustness Enhancement in LLM-Based Code Vulnerability Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/cisce65916.2025.11065803): Applies adversarial training to improve robustness of LLM-based code vulnerability detectors.
- [Fidelity of Cloud Emulators: The Imitation Game of Testing Cloud-Based Software](https://doi.org/10.1109/icse55347.2025.00077): Bench and testing-infrastructure candidate; evaluates how faithfully cloud emulators support testing of cloud-based software.
- [AutoGuard: Reporting Breaking Changes of REST APIs from Java Spring Boot Source Code](https://doi.org/10.1109/saner64311.2025.00083): Analyzes Java Spring Boot source code to report breaking changes in REST APIs.
- [Joint Adaptive Modulation Coding and Power Optimization in Heterogeneous Networks Based on Constrained Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/twc.2025.3609334): Off-axis networking candidate; uses constrained deep reinforcement learning for modulation coding and power optimization in heterogeneous networks.
- [SABLM-VD: Vulnerability detection with a semantic-aware binary language model](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.infsof.2025.107959): Uses a semantic-aware binary language model for vulnerability detection in binary code.
- [COSTAR: Software Code Smell Detection Through Tree-Based Abstract Representation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tr.2025.3648404): Detects code smells using tree-based abstract representations of software code.
- [LLM-assisted Performance Estimation of Embedded Software on RISC-V Processors](https://doi.org/10.1109/ddecs63720.2025.11006767): Uses LLMs to estimate embedded-software performance on RISC-V processors.
- [TSDCG: Tabular Synthetic Data with Code Generation LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1109/icici65870.2025.11069449): Data-synthesis candidate; uses code-generation LLMs to generate tabular synthetic data.
- [AutoOptiCode-LLM: An Autonomous Large Language Model Framework for Intelligent Code Generation and Optimization](https://doi.org/10.1109/icicnis66685.2025.11315793): Agent Harness candidate; wraps an LLM in an autonomous framework for code generation and optimization.
- [Introduction to the Special Section on software engineering for hybrid quantum computing systems](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jss.2025.112362): Survey and editorial candidate; frames software-engineering issues for hybrid quantum computing systems.
- [AI for Productivity: Transforming Enterprise Software Development](https://doi.org/10.1109/mc.2025.3543181): Industry adoption candidate; discusses AI-driven productivity changes in enterprise software development.
- [Unified Artificial Intelligence and Formal Reasoning Approach to Software Security](https://doi.org/10.1109/icmnwc66779.2025.11354207): Combines AI with formal reasoning for software-security analysis.
- [Agile Software Development for Cost Cutting and Operational Efficiency](https://doi.org/10.1109/icscds65426.2025.11166951): Off-axis process candidate; discusses agile development for cost and operational efficiency, not a model-side method.
- [Neural Representations for Scalable Video Coding](https://doi.org/10.1109/icme59968.2025.11209859): Off-axis video-compression candidate; learns neural representations for scalable video coding.
- [Attentionsmelling: Using Large Language Models to Identify Code Smells](https://doi.org/10.5753/sbes.2025.9921): Uses LLMs to identify code smells.
- [Weir: Scalable RDMA With Delay-Based RNIC Cache Control Software Middleware for Data Center Networks](https://doi.org/10.1109/ton.2025.3635230): Off-axis systems candidate; introduces delay-based RNIC cache-control middleware for scalable RDMA.
- [FIN: Boosting binary code embedding by normalizing function inlinings](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jss.2025.112603): Improves binary code embeddings by normalizing function inlining effects.
- [Large Language Model Company Policies and Policy Implications in Software Organizations](https://doi.org/10.1109/ms.2025.3622039): Survey and governance candidate; analyzes LLM company policies and their implications for software organizations.
- [Bug or feature? Investigating the impact of generative AI on knowledge creation in software engineering](https://doi.org/10.1108/vjikms-05-2025-0209): Empirical adoption candidate; studies how generative AI affects knowledge creation in software engineering.
- [From description to prescription: Unraveling log severity adjustments in open-source software](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jss.2025.112643): Mines open-source projects to characterize how developers adjust log severity from descriptive evidence to prescriptive guidance.
- [Is AI Code Generation Undermining Developers’ Problem‑Solving Skills?](https://doi.org/10.62762/jse.2025.847963): Education and human-factors candidate; studies whether AI code generation weakens developer problem-solving skills.
- [Advancing zero-shot humorous video understanding with test-time humor knowledge augmentation](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patcog.2025.113014): Off-axis video-understanding candidate; uses test-time humor knowledge augmentation for zero-shot humorous video understanding.
- [Prompt-to-G-Code Generation Using Large Language Models for Additive Manufacturing](https://doi.org/10.1109/csitss67709.2025.11294195): Uses LLMs to translate natural-language prompts into G-code for additive manufacturing.
- [LLMSDH: An Integrated Software-assisted Development Tool Based on Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/qrs-c65679.2025.00087): Agent Harness candidate; integrates LLM assistance into a software-development tool.
- [On the Performance of Large Language Models for Code Change Intent Classification](https://doi.org/10.1109/saner64311.2025.00062): Evaluates LLMs on code-change intent classification.
- [Refactoring Prediction Using Multimodal Representation of Code Snippets and Comments](https://doi.org/10.1109/giet65294.2025.11234820): Predicts refactoring with multimodal representations that combine code snippets and comments.
- [Offline and Continual Just-in-Time Software Defect Prediction with Pre-trained Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/smc58881.2025.11343039): Uses pretrained language models for offline and continual just-in-time defect prediction.
- [Breaking the Code Barrier: Analyzing the Effectiveness of LLMs in Natural Language Interfaces for Collaborative Robotics](https://doi.org/10.1109/airc64931.2025.11077511): Off-axis robotics candidate; evaluates LLMs as natural-language interfaces for collaborative robots.
- [Metamorphic-Based Many-Objective Distillation of LLMs for Code-Related Tasks](https://doi.org/10.1109/icse55347.2025.00230): Uses metamorphic relations to guide many-objective distillation of LLMs for code-related tasks.
- [MetaIndux-PLC: A Control Logic-Guided LLM for PLC code generation in industrial control systems](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.asoc.2025.113673): Guides LLM PLC code generation with control-logic knowledge for industrial control systems.
- [Semantic-Aligned Code Summarization: Bridging the Gap Between Code and Natural Language Through Data Flow Analysis](https://doi.org/10.1109/tnnls.2025.3581792): Aligns natural-language code summaries with data-flow dependencies so summaries reflect program semantics rather than surface tokens alone.
- [OpCodeBERT: A Method for Python Code Representation Learning by BERT With Opcode](https://doi.org/10.1109/tse.2025.3610244): Represents Python programs with opcode-aware BERT features, using compiled execution-level signals alongside source text.
- [Configuring Parameters of Search-based Software Engineering Tools with Multiple LLM Models](https://doi.org/10.5753/sbes.2025.11005): Uses multiple LLMs to recommend configurations for search-based software-engineering tools, a tool-configuration study rather than a new code model.
- [Leveraging AI for Automated Code Generation from Systems Engineering Specifications](https://doi.org/10.4271/2025-01-0295): Generates implementation code from systems-engineering specifications, connecting requirements artifacts to executable software in specification-heavy workflows.
- [Cross-Level Requirements Tracing Based on Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/tse.2025.3572094): Uses LLMs for cross-level requirements tracing, linking high-level requirements to lower-level software artifacts across lifecycle stages.
- [Optimizing text-to-SQL conversion techniques through the integration of intelligent agents and large language models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ipm.2025.104136): Combines LLMs with agent orchestration for Text-to-SQL optimization, making the retained entry workflow evidence around SQL generation.
- [A comparative analysis on using GPT and BERT for automated vulnerability scoring](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.iswa.2025.200515): Compares GPT-style and BERT-style models for automated vulnerability scoring, positioning generative and encoder models on security severity prediction.
- [An enhanced transformer-based framework for interpretable code clone detection](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jss.2025.112347): Uses a Transformer framework for interpretable code clone detection, exposing clone evidence rather than only returning a similarity label.
- [Adapting Installation Instructions in Rapidly Evolving Software Ecosystems](https://doi.org/10.1109/tse.2025.3552614): Adapts installation instructions as dependencies and software ecosystems change, treating documentation maintenance as an AI-assisted software task.
- [DSL-Xpert 2.0: Enhancing LLM-driven code generation for domain-specific languages](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.infsof.2025.107954): Extends LLM-driven generation to domain-specific languages, targeting DSL syntax and semantics rather than general-purpose code alone.
- [GNNContext: GNN-based Code Context Prediction for Programming Tasks](https://doi.org/10.1109/tse.2025.3578390): Predicts code context with a graph neural network so programming models can use structural neighborhood information around code elements.
- [LLM-Driven Code Generation for Neural Networks on FPGAs: Bridging Python and HLS](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccd65941.2025.00090): Translates neural-network Python into FPGA HLS code with LLM-driven generation, bridging ML code and hardware-synthesis constraints.
- [GRAPHCODEBERT-GAT: Hierarchical Graph Attention Networks for Explainable Neural Code Retrieval](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccwamtip68645.2025.11352717): Combines GraphCodeBERT with hierarchical graph attention to make neural code retrieval explainable through graph-structured evidence.
- [DetectorsForge: A Software Product Line for Transfer-learning in Code Smells Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/aiccsa66935.2025.11315279): Packages transfer-learning variants for code-smell detection as a software product line, allowing smell detectors to be configured across projects and tasks.
- [Detecting and Mitigating Inconsistencies Between Code, Documentation and Tests](https://doi.org/10.1109/ase63991.2025.00397): Detects inconsistencies among code, documentation, and tests, then mitigates mismatches so maintained artifacts stay synchronized.
- [Constraint Discovery for Structured Generation via LLM-Guided SMT Inference](https://doi.org/10.1109/icsme64153.2025.00059): Infers structured-generation constraints with LLM-guided SMT solving, making constraint discovery a verifier-backed generation step.
- [BinCOP: Automated mining of code reuse paths for binary component oriented programming](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ins.2025.122563): Mines code reuse paths for binary component-oriented programming, treating binary reuse as a graph and mining problem rather than source-only completion.
- [A System Development Code Auto-Completion Method Based on Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/ai3e69313.2025.00032): Applies deep reinforcement learning to system-development code autocompletion, using sequential feedback to select completion actions.
- [Lightweight Code Coverage Analysis for Deep Learning Library Fuzzing](https://doi.org/10.1109/dsc67331.2025.00090): Provides lightweight coverage analysis for fuzzing deep-learning libraries, giving fuzzers cheaper feedback about exercised library code.
- [MTL-CR: A Multitask Learning Approach for Code Representation](https://doi.org/10.1109/apsec66846.2025.00030): Uses multitask learning to train code representations across related software-engineering tasks.
- [Design of automatic code defect detection model based on Transformer](https://doi.org/10.1109/ecnct66493.2025.11172641): Builds a Transformer-based automatic code-defect detector, applying sequence modeling to defect prediction.
- [Using Prompt Tuning to Identify Relevant API Knowledge from API Tutorial and Stack Overflow](https://doi.org/10.1109/qrs65678.2025.00034): Uses prompt tuning to identify relevant API knowledge from tutorials and Stack Overflow.
- [CodeScent: A Deep Ensemble and LLM-Based Framework for Python and Java Code Smell Detection with Android Integration](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccit68739.2025.11490402): Adds an LLM and deep-ensemble method for code-smell detection.
- [Package-Aware Approach for Repository-Level Code Completion in Pharo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05617): Uses package-level repository context for code completion in Pharo, adding a language-specific but repository-aware model-side code-completion method.
- [Copiloting the future: How generative AI transforms Software Engineering](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.infsof.2025.107751): Information and Software Technology article on generative AI's effects across software engineering practice; evidence found here supports process-level transformation tracking rather than a specific code-model recipe.
- [Translating code with Large Language Models and human-in-the-loop feedback](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.infsof.2025.107785): Studies LLM-based code translation as an iterative human-feedback workflow, keeping expert review in the loop so translated programs can be corrected beyond one-shot model output.
- [OpGPT: LLM-driven Operator Generation Prompt Framework for Domain-Specific Architectures](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.procs.2025.07.117): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for software-development capability. Core idea: Published in Procedia Computer Science; metadata title: OpGPT: LLM-driven Operator Generation Prompt Framework for Domain-Specific Architectures.
- [Automating Software Diagram Generation with Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.procs.2025.09.191): Procedia Computer Science paper on using LLMs to generate software diagrams, extending AI-for-SE coverage from code text to design artifacts.
- [How Does Quantization Impact Privacy Risk on LLMS for Code?](https://doi.org/10.1109/aiware69974.2025.00013): AIware study of how quantizing code LLMs changes privacy risk, treating model compression as a deployment variable for memorization and leakage in code generation.
- [Advancing Binary Code Similarity Detection via Context-Content Fusion and LLM Verification](https://doi.org/10.1109/ase63991.2025.00033): Combines binary-code context and content features with LLM verification for similarity detection, extending code models to compiled-code comparison.
- [Can Mamba Be Better? An Experimental Evaluation of Mamba in Code Intelligence](https://doi.org/10.1109/ase63991.2025.00155): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for software-development capability. Core idea: Published in 2025 40th IEEE/ACM International Conference on Automated Software Engineering (ASE); metadata title: Can Mamba Be Better?
- [Evaluating Program Coverage for Code-Model Training](https://doi.org/10.1109/ase63991.2025.00379): Studies program coverage as a signal for code-model training data. Core idea: make coverage of programming behaviors explicit when evaluating or constructing code-model corpora.
- [LLMs in Debate: Does Arguing Make Them Better at Detecting Metamorphic Relations?](https://doi.org/10.1109/asew67777.2025.00019): Tests whether debate-style interaction improves LLM detection of metamorphic relations, turning software-testing oracle identification into a model-side reasoning and agreement protocol.
- [Code Summarization Using Mamba-SoTaNa](https://doi.org/10.1109/bigcomp64353.2025.00038): Applies a Mamba-SoTaNa sequence model to code summarization, adding a state-space-model alternative to Transformer-only summary generation.
- [Exploration of Code Analysis Driven Unit Test Generation Method Based on Llm](https://doi.org/10.1109/cisp-bmei68103.2025.11259220): Uses code-analysis signals to guide LLM-based unit-test generation, making structural program information part of the test synthesis prompt or generation process.
- [Exploring Large Language Models for Analyzing and Improving Method Names in Scientific Code](https://doi.org/10.1109/esem64174.2025.00030): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for software-development capability. Core idea: Published in 2025 ACM/IEEE International Symposium on Empirical Software Engineering and Measurement (ESEM); metadata title: Exploring Large Language Models for Analyzing and Improving Method Names in Scientific Code.
- [From Assessment to Enhancement of Pull Requests at Scale: Aligning Code Reviews with Developer Competencies Using Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/esem64174.2025.00061): Uses LLMs to assess and improve pull requests at scale against developer competencies.
- [Enhancing Code Optimization in LLMs with Dual Encoder Architecture for Syntax and Semantic Refinement](https://doi.org/10.1109/icaide65466.2025.11189637): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [Automating Software Test Case Generation with Pre-trained Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/iceccme64568.2025.11277902): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for software-development capability. Core idea: Published in 2025 5th International Conference on Electrical, Computer, Communications and Mechatronics Engineering (ICECCME); metadata title: Automating Software Test Case Generation with Pre-trained Large Language Models.
- [Enabling Architecture Traceability by LLM-based Architecture Component Name Extraction](https://doi.org/10.1109/icsa65012.2025.00011): Uses LLMs for architecture-component name extraction to support architecture traceability.
- [Large Language Model assisted Transformation of Software Variants into a Software Product Line](https://doi.org/10.1109/icsr66718.2025.00008): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [Prefix-Tuning for Large Language Models Enhanced with Hierarchical Graph Semantics in CI/CD Workflow Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/ispds67367.2025.11391071): Combines prefix tuning with hierarchical graph semantics so LLMs generate CI/CD workflows from structured pipeline context.
- [Tuning DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite-Base for C# Code Smell Detection: Advancing Towards Task Versatility in Software Maintenance](https://doi.org/10.1109/meco66322.2025.11049257): Fine-tunes DeepSeek-Coder for C# code-smell detection.
- [Prompt Learning for Source Code Summarization](https://doi.org/10.1109/qrs65678.2025.00028): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [Generating Secure Artificial Intelligence Model Source Code: A Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://doi.org/10.1109/spw67851.2025.00037): A reinforcement-learning approach for secure source-code generation. Core idea: optimize code generation toward security-aware objectives instead of only functional completion.
- [Just-in-Time Detection of Outdated Comments in Software Development by Jointly Reasoning](https://doi.org/10.1109/tce.2025.3535632): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [Enhancing Code Transformation in Large Language Models Through Retrieval-Augmented Fine-Tuning](https://doi.org/10.1109/tce.2025.3565294): Adds a retrieval-augmented fine-tuning route for code transformation, making it relevant to code-model adaptation beyond one-shot generation.
- [DockerFill: Automatically Completing Dockerfile Code With Syntax-Aware Multi-Task Learning](https://doi.org/10.1109/tse.2025.3632074): Completes Dockerfile code with syntax-aware multi-task learning.
- [A Novel Approach to Test Case Generation in Software Testing Using Pre-Trained Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/wincom65874.2025.11313396): Uses pretrained LLMs for software test-case generation, expanding code-model coverage to test synthesis rather than application code alone.
- [RepoFusion-in-Decoder: Efficient Cross-File Code Completion via Lightweight Encoder Fusion](https://doi.org/10.1109/aann66429.2025.11257694): Adds lightweight encoder fusion for efficient cross-file code completion, targeting repository context without full-context decoding cost.
- [DeepTriFix: An LLM-Based Code Defect Detection Framework Guided Abstract Syntax Tree and Prompt Reasoning](https://doi.org/10.1109/aiotc66747.2025.11198685): Guides LLM-based code defect detection with abstract syntax trees and prompt reasoning.
- [Improving Quality of LLM Code Generation in Low-Resource Programming Languages via Uncertainty Estimation](https://doi.org/10.1109/ase63991.2025.00399): Uses uncertainty estimation to improve LLM code generation quality in low-resource programming languages.
- [Repository-Level Code Smell Detection Based on Multi-Scale Code Information and LLM Assistance](https://doi.org/10.1109/ijcnn64981.2025.11228932): Combines multi-scale code information with LLM assistance to detect code smells at repository level.
- [Structured Instruction Tuning for Syntax-Semantic Fused Software Defect Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/mind67540.2025.11351533): Tunes code models with syntax-semantic fused instructions for software-defect detection.
- [An Empirical Study of Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning in Code Change Learning and Beyond](https://doi.org/10.1109/tse.2025.3637335): Studies how PEFT methods transfer across code-change learning tasks, adding a model-adaptation line for software-maintenance models.
- [CodeWisp: AST Guided Retrieval Augmented Generation for Code Generation and Completion](https://doi.org/10.1109/wincom65874.2025.11313399): Uses AST-guided retrieval-augmented generation to improve code generation and completion with structure-aware context.
- [Weights to Code: Extracting Interpretable Algorithms from the Discrete Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05770): Interprets a discrete Transformer by extracting algorithmic code from weights, making learned program-like behavior inspectable.
- [CSR-RAG: An Efficient Retrieval System for Text-to-SQL on the Enterprise Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06564): Optimizes enterprise Text-to-SQL retrieval over schemas and documents, a retrieval-system contribution around SQL generation rather than model training.
- [FairRF: Multi-Objective Search for Single and Intersectional Software Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07537): Uses multi-objective search to optimize single and intersectional fairness goals in software systems.
- [GRPO with State Mutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07593): Applies GRPO-style reinforcement learning with state mutations to LLM-based hardware test-plan generation.
- [Hybrid Distillation with CoT Guidance for Edge-Drone Control Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08412): Uses chain-of-thought guided hybrid distillation to generate edge-drone control code.
- [LLMs in Code Vulnerability Analysis: A Proof of Concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08691): Proof-of-concept study of LLMs for code vulnerability analysis.
- [Memo-SQL: Structured Decomposition and Experience-Driven Self-Correction for Training-Free NL2SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10011): Memo-SQL reuses historical error-fix pairs and structured question decomposition at test time, improving NL2SQL self-correction without additional model training.
- [RM -RF: Reward Model for Run-Free Unit Test Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.13097): Trains a run-free reward model to predict compile, coverage, and mutation-test signals for generated unit tests, reducing feedback cost in code-RL loops.
- [SWE-Tester: Training Open-Source LLMs for Issue Reproduction in Real-World Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.13713): Software testing is crucial for ensuring the correctness and reliability of software systems.
- [Software Testing in the Quantum World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.13996): Survey and position candidate; outlines software-testing challenges that arise when software targets quantum computing systems.
- [Lost in Transcription: How Speech-to-Text Errors Derail Code Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15339): Studies how speech-to-text errors degrade code understanding, relevant to voice-driven programming interfaces rather than code-model training.
- [Combining Tests and Proofs for Better Software Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16239): Verification survey and method note; links tests with SMT-backed proofs through counterexample generation and Design by Contract rather than introducing a code LLM.
- [Revisiting the Role of Natural Language Code Comments in Code Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16661): Tests how natural-language comments affect code translation, separating helpful semantic hints from noisy or misleading context.
- [Reflexa: Uncovering How LLM-Supported Reflection Scaffolding Reshapes Creativity in Creative Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17769): HCI and creative-coding candidate; studies how LLM-supported reflection scaffolding changes creative coding practice.
- [daVinci-Dev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18418): Studies agent-native mid-training with contextual and executable trajectories for software-engineering agents before post-training.
- [Detecting and Correcting Hallucinations in LLM-Generated Code via Deterministic AST Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19106): Detects and repairs knowledge-conflicting hallucinations in generated code with deterministic AST analysis, extending code-generation reliability methods beyond LLM-in-the-loop repair.
- [Not All Tokens Matter: Data-Centric Optimization for Efficient Code Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20147): Optimizes code summarization data by identifying which code tokens matter for efficient model training or inference.
- [LogSieve: Task-Aware CI Log Reduction for Sustainable LLM-Based Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20148): Reduces CI logs for sustainable LLM-based software analysis.
- [Control Models for IDE Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20223): Trains control models that steer IDE completions toward developer-preferred behavior while preserving completion quality.
- [SERA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20789): Generates soft-verified repository trajectories for low-cost SFT of open, repository-specialized coding agents.
- [Detecting Multiple Semantic Concerns in Tangled Code Commits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21298): Detects multiple semantic concerns in tangled code commits.
- [SWE-Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22129): Reuses and branches prior trajectories for low-cost test-time scaling of modern SWE agents.
- [CVeDRL: An Efficient Code Verifier via Difficulty-aware Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22803): Trains an efficient code verifier with difficulty-aware reinforcement learning.
- [DreamOn: Diffusion Language Models For Code Infilling Beyond Fixed-size Canvas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01326): Adds dynamic variable-length infilling to diffusion language models through two length-control states, letting Dream-Coder and DiffuCoder expand or contract completions without architecture changes.
- [Kimi K2.5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02276): Extends the Kimi agentic line with visual-text joint training, reinforcement learning, and Agent Swarm for multimodal coding and agentic tasks.
- [SWE-Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02361): Scales verifiable SWE environments to hundreds of thousands of tasks and demonstrates their use for agentic mid-training and reinforcement learning.
- [SWE-World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03419) ([code](https://github.com/RUCAIBox/SWE-World)): Replaces Docker execution with learned surrogate feedback models, enabling Docker-free SFT, RL, and test-time scaling for SWE agents.
- [Formal Evidence Generation for Assurance Cases for Robotic Software Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03550): Agent Harness and assurance candidate; generates formal evidence to support assurance cases for robotic software models.
- [Can We Classify Flaky Tests Using Only Test Code? An LLM-Based Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05465): Tests three LLMs and three prompting styles on two flaky-test benchmarks, finding best-case performance only marginally above random because test code alone often lacks the needed flakiness evidence.
- [Automated Customization of LLMs for Enterprise Code Repositories Using Semantic Scopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05780): Customizes LLMs for enterprise code repositories with semantic scopes.
- [VisRefiner: Learning from Visual Differences for Screenshot-to-Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05998): Trains screenshot-to-code models from visual differences between rendered outputs and target designs.
- [Correctness-Optimized Residual Activation Lens (CORAL): Transferrable and Calibration-Aware Inference-Time Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06022): Uses regularized MLP probes over residual activations for inference-time MCQA steering, improving calibration and accuracy without retraining but not targeting software tasks.
- [Towards Understanding What State Space Models Learn About Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06774): Compares SSM and Transformer code models, introduces SSM-Interpret to expose fine-tuning spectral shifts toward short-range dependencies, and uses the diagnosis to improve SSM code architectures.
- [Artificial Intelligence in Open Source Software Engineering: A Foundation for Sustainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07071): Literature-review candidate; surveys AI uses for OSS sustainability across bug triage, maintenance, contributor onboarding, community analytics, vulnerability detection, and task automation.
- [Pull Requests as a Training Signal for Repo-Level Code Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07457): Clean-PR converts noisy GitHub pull-request diffs into validated search/replace edit blocks, building a 2M-PR mid-training corpus for agentless repository-level editing.
- [Still Manual? Automated Linter Configuration via DSL-Based LLM Compilation of Coding Standards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07783): LintCFG parses natural-language coding standards into a tool-agnostic DSL, matches DSL rules to linter options, verifies consistency, and emits Checkstyle or ESLint configurations.
- [Specification Vibing for Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08263): VibeRepair translates buggy code into structured behavior specifications, repairs specification misalignments, and synthesizes patches guided by the corrected behavior intent.
- [Map of Encoders - Mapping Sentence Encoders using Quantum Relative Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08740): Off-axis model-map candidate; represents 1,101 sentence encoders by PIP matrices and quantum-relative-entropy features to visualize encoder relationships and infer downstream performance.
- [Toward Linking Declined Proposals and Source Code: An Exploratory Study on the Go Repository](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09467): Builds an LLM-driven traceability pipeline that links declined Go language proposals to source-code locations, then analyzes granularity selection, link precision, and failure modes.
- [Verifiable Provenance of Software Artifacts with Zero-Knowledge Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11887): Uses zkVM-based compilation to output both a binary and a cryptographic proof that the claimed compiler processed the claimed source, blocking compiler-substitution and source-tampering attacks.
- [Improving HPC Code Generation Capability of LLMs via Online Reinforcement Learning with Real-Machine Benchmark Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12049): Fine-tunes LLMs for high-performance code generation with online rewards measured on real supercomputer executions.
- [FlexAM: Flexible Appearance-Motion Decomposition for Versatile Video Generation Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13185): Off-axis video-generation candidate; controls image-to-video and video-to-video editing by decomposing appearance and motion through depth-aware 3D point-cloud control signals.
- [LogitsCoder: Towards Efficient Chain-of-Thought Path Search via Logits Preference Decoding for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14054): This is primarily a model-side test-time reasoning method: it steers token selection with Logits Preference Decoding, ranks diverse reasoning paths, and aggregates thoughts for code generation rather than adding a repository tool loop.
- [DALL: Data Labeling via Data Programming and Active Learning Enhanced by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14102): Off-axis data-labeling framework; combines configuration-defined labeling functions, active-learning review, and LLM-assisted refinement for text classification datasets.
- [Learning State-Tracking from Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14814): Converts permutation-composition state tracking into REPL-style code traces and shows linear RNNs can track explicit state reveals where Transformers struggle.
- [Social Life of Code: Modeling Evolution through Code Embedding and Opinion Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15412): Models open-source evolution by projecting code embeddings into opinion-dynamics trajectories, revealing developer influence and consensus patterns across GitHub repositories.
- [Accelerated Predictive Coding Networks via Direct Kolen-Pollack Feedback Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15571): Off-axis neural-training method; DKP-PC adds direct learnable feedback paths to predictive-coding networks to reduce error-propagation delay from depth-dependent to constant time.
