- [Stoichiometry Dependent Properties of Cerium Hydride: An Active Learning Developed Interatomic Potential Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16628): Off-axis materials-modeling work; trains an active-learning interatomic potential for cerium hydride and studies stoichiometry-dependent properties with molecular dynamics.
- [Simple Baselines are Competitive with Code Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16805): Shows that simple search baselines can match sophisticated code-evolution pipelines across mathematical bounds, agent scaffold design, and ML competitions, reframing gains around search-space design and evaluation variance.
- [Hybrid-Gym](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16819): Defines synthetic training tasks for codebase exploration, localization, dependency search, testing, and repair so coding agents learn transferable SWE skills before real issue solving.
- [LLM4Cov: Execution-Aware Agentic Learning for High-coverage Testbench Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16953): Models high-coverage hardware testbench generation as execution-aware offline agent learning with deterministic evaluators.
- [What to Cut? Predicting Unnecessary Methods in Agentic Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17091): Trains a review-support predictor for functions likely to be deleted from agent-generated pull requests, reaching 87.1 AUC on method-removal prediction.
- [CodeScaler: Scaling Code LLM Training and Test-Time Inference via Execution-Free Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17684): Trains a syntax-aware reward model from verified code preferences so code LLMs can use reinforcement learning and test-time selection without unit tests.
- [Mining Type Constructs Using Patterns in AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17955): Empirically compares TypeScript code from AI agents and humans, finding AI pull requests use `any` and type-check-bypassing constructs far more often despite higher acceptance rates.
- [ComUICoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19276): Generates reusable UI code for complex multi-page websites by semantic block segmentation, graph-based component merging, and priority-based element-level feedback.
- [Towards Automated Page Object Generation for Web Testing using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19294): Evaluates GPT-4o and DeepSeek Coder on generating Page Objects for five web apps, measuring ground-truth element accuracy and recognition rates for maintainable end-to-end tests.
- [From Restructuring to Stabilization: A Large-Scale Experiment on Iterative Code Readability Refactoring with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21833): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [Enhancing LLM-Based Test Generation by Eliminating Covered Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21997): Iteratively retrieves context, generates unit tests, measures coverage, and removes already covered code slices so LLMs focus on uncovered complex methods.
- [EyeLayer: Integrating Human Attention Patterns into LLM-Based Code Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22368): Adds a lightweight gaze-informed attention module that redistributes code-token embeddings from developer eye-tracking priors to improve LLaMA, Qwen, and CodeBERT summarization.
- [CUDA Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.24286): Combines synthetic CUDA-task generation, a verification-and-profiling development environment, and stable agentic reinforcement learning for high-performance kernel generation.
- [COG: Confidence-aware Optimal Geometric Correspondence for Unsupervised Single-reference Novel Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00493): Off-axis vision candidate; learns confidence-weighted optimal-transport correspondences for single-reference 6DoF object pose estimation.
- [Qwen3-Coder-Next](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00729): Reports an 80B-parameter open coding-agent model with 3B active parameters, trained on verifiable coding tasks, executable environments, mid-training, and reinforcement learning.
- [Compliance as Code: A Study of Linux Distributions and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01520): Empirical compliance-engineering candidate; analyzes more than 1,500 compliance rules across Linux distributions and maps their checks to Cyber Resilience Act requirements.
- [Code Fingerprints: Disentangled Attribution of LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04212): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [ReflexiCoder: Teaching Large Language Models to Self-Reflect on Generated Code and Self-Correct It via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05863): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [Balancing Latency and Accuracy of Code Completion via Local-Cloud Model Cascading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05974): Balances latency and accuracy by routing code-completion requests between local and cloud models.
- [Track-SQL: Enhancing Generative Language Models with Dual-Extractive Modules for Schema and Context Tracking in Multi-turn Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05996): Adds semantic schema extraction and schema-aware context extraction modules to generative Text-to-SQL models for multi-turn SparC and CoSQL interactions.
- [Breaking Training Bottlenecks: Effective and Stable Reinforcement Learning for Coding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07777): MicroCoder-GRPO improves code-model RL with conditional truncation masking, diversity-driven temperature selection, KL-loss removal, a harder dataset, and a faster evaluator.
- [Wrong Code, Right Structure: Learning Netlist Representations from Imperfect LLM-Generated RTL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09161): Uses structurally useful but functionally imperfect LLM-generated RTL as synthetic training data for netlist representation learning on circuit analysis tasks.
- [PIM-SHERPA: Software Method for On-device LLM Inference by Resolving PIM Memory Attribute and Layout Inconsistencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09216): Off-axis systems candidate; resolves cacheability and layout mismatches between prefill and decode so processing-in-memory LLM inference can run on product-level devices.
- [QuantumX: an experience for the consolidation of Quantum Computing and Quantum Software Engineering as an emerging discipline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10621): Quantum-software survey candidate; synthesizes the first QuantumX track on quality, governance, testing, orchestration, abstraction, and community-building for quantum software engineering.
- [EvolveCoder: Evolving Test Cases via Adversarial Verification for Code Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12698): Evolves solution-conditioned adversarial tests into the EvolveCoder-22k RL dataset, strengthening verifiable rewards and improving Qwen3-4B across code benchmarks.
- [MoEKD: Mixture-of-Experts Knowledge Distillation for Robust and High-Performing Compressed Code Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13213): Distills multiple specialized vulnerability-detection experts through a learned MoE router into compact CodeBERT and GraphCodeBERT models with stronger robustness.
- [TriFusion-LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15004): Fuses heuristic similarity priors, AST structure, and CodeBERT embeddings for seven-class BigCloneBench clone detection, then applies LLM arbitration only to high-uncertainty cases.
- [A Longitudinal Study of Usability in Identity-Based Software Signing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17133): Software-supply-chain usability candidate; mines 3,900 issues across Sigstore, OpenPubKey, Vault, Keyfactor, and Notary v2 to locate signing and verification friction.
- [FailureMem: A Failure-Aware Multimodal Framework for Autonomous Software Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17826): Agent-harness migration candidate; combines structured repair workflow, region-level screenshot perception tools, and a failure memory bank for SWE-bench Multimodal repair.
- [CodeScout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17829): Trains code-localization agents with reinforcement learning in standard Unix-terminal environments, focusing on reward design and repurposed SWE-agent environments for repository search.
- [scicode-lint: Detecting Methodology Bugs in Scientific Python Code with LLM-Generated Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17893): Generates methodology-bug patterns with frontier models at build time and runs a small local model at lint time to catch data leakage, cross-validation, and random-seed issues.
- [Defusing Logic Bombs in Symbolic Execution with LLM-Generated Ghost Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19239): Gordian asks LLMs to generate solver-friendly ghost code for inversions, surrogates, and heap partitions while KLEE retains precise global symbolic reasoning.
- [Engineering Pitfalls in AI Coding Tools: An Empirical Study of Bugs in Claude Code, Codex, and Gemini CLI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20847): Empirically codes more than 3,800 public bugs in Claude Code, Codex, and Gemini CLI, showing failures cluster around tool invocation, command execution, APIs, integration, and configuration.
- [On the Challenges and Opportunities of Learned Sparse Retrieval for Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22008): Reviews challenges and opportunities for learned sparse retrieval over code.
- [Steering Code LLMs with Activation Directions for Language and Library Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23629): Estimates activation-space steering vectors for language and library pairs, then injects them during generation to shift code LLM outputs toward target ecosystems.
- [Enhancing and Reporting Robustness Boundary of Neural Code Models for Intelligent Code Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24119): Reports robustness boundaries for neural code models in code understanding.
- [Error Understanding in Program Code With LLM-DL for Multi-label Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25005): Combines fine-tuned code encoders with GRU, LSTM, BiLSTM, and attention decoders for multi-label classification of student Python programming errors.
- [IncreRTL: Traceability-Guided Incremental RTL Generation under Requirement Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25769): Builds requirement-code traceability links so evolving hardware requirements trigger localized RTL regeneration instead of full redesign.
- [OSA: Echocardiography Video Segmentation via Orthogonalized State Update and Anatomical Prior-aware Feature Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26188): Off-axis medical-video candidate; stabilizes echocardiography segmentation with Stiefel-manifold state updates and anatomical-prior feature enhancement.
- [Finding Memory Leaks in C/C++ Programs via Neuro-Symbolic Augmented Static Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27224): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- ["An Endless Stream of AI Slop": The Growing Burden of AI-Assisted Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27249): Qualitatively analyzes 1,154 Reddit and Hacker News posts to characterize AI-slop review friction, quality degradation, incentives, and developer countermeasures.
- [TokenDial: Continuous Attribute Control in Text-to-Video via Spatiotemporal Token Offsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27520): Off-axis video-generation candidate; learns additive spatiotemporal token offsets that provide slider-style control over appearance and motion attributes.
- [KAT-Coder-V2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27703): Uses a specialize-then-unify recipe across SWE, web coding, terminal, web search, and general coding domains, followed by on-policy distillation and large-scale sandboxed RL.
- [DOne](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01226): Decouples structure understanding from element rendering for design-to-code generation with learned layout segmentation, hybrid element retrieval, and schema-guided code generation.
- [From SWE-ZERO to SWE-HERO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01496): Distills 300K execution-free and 13K execution-backed trajectories from Qwen3-Coder-480B into open Qwen2.5-Coder SWE agents for repository repair and multilingual transfer.
- [MM-ReCoder: Advancing Chart-to-Code Generation with Reinforcement Learning and Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01600): Applies reinforcement learning and execution-feedback self-correction to chart-to-code generation.
- [Generalization Bounds and Statistical Guarantees for Multi-Task and Multiple Operator Learning with MNO Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01961): Off-axis theory candidate; derives covering-number generalization bounds and sample-complexity tradeoffs for Multiple Neural Operator architectures.
- [LLMs as Idiomatic Decompilers: Recovering High-Level Code from x86-64 Assembly for Dart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02278): Trains small specialized LLM decompilers for x86-64-to-Dart recovery, comparing synthetic same-language augmentation with Swift-to-Dart transfer.
- [Ambig-IaC: Multi-level Disambiguation for Interactive Cloud Infrastructure-as-Code Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02382): Generates diverse IaC specifications, detects disagreements over resources, topology, and attributes, and asks targeted clarification questions on the Ambig-IaC benchmark.
- [Dependency-Guided Repository-Level C-to-Rust Translation with Reinforcement Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02852): Combines dependency-guided context modeling, large-scale C-Rust data, and reinforcement alignment for repository-level C-to-Rust translation.
- [LPC-SM: Local Predictive Coding and Sparse Memory for Long-Context Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03263): General long-context LM candidate; separates local attention, persistent memory, predictive correction, and sparse run-time control in a hybrid autoregressive architecture.
- [EffiPair: Improving the Efficiency of LLM-generated Code with Relative Contrastive Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05137): Improves efficiency of LLM-generated code with relative contrastive feedback.
- [Content Fuzzing for Escaping Information Cocoons on Digital Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05461): Off-axis social-media candidate; rewrites posts with confidence feedback from stance detectors so machine-inferred stance changes while human-interpreted intent is preserved.
- [Evaluating Repository-level Software Documentation via Question Answering and Feature-Driven Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06793): Benchmark migration candidate; SWD-Bench evaluates repository documentation through QA tasks for functionality detection, localization, and completion mined from pull requests.
- [An empirical study of LoRA-based fine-tuning of large language models for automated test case generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06946): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for software-development capability. Core idea: Automated test case generation from natural language requirements remains a challenging problem in software engineering due to the ambiguity of requirements and the need to produce structured, executable test artifacts.
- [An Empirical Analysis of Static Analysis Methods for Detection and Mitigation of Code Library Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07755): Measures how much static analysis can detect nonexistent library features in LLM-generated code, reporting tool-specific detection ranges and an upper bound from manual analysis.
- [ZeroCoder: Can LLMs Improve Code Generation Without Ground-Truth Supervision?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07864): Co-evolves coder and tester policies from self-generated code-test interactions, reducing reliance on human-curated tests for code-generation RLVR.
- [DeepGuard: Secure Code Generation via Multi-Layer Semantic Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09089): This is model-side secure-code generation work: it aggregates upper-layer representations into a security analyzer and inference-time steering signal to improve secure and correct code generation.
- [The Role of LLMs in Collaborative Software Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09120): HCI study candidate; observes 18 professional pairs using LLMs in software design, contrasting shared-instance understanding with parallel-use context drift and anchoring effects.
- [MR-Coupler: Automated Metamorphic Test Generation via Functional Coupling Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10126): Finds functionally coupled method pairs, asks LLMs for metamorphic test cases, and validates them with amplification and mutation analysis to reduce false alarms.
- [CodeComp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10235): Compresses KV cache for agentic coding by adding Joern code-property-graph priors so structurally critical tokens survive long-context inference.
- [SWE-Shepherd](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10493): Trains process reward models on SWE-Bench action trajectories to score intermediate repository-agent actions during inference without full reinforcement learning.
- [DynamicsLLM: a Dynamic Analysis-based Tool for Generating Intelligent Execution Traces Using LLMs to Detect Android Behavioural Code Smells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10661): Uses LLM-generated Android execution traces to trigger behavior-code-smell events that the Dynamics dynamic-analysis tool misses under limited action budgets.
- [Compliant But Unsatisfactory: The Gap Between Auditing Standards and Practices for Probabilistic Genotyping Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10875): Off-axis audit-governance candidate; analyzes ASB 018 and five probabilistic-genotyping audit reports to show how vague standards can permit compliant but weak audits.
- [RESP: Reference-guided Sequential Prompting for Visual Glitch Detection in Video Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11082): Uses reference-guided sequential prompting for visual glitch detection in video games.
- [AnomalyGen: Enhancing Log-Based Anomaly Detection with Code-Guided Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11107): Synthesizes labeled log sequences from source code using log-oriented control-flow graphs, LLM consistency checks, runtime-parameter generation, and heuristic labels.
- [Source-Attribution Regularization for Code Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14053): SA-BPE modifies BPE training with source attribution and merge skipping to reduce unused, under-trained, repository-specific code tokens without changing inference.
- [MARS^2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14564): Scales multi-agent tree search with reinforcement learning for code generation.
- [HintPilot: LLM-based Compiler Hint Synthesis for Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15041): Synthesizes compiler hints with LLMs for code optimization.
- [Exploring LLM-based Verilog Code Generation with Data-Efficient Fine-Tuning and Testbench Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15388): Uses multi-agent testbench generation to create higher-quality fine-tuning data for specification-to-Verilog models under limited HDL supervision.
- [LLM4C2Rust: Large Language Models for Automated Memory-Safe Code Transpilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15485): Uses segmented C and C++ inputs, retrieved Rust documentation and compiler-error context, an LLM, and a smaller model to reduce unsafe patterns in Rust transpilation.
- [Majority Voting for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15618): Functional Majority Voting selects among code generations by runtime execution-signature consensus and reuses the signal for label-free test-time reinforcement learning.
- [IACDM: Interactive Adversarial Convergence Development Methodology -- A Structured Framework for AI-Assisted Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16399): Methodology or harness candidate; proposes an eight-phase AI-assisted development process with problem discovery, persistent knowledge management, and adversarial verification gates.
- [Surgical Repair of Insecure Code Generation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16697): Finds security concepts encoded early but suppressed by format compliance near the final layer, then applies vulnerability-specific steering vectors to reduce insecure code generation.
- [AI Slop and the Software Commons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16754): Position candidate; frames cheap AI-generated software artifacts as a commons problem that shifts review and maintenance costs onto maintainers, teams, and educators.
- [Mitigating Prompt-Induced Cognitive Biases in General-Purpose AI for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16756): Uses PROBE-SWE biased and unbiased SE dilemmas to test prompt-debiasing strategies, then reduces bias sensitivity by injecting elicited best-practice axioms.
- [Improving LLM Code Reasoning via Semantic Equivalence Self-Play with Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17010): Trains generator and evaluator models through Haskell semantic-equivalence self-play, using Liquid Haskell proofs, counterexamples, and the OpInstruct-HSx dataset.
- [Layer-wise MoE Routing Locality under Shared-Prefix Code Generation: Token-Identity Decomposition and Compile-Equivalent Fork Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17182): Analyzes Qwen3.5 MoE routing overlap during shared-prefix code generation, separating token identity from compile-equivalent forks to expose search redundancy.
- [Augmenting unit test suites from integration tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17508): Uses static and dynamic analysis to derive isolated unit tests from integration tests in Node.js projects, improving test-pyramid balance and fault localization.
- [Program Structure-aware Language Models: Targeted Software Testing beyond Textual Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17715): GLMTest conditions Qwen2.5-Coder test generation on code-property graphs and target branches, improving branch-targeted test accuracy on TestGenEval.
- [When AI Models Become Dependencies: Studying the Evolution of Pre-Trained Model Reuse in Downstream Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17940): Empirical maintenance candidate; studies 4,988 releases across 323 OSS projects to show pretrained model dependencies accumulate, change less often than libraries, and are documented differently.
- [CodePivot: Bootstrapping Multilingual Transpilation in LLMs via Reinforcement Learning without Parallel Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18027): Trains multilingual transpilation through Python as an intermediate representation and an aggressive partial functional RL reward, avoiding pairwise parallel corpora across 10 languages.
- [LeGo-Code: Can Modular Curriculum Learning Advance Complex Code Generation? Insights from Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18254): Uses modular curriculum learning and adapter-based training to improve complex text-to-SQL generation under noisy schemas and nested query logic.
- [Towards Better Static Code Analysis Reports: Sentence Transformer-based Filtering of Non-Actionable Alerts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18525): STAF uses sentence-transformer embeddings to filter non-actionable static-analysis alerts, reducing alert fatigue while preserving actionable Java findings.
- [Choose Your Own Adventure: Non-Linear AI-Assisted Programming with EvoGraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18883): Agent Harness candidate; implements an IDE graph that records branching AI interactions and code changes so developers can compare, merge, and revisit non-linear programming states.
- [Cascaded Code Editing: Large-Small Model Collaboration for Effective and Efficient Code Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19201): Uses large-small model collaboration for efficient code editing without regenerating whole files unnecessarily.
- [CASCADE: Detecting Inconsistencies between Code and Documentation with Automatic Test Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19400): CASCADE converts code-documentation mismatch hypotheses into generated tests, using execution outcomes to reduce false positives in inconsistency reports.
- [Conjecture and Inquiry: Quantifying Software Performance Requirements via Interactive Retrieval-Augmented Preference Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21380): IRAP turns vague natural-language performance requirements into mathematical functions through retrieval-augmented preference elicitation with stakeholders.
- [What Makes Software Bugs Escape Testing? Evidence from a Large-Scale Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26672): Empirical testing entry: analyzes more than 14,000 C, C++, and Java defects to characterize post-release bugs by complexity, size, structure, and development-history metrics.
- [Static Program Slicing Using Language Models With Dataflow-Aware Pretraining and Constrained Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26961): Sliceformer adds dataflow-aware pretraining and constrained decoding so language models predict static slices without hallucinated tokens or dependency-breaking statements.
- [GenAI in Software Engineering: The Role of Technology Acceptance Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27642): Survey and methods-adoption entry: reviews how UTAUT-style technology acceptance models can study GenAI adoption in software engineering, with Bayesian analysis as a future direction.
- [Reliable Answers for Recurring Questions: Boosting Text-to-SQL Accuracy with Template Constrained Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.28028): TeCoD mines recurring query templates from labeled workloads and constrains decoding to those templates, improving Text-to-SQL validity on complex or unseen schemas.
- [GeoContra: From Fluent GIS Code to Verifiable Spatial Analysis with Geography-Grounded Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00782): GeoContra wraps LLM-generated Python GIS code in executable geospatial contracts, then applies static checks, runtime validation, semantic verification, and bounded repair.
- [Fine-Tuning Pre-Trained Code Models for AI-Generated Code Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01596): SemEval system paper fine-tunes CodeBERT, GraphCodeBERT, UniXcoder, and CodeT5+ for binary AI-code detection and 11-way generator attribution.
- [Standing on the Shoulders of Giants: Stabilized Knowledge Distillation for Cross--Language Code Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02860): Uses stabilized knowledge distillation for cross-language code clone detection, improving multilingual code understanding.
- [KVerus: Scalable and Resilient Formal Verification Proof Generation for Rust Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03822): KVerus targets Rust proof generation by bridging LLM semantic patterns with Verus structural dependencies, making proofs less brittle across modules and toolchain changes.
- [Reproduction Test Generation for Java SWE Issues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04320): Introduces TDD-Bench and a Java-focused reproduction-test generator that turns repository issues into tests expected to fail before a fix and pass afterward.
- [Patterns of Developer Adoption of LLM-Generated Code Refactoring Suggestions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04835): Empirically analyzes 169 GitHub commits linked to ChatGPT refactoring conversations to study which LLM suggestions developers actually adopt.
- [Identifier-Free Code Embedding Models for Scalable Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05251): This is model-side search infrastructure for reverse engineering, fine-tuning a Qwen3-Embedding model to associate source functions with stripped decompiled code without relying on identifiers.
- [TACT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05980): Detects overthinking and overacting as linear activation-space drift directions and uses test-time steering to improve long-horizon coding agents on SWE-bench Verified, Terminal-Bench 2.0, and CLAW-Eval.
- [Schedule-and-Calibrate: Utility-Guided Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning for Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06111): Uses utility-guided multi-task RL for code LLMs.
- [Boosting Automatic Java-to-Cangjie Translation with Multi-Stage LLM Training and Error Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07403): Improves Java-to-Cangjie translation with multi-stage LLM training and error repair, addressing scarce Cangjie parallel corpora and weak language-specific knowledge.
- [Bridging the Programming Language Gap: Constructing a Multilingual Shared Semantic Space through AST Unification and Graph Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07788): Constructs a multilingual code semantic space by unifying ASTs and matching graphs, supporting cross-language clone detection and retrieval between languages such as Java and Python.
- [What Software Engineering Looks Like to AI Agents? -- An Empirical Study of AI-Only Technical Discourse on MoltBook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08380): Empirical agent-discourse entry: compares 4,707 MoltBook technology posts with GitHub Discussions to characterize what autonomous agents discuss about software work.
- [BoostAPR: Boosting Automated Program Repair via Execution-Grounded Reinforcement Learning with Dual Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09134): Trains automated program repair models with execution-grounded reinforcement learning and dual reward models, using runnable feedback as the optimization signal.
- [FreeMOCA: Memory-Free Continual Learning for Malicious Code Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09664): FreeMOCA performs memory-free continual learning for malware analysis through adaptive layer-wise interpolation and plasticity-aware scheduling, reducing forgetting without replaying old samples.
- [Step Rejection Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10674): Uses critic-scored trajectory steps to retain unresolved SWE trajectories as context while masking erroneous steps during distillation.
- [Orchard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15040): Provides an open agentic-modeling stack for training software agents rather than only orchestrating them. Core idea: Orchard Env supplies reusable sandbox lifecycle primitives, while Orchard-SWE distills 107K coding trajectories, applies credit-assignment SFT to productive segments of unresolved runs, and then uses Balanced Adaptive Rollout for RL, reaching strong open-source SWE-bench Verified results.
- [Syntax Without Semantics: Teaching Large Language Models to Code in an Unseen Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15607): Uses an unseen imperative language to separate syntax acquisition from semantic code reasoning when training and evaluating code language models.
- [XSearch: Explainable Code Search via Concept-to-Code Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16046): Aligns concepts to code for explainable code search, strengthening retrieval and localization in software-development models.
- [LogGen](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jss.2026.112816): Combines traditional code analysis with LLM generation for precise log generation, adding a logging-specific software-model method.
- [Compiler-Runtime Co-operative Chain of Verification for LLM-Based Code Optimization](https://doi.org/10.1109/cgo68049.2026.11395240): Verifies LLM-suggested compiler optimizations with syntax checks, profiling checksums, Alive2 symbolic equivalence, and batched runtime checks inside an LLVM vectorizer.
- [CodeEnhancer: LLM-generated Python code enhancement through SAST integration and fine-tuning](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.knosys.2026.115925): Enhances LLM-generated Python by integrating static application security testing feedback with fine-tuning, making SAST findings part of the code-improvement signal.
- [Issue classification with LLMs: An empirical study of the NASA flight software systems](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jss.2026.112851): Parent-review candidate; empirically evaluates LLMs for classifying NASA flight-software issues, closer to software-engineering assessment than a model-training contribution.
- [Rosetta-XAI: An automated evaluation and explainability framework for code translation models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.simpa.2026.100811): Evaluates 15 code LLMs on 42 bidirectional Rosetta Code translation pairs with syntax, compilation, execution, and explainability checkpoints.
- [Airborne Software Requirements Generation Method Based on Memory-Enhanced Graph RAG](https://doi.org/10.1109/cacml68972.2026.11507104): Adds memory-enhanced Graph RAG to airborne software requirements generation so retrieved domain documents are combined with accumulated expert experience.
- [Generative Artificial Intelligence for Software Development Using ISO/IEC 29110 Basic Profile: Gaps and Opportunities](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2026.3659713): Survey or parent-review candidate; maps 52 studies on GenAI use in ISO/IEC 29110 small-enterprise software development rather than proposing a model.
- [VeriGen: An LLM-Augmented Framework for End-to-End Automation of Software Development Lifecycle–From Requirements Specifications to Code Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2026.3686308): Integrates requirements formalization, UML class and sequence diagram generation, code generation, and verification gates into an LLM-augmented lifecycle pipeline.
- [Towards Project-Aware Actionability Detection for Coding Rule Violations](https://doi.org/10.1109/saner-c67878.2026.00040): Uses an active-learning framework to identify which static-analysis rule violations are actionable in a specific project context.
- [Cost-Effective Adversarial Attacks Against Code LLM With Model Attention](https://doi.org/10.1109/tse.2026.3663143): Attacks code LLMs with attention-guided, semantics-preserving code mutations to expose robustness failures at lower query cost.
- [Refactoring techniques for software vulnerabilities](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jss.2026.112962): Parent-review candidate; focuses on vulnerability-oriented refactoring techniques and should be checked for Survey or software-security placement.
- [BMGANet: A deep learning model for source code vulnerability detection by integrating token-level and function-level features](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.compeleceng.2026.110999): Combines Joern-derived PDG and AST slices, normalized code, BERT features, LSTM temporal modeling, and attention for source-code vulnerability detection.
- [Token–Node Aligned Cross-Attention for Source-Code Vulnerability Detection](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.scico.2026.103492): Aligns token-level and graph-node representations with cross-attention for source-code vulnerability detection.
- [Automated Software Vulnerability Detection Using Transformer-Based Code Analysis](https://doi.org/10.1109/ictp67998.2026.11485262): Applies Transformer-based code analysis to automated vulnerability detection in increasingly complex software systems.
- [Towards multi-language repository-level code generation: From-scratch to guided tasks](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2026.133204): Studies repository-level code generation across multiple languages, contrasting from-scratch generation with guided tasks and correctness constraints.
- [Enhanced Text-to-SQL using cooperative reinforcement learning for small language models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2026.132865): Uses cooperative reinforcement learning to improve small-language-model Text-to-SQL generation, tying SQL correctness to an RL training signal.
- [A lightweight approach to software fault localization using static features of statements in cloud computing environments](https://doi.org/10.3389/fcomp.2026.1734298): Trains a lightweight ranking SVM that combines SBFL suspiciousness scores with statement-level static features for cross-project fault localization in cloud environments.
- [Model Reuse Through Retargeted-Architecture Binary Code Analysis](https://doi.org/10.1109/tdsc.2026.3672389): Reuses binary-analysis models across instruction-set architectures through retargeted-architecture analysis, addressing scarce training data for less common ISAs.
- [vSim: Semantics-Aware Value Extraction for Efficient Binary Code Similarity Analysis](https://doi.org/10.14722/ndss.2026.240213): Improves binary-code similarity analysis by extracting semantics-aware values from binaries before comparison.
- [Thinking Fast and Correct: Automated Rewriting of Numerical Code through Compiler Augmentation](https://doi.org/10.1109/cgo68049.2026.11395228): Augments the compiler to rewrite numerical code while balancing floating-point accuracy and performance under low-precision hardware constraints.
- [Detection of vulnerabilities in software for unmanned aerial vehicles by using large language models](https://doi.org/10.15587/1729-4061.2026.352029): Uses an LLM-guided instruction template and architecture to detect vulnerabilities in UAV binary software when source code is unavailable.
- [ExoCode: A Generative-Native IDE for Secure, Executable, and Iterative Code Prototyping](https://doi.org/10.1109/ic3ecsbhi67834.2026.11469052): Agent Harness parent-review candidate; implements a generative-native IDE with secure sandbox execution, iteration, debugging, and durable agent workflow around LLM-generated code.
- [Resolving ambiguity in code refinement via conidfine: A conversationally-Aware framework with disambiguation and targeted retrieval](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neunet.2026.108650): Uses conversational disambiguation and targeted retrieval to resolve underspecified code-refinement requests before applying edits.
- [Rapid End-to-End Test Generation and Hallucination Mitigation Using Generative Artificial Intelligence](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2026.3657407): Generates distributed-system end-to-end tests with abstract-class DOM references and operation-specific prompts to reduce selector fragility and hallucinated steps.
- [Llm-Based Framework for Improving Code Quality in Oilfield Data Acquisition Systems](https://doi.org/10.1109/southeastcon63549.2026.11476591): Uses a multi-agent LLM framework to find context-sensitive reference errors such as configuration keys, API endpoints, and file paths in a large oilfield codebase.
- [Automated software engineering knowledge transfer: A case study on small and medium-sized software enterprises in Thailand](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jss.2026.112817): Parent-review candidate; reports knowledge-transfer interventions for automated-software-engineering tool adoption in Thai small and medium software enterprises, not a model method.
- [I Will Try to Fix You: Large Language Models for Mobile GUI Test Repair](https://doi.org/10.1109/saner-c67878.2026.00052): Studies mobile GUI test breakages across app versions and applies LLMs to repair obsolete tests affected by structural, visual, or property changes.
- [An MBSE approach for early validation of safety-critical software: Lessons learned from the cross-organizational development of the HTV-X automated docking system](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jsse.2026.01.003): Parent-review candidate; describes a SysML-based workflow for early validation of safety-critical spacecraft software, closer to process or systems-engineering coverage.
- [Language-Agnostic Reverse Engineering of Software into SysML v2 via LLM Reasoner](https://doi.org/10.1109/syscon66367.2026.11503570): Converts heterogeneous source files into SysML v2 through language detection, AST-to-XML normalization, rule-structured LLM prompting, iterative repair, and SysML validation.
- [SCOTT: Source Code Clone Detection Based on Semantic Mining from Graph and Text Data](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2026.132000): Detects source-code clones by combining graph-based structural mining with text-semantic representations.
- [Enhancing Semantic Code Search and Navigation Systems Using Dual Embedding Representations](https://doi.org/10.1109/icnwc68145.2026.11518020): CodeSense embeds source code and natural-language queries separately, maps them into a shared vector space, and supports semantic search across large repositories.
- [Improving Gemini's Ability to Generate Syntactically Correct Mermaid Code](https://doi.org/10.1109/southeastcon63549.2026.11476269): Adds a lightweight validation and repair framework for improving Gemini-generated Mermaid diagram syntax.
- [Drill-type adaptive debugging to boost programming skills](https://doi.org/10.1080/08993408.2026.2672717): Education candidate; studies adaptive debugging drills for programming-skills training rather than a reusable code-model recipe.
- [Semantic Code Smell Detection from Raw Source Code using Transformers with Chunking and Data Augmentation](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.mlwa.2026.100908): Evaluates CodeBERT and CodeT5 on raw-source code-smell detection with chunking strategies and text-level augmentation on the MLCQ dataset.
- [RAGdeterm: Deterministic retrieval-augmented generation for code generation](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.softx.2026.102636): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for software-development capability. Core idea: Published in SoftwareX; metadata title: RAGdeterm: Deterministic retrieval-augmented generation for code generation.
- [Source Code Error Categorization and Explanation by Retrieval Augmented Generation](https://doi.org/10.1109/aaiml67890.2026.11498214): Applies retrieval-augmented generation to source-code error categorization and explanation.
- [Automated Software Test Case Generation Using Directional Partially Weighted Ensemble Large Language Models With Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG)](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2026.3667925): Uses LLM ensembles and RAG for automated software test-case generation.
- [Design System-Compliant User Interface Generation with LLM Agents: A Comparative Study of Context Engineering Strategies](https://doi.org/10.1145/3772363.3798616): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for software-development capability. Core idea: Published in Proceedings of the Extended Abstracts of the 2026 CHI Conference on Human Factors in Computing Systems; metadata title: Design System-Compliant User Interface Generation with LLM Agents: A Comparative Study of Context Engineering Strategies.
- [Constrained LLM-Guided Refactoring of JavaScript: A Smell-Targeted Transformation Framework with Human-in-the-Loop Validation](https://doi.org/10.32604/cmc.2026.080737): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for software-development capability. Core idea: Published in Computers, Materials &amp; Continua; metadata title: Constrained LLM-Guided Refactoring of JavaScript: A Smell-Targeted Transformation Framework with Human-in-the-Loop Validation.
- [OpenMP-annotated code dataset for large language model fine-tuning on parallel programming tasks](https://doi.org/10.3389/fhpcp.2026.1771927): Provides an OpenMP-annotated code dataset for LLM fine-tuning on parallel programming.
- [SFS: Smarter Code Space Search improves LLM Inference Scaling](https://openreview.net/forum?id=MCHuGOkExF): Uses smarter search over code candidates as an inference-scaling route for programming tasks, complementing training-time RL with test-time code-space exploration.
- OpenAI Codex / `codex-1` ([official release](https://openai.com/index/introducing-codex/); [system-card addendum](https://openai.com/index/o3-o4-mini-codex-system-card-addendum/)): Reports an o3-derived software-engineering model optimized with reinforcement learning on real coding tasks, while the Codex CLI and cloud workflow remain harness-side artifacts.
- Claude 4 and Claude Sonnet 4.5 coding model cards ([Claude 4](https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-4); [Sonnet 4.5](https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-sonnet-4-5)): Provide closed-model evidence for long-running coding, repository repair, terminal work, and SWE-bench-style software-agent capability.
- [D3: A Dataset for Training Code LMs to Act Diff-by-Diff](https://openreview.net/forum?id=sy71y74U80): Trains code models on diff-by-diff source evolution, matching the incremental edit actions used by repository agents.
- Qwen3-Coder ([official report](https://qwenlm.github.io/blog/qwen3-coder/)): Trains a 480B-A35B MoE coding-agent model with code-heavy pretraining, execution-driven Code RL, and long-horizon Agent RL across large parallel coding environments.
- Grok Code Fast 1 ([model docs](https://docs.x.ai/docs/models/grok-code-fast-1)): A purpose-built agentic coding model with programming-rich pretraining, post-training on real PR and coding-task data, and terminal/file-editing tool behavior.
- GPT-5-Codex family ([GPT-5-Codex](https://openai.com/index/introducing-upgrades-to-codex/); [GPT-5.2-Codex](https://openai.com/index/introducing-gpt-5-2-codex/); [GPT-5.3-Codex](https://openai.com/index/introducing-gpt-5-3-codex/)): Tracks OpenAI's closed coding-agent model line across real engineering tasks, native compaction, long-running work, SWE-Bench Pro, Terminal-Bench, and vulnerability-oriented coding.
- [Afterburner](https://openreview.net/forum?id=At8OUlyTOu): Uses reinforcement learning to support self-improving code-efficiency optimization, extending code-agent training from correctness toward performance.
- [More Than Just Functional](https://openreview.net/forum?id=0Zri6HSYaK): Prompts LLMs as efficiency critiques during code generation, reducing execution time and memory on EffiBench and HumanEval+ without running candidate code.
- MiniMax M2 ([official report](https://www.minimax.io/news/minimax-m2); [interleaved-thinking note](https://www.minimax.io/news/why-is-interleaved-thinking-important-for-m2)): Frames a vendor model around agentic coding, tool use, and deep search, with interleaved thinking emphasized for reliable tool-mediated code work.
- Cursor Composer 2 ([technical report](https://cursor.com/blog/composer-2-technical-report); [PDF](https://cursor.com/resources/Composer2.pdf)): Reports an IDE-native agentic coding model trained with continued pretraining and large-scale RL in realistic Cursor sessions.
- Composer 2.5 ([official release](https://forum.cursor.com/t/composer-2-5-is-now-live/160934)): Updates Cursor's IDE-native coding model with a much larger synthetic long-horizon RL task mix and targeted textual-feedback RL for tool use, communication style, and effort calibration, making it a vendor evidence point for coding-agent post-training rather than a standalone benchmark.
- Kimi K2.6 ([official report](https://www.kimi.com/blog/kimi-k2-6); [model card](https://huggingface.co/moonshotai/Kimi-K2.6)): Focuses the open Kimi line on long-horizon coding execution, Kimi Code Bench, Terminal-Bench, SWE-Bench Pro, and agent-swarm coding tasks.
- [Automated Software Architecture Design Recovery from Source Code Using LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-032-02138-0_5): Uses LLMs to recover software-architecture design from source code, adding an architecture-comprehension model task for repositories.
- [HLSRewriter](https://doi.org/10.1145/3749986): Uses LLMs to refactor and optimize C/C++ code for high-level synthesis, extending code-model coverage to hardware-oriented optimization.
- [RECODE](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-981-95-0014-7_43): Uses reliable self-generated tests and fine-grained execution feedback to improve LLM code generation.
- [Optimizing Type Migration for LLM-Based C-to-Rust Translation: A Data Flow Graph Approach](https://doi.org/10.1145/3735544.3735582): Uses data-flow graph structure to improve type migration in LLM-based C-to-Rust translation, extending memory-safe code-transpilation methods.
- [CodeDoctor: multi-category code review comment generation](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10515-025-00491-y): Generates multi-category code-review comments, adding a model-side code-review assistance method that separates review feedback types rather than treating comments as one generic generation task.
- [Enhanced Prompting Framework for Code Summarization with Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3728949): Uses task-specific prompt design for LLM-based code summarization, improving the alignment between code structure and generated natural-language summaries.
- [Integrating Large Language Models into Automated Software Testing](https://doi.org/10.3390/fi17100476): Fine-tunes Codestral Mamba with LoRA for context-aware test-script generation over text-to-code and text-plus-code-to-code testing tasks.
- [Leveraging Search-Based and Pre-Trained Code Language Models for Automated Program Repair](https://doi.org/10.1145/3672608.3707774): Combines search-based repair with pretrained code language models, adding a model-side automated-program-repair method.
- [Improving Project-Level Code Generation Using Combined Relevant Context](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-031-97635-3_52): Combines relevant project context for code generation, adding another context-construction method for repository-scale coding models.
- [Fine Tuning Large Language Model for Secure Code Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3650105.3652299): Fine-tunes large language models to improve secure code generation.
- [CoLadder: Manipulating Code Generation via Multi-Level Blocks](https://doi.org/10.1145/3654777.3676357): Studies how multi-level code blocks can manipulate code generation.
- [Exploring Large Language Models for Hierarchical Hardware Circuit and Testbench Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3742430): Evaluates commercial and open code LLMs across hierarchical HDL circuit generation, paired testbench generation, and prompt-complexity choices rather than only single-module Verilog tasks.
- [Invited: Infusing EDA Knowledge into LLM Systems: An Information-Source Perspective](https://doi.org/10.1145/3764386.3779640): Reviews EDA knowledge-injection routes for LLM systems, contrasting finetuning on domain corpora, graph-based RAG over design documents, and multi-agent flows with environment feedback.
- [From Triumph to Uncertainty: The Journey of Software Engineering in the AI Era](https://doi.org/10.1145/3709360): Frames AI-era software engineering around changing roles, methods, and human creativity rather than introducing a new code model or training recipe.
- [RustC4++: Improving Rust Code-Comment Inconsistency Detection via Hybrid LLM and Static Analysis](https://doi.org/10.1145/3800689): Combines LLM constraint extraction, Rust-aware static analysis, and SMT bidirectional verification to detect comment-code mismatches over interval, boundary, existence, option, and finiteness constraints.
- [A Catalog of Data Smells for Coding Tasks](https://doi.org/10.1145/3707457): Systematically catalogs data smells and quality filters used in pretraining, finetuning, and testing datasets for LLM coding tasks.
- [HSG-RAG: Hierarchical Knowledge Base Construction for Embedded System Development](https://doi.org/10.1145/3731680): Builds an embedded-systems RAG knowledge base from hierarchical technical documentation, using knowledge graphs to preserve relationships among domain concepts before retrieval.
- [Delving into History: Retrieve Less but Augment More on Commit Message Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3794857): Introduces HisRag, which retrieves relevant historical commit messages and augments commit-message generation with repository- and developer-specific context.
- [LEDGE : Leveraging dependency graphs for enhanced context aware documentation generation](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10515-026-00596-y): Uses dependency graphs to supply context for documentation generation, making generated descriptions depend on code relationships rather than isolated snippets.
- [KerSpecGen: Co-piloting formal Kernel specification synthesis with refined knowledge graphs and large language models](https://doi.org/10.1371/journal.pone.0338821): Synthesizes formal microkernel specifications by linking requirements to specification properties with refined knowledge graphs, then fine-tuning property-to-code generation and templating complete specification programs.
- [Generating REST API Specifications through Static Analysis](https://doi.org/10.1145/3597503.3639137): Presents Respector, a static and symbolic analysis generator that infers OpenAPI-style endpoint methods, parameters, responses, and constraints directly from REST API source code.
- [RepoMinCoder: Improving Repository-Level Code Generation Based on Information Loss Screening](https://doi.org/10.1145/3671016.3674819): Adds information-loss screening and ranking after repository-context retrieval so prompts preserve code context that is likely to survive model encoding.
- [DeepVulHunter: enhancing the code vulnerability detection capability of LLMs through multi-round analysis](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10844-025-00982-0): Uses multi-round LLM analysis for vulnerability detection, emphasizing iterative inspection of code security signals rather than one-shot classification.
- [DiffCoder: Enhancing Large Language Model on API Invocation via Analogical Code Exercises](https://doi.org/10.1145/3643745): Trains code LLMs on differences between analogical code exercises to improve library-oriented API invocation across mono-library and cross-library benchmarks.
- [Refactoring Loops in the Era of LLMs: A Comprehensive Study](https://doi.org/10.3390/fi17090418): Empirically tests ChatGPT on refactoring Java for-loops into streams using 2,132 loops from open-source projects, separating feasible transformations from traditional precondition-based refactoring limits.
- [REFD:recurrent encoder and fusion decoder for temporal knowledge graph reasoning](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10489-025-06445-x): Proposes a recurrent encoder and fusion decoder for temporal knowledge-graph reasoning, an off-axis graph-reasoning model rather than a software-development code model.
- [BinQuery: A Novel Framework for Natural Language-Based Binary Code Retrieval](https://doi.org/10.1145/3728927): Retrieves binary functions from natural-language queries for reverse engineering, replacing heuristic binary search with language-guided function retrieval.
- [TestGPT-Server: Automatically Testing Microservices with Large Language Models at ByteDance](https://doi.org/10.1145/3696630.3728545): Describes a ByteDance microservice API testing system that uses LLM-enhanced request generation and bug detection for fully automated service tests.
- [Semantic-aware Source Code Modeling](https://doi.org/10.1145/3691620.3695605): Positions source-code modeling as learning statistical regularities in human-written programs for generation, bug repair, and program analysis.
- [Research on Code Generation Technology based on LLM Pre-training](https://doi.org/10.54097/scrwpt34): Surveys LLM pretraining-based natural-language-to-code generation and its role in lowering programming-language barriers for code practitioners.
- [LEGO-compiler: enhancing neural compilation through translation composability](https://doi.org/10.1007/s42514-025-00272-9): Improves neural compilation by composing translation units, treating source-to-target compilation as reusable translation pieces rather than one monolithic generation.
- [LLM-Driven Python-to-Rust Translation for Efficient and Safe Code for Neural Networks: llm4py2rs](https://doi.org/10.5220/0014383000004061): Applies LLM-driven Python-to-Rust translation to neural-network code, targeting safer and more efficient implementations of numerical workloads.
- [JIT-DCK: A KAN Multi-Task Model for Just-In-Time Code Defect Prediction and Localization](https://doi.org/10.1145/3801547): Uses a Kolmogorov-Arnold Network multi-task model to jointly predict defective code changes and localize defect lines from commit context and change features.
- [EVGen: Trajectory-conditioned forward-view video generation under minimal visual observations](https://doi.org/10.1007/s44443-026-00627-4): Generates autonomous-driving forward-view videos conditioned on ego-vehicle trajectories, making it a simulation and video-generation model outside this software-development model axis.
- [Pondering the Future of Chemical Research amid the Wider Adoption of Artificial Intelligence Technologies.](https://doi.org/10.1002/chem.202503630): Discusses AI adoption, automation, and structural changes in chemistry research, so it is a chemistry perspective rather than a coding-model contribution.
- [On-the-fly Generation-Quality Enhancement of Deep Code Models via Model Collaboration](https://doi.org/10.1145/3765752): Improves deployed deep code models at inference time through model collaboration, targeting hard generative inputs without retraining or finetuning.
- [Syntax-aware tokenizer for Go code style analysis](https://ceur-ws.org/Vol-4005/paper1.pdf): Designs a Go syntax-aware tokenizer for code-style analysis, using language structure as preprocessing rather than generic text tokenization.
- [Large Language Models for Serverless Function Generation: An Investigation on FaaS Performance](https://doi.org/10.1145/3774899.3775016): Compares large and locally hosted LLMs for generating serverless functions, measuring runtime behavior and cloud-hosting costs across benchmark, interview, and custom prompts.
- [Commentary Master: Exploring Fine-grained Video Action Commentary](https://doi.org/10.1145/3728423.3759414): Builds an AI commentator for long natural-language sports action commentary, an off-axis video-understanding and agent framework rather than software-development modeling.
- [Translating short-form Python exercises to other programming languages using diverse prompting strategies](https://doi.org/10.1093/gigascience/giaf149): Evaluates GPT-4 prompting strategies for translating short Python exercises into other languages for scientific programming and education.
- [Concept Siever : Towards Controllable Erasure of Concepts from Diffusion Models without Side-effect](https://openreview.net/forum?id=foq3J3WUm7): Introduces a diffusion-model concept-erasure method, which is off-axis for software-development code models despite being a model-side adaptation technique.
- [BlockGuardian: Intelligent Multi-Dimensional Blockchain Code Analysis Framework](https://doi.org/10.1145/3766918.3766926): Combines LLM semantic reasoning with adaptive parallel analysis to assess blockchain code structure, quality, vulnerabilities, and documentation completeness.
- [HinglishEval: Evaluating the Effectiveness of Code-Generation Models on Hinglish Prompts](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-031-84391-4_2): Evaluates code-generation models on Hinglish prompts, probing multilingual prompt robustness rather than adding a new training method.
- [Enhancing Static Analysis for Practical Bug Detection: An LLM-Integrated Approach](https://doi.org/10.1145/3649828): Presents LLift, which combines static analysis and LLM guidance to detect use-before-initialization bugs in the Linux kernel.
- [Measuring GitHub Copilot's Impact on Productivity](https://doi.org/10.1145/3633453): Measures Copilot productivity impact through user perceptions and usage data, making it an empirical adoption study rather than a model-side method.
- [Enhancing Software Code Vulnerability Detection Using GPT-4o and Claude-3.5 Sonnet: A Study on Prompt Engineering Techniques](https://doi.org/10.3390/electronics13132657): Compares concise, tip-setting, and step-by-step prompts for GPT-4o, Claude 3.5 Sonnet, and GPT-3.5 Turbo on SARD vulnerability detection across C++, Java, and Python.
- [iSMELL: Assembling LLMs with Expert Toolsets for Code Smell Detection and Refactoring](https://doi.org/10.1145/3691620.3695508): Assembles LLMs with expert code-smell tools so repository structure and dynamic analysis compensate for LLM context and analysis limits.
- [Type-migrating C-to-Rust translation using a large language model](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10664-024-10573-2): Targets C-to-Rust migration by prompting or guiding LLMs to replace C types with Rust ownership- and safety-oriented type constructs.
- [TM-fuzzer: fuzzing autonomous driving systems through traffic management](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10515-024-00461-w): Fuzzes autonomous-driving systems by manipulating traffic-management scenarios, an off-axis testing framework rather than a code-generation model.
- [Leveraging Large Language Models for Direct Interaction with SysML v2](https://doi.org/10.1002/iis2.13262): Explores using LLMs as a natural-language interface to SysML v2 models, leveraging the language's human-readable syntax for systems-engineering interaction.
- [Establishing Traceability Between Natural Language Requirements and Software Artifacts by Combining RAG and LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-031-75872-0_16): Combines retrieval-augmented generation with LLMs to recover traceability links between requirements text and related software artifacts.
- [Effectiveness of ChatGPT for Static Analysis: How Far Are We?](https://doi.org/10.1145/3664646.3664777): Tests ChatGPT on static bug detection and false-positive warning removal for null dereference and resource leak findings gathered with Infer.
- [Using model-driven engineering to automate software language translation](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10515-024-00419-y): Uses model-driven engineering for language translation, comparing it with machine-learning and hand-written rule approaches for porting software across programming languages.
- [Generating SPARQL Queries over CIDOC-CRM Using a Two-Stage Ontology Path Patterns Method in LLM Prompts](https://doi.org/10.1145/3708326): Prompts LLMs with two-stage CIDOC-CRM ontology path patterns so natural-language cultural-heritage questions map to long-path SPARQL queries.
- [LibvDiff: Library Version Difference Guided OSS Version Identification in Binaries](https://doi.org/10.1145/3597503.3623336): Identifies open-source library versions in binaries by using version-difference signals instead of relying only on strings or constant features.
- [Requirements Traceability Link Recovery via Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-031-88531-0_27): Applies RAG to requirements traceability-link recovery, retrieving candidate artifact context before LLM-based link judgment.
- [BatFix: Repairing language model-based transpilation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3658668): Repairs language-model transpilation outputs with program repair and synthesis, using original code, generated target code, and tests to produce translations that pass the suite.
- [Understanding Code Changes Practically with Small-Scale Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3691620.3694999): Builds the expert-validated HQCM dataset and fine-tunes 7B and 220M small language models for code-change understanding, comparing them with large LMs and traditional methods.
- [NL2CTL: Automatic Generation of Formal Requirements Specifications via Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-981-96-0617-7_1): Uses LLMs to translate natural-language requirements into CTL formal specifications for model-checking workflows.
- [Three Heads Are Better Than One: Suggesting Move Method Refactoring Opportunities with Inter-class Code Entity Dependency Enhanced Hybrid Hypergraph Neural Network](https://doi.org/10.1145/3691620.3695068): Detects Move Method refactoring opportunities with a hybrid hypergraph neural network over inter-class code-entity dependencies, addressing feature-envy cases beyond pairwise metrics.
- [Towards Big Data in AI for EDA Research: Generation of New Pseudo Circuits at RTL Stage](https://doi.org/10.1145/3658617.3697613): Generates RTL-stage pseudo circuits to expand EDA training data where proprietary circuit IP limits direct dataset sharing.
- [Generative Feedback for Code Learning: A Study on LLM-Driven Formative Assessment](https://doi.org/10.1145/3768421.3768464): Studies LLM-driven formative feedback in programming education, linking student interaction frequency with comprehension, self-regulation, and instructor workload changes.
- [Predicting Software Perfection Through Advanced Models to Uncover and Prevent Defects](https://doi.org/10.1049/sfw2/8832164): Compares logistic regression, random forests, SVMs, CNNs, and XGBoost for defect prediction after preprocessing, feature selection, and hyperparameter tuning.
- [Code Generation by Large Language Models: A Comparative Analysis of ChatGPT, Claude, and DeepSeek](https://doi.org/10.18178/ijeetc.15.1.19-28): Benchmarks GPT-4-turbo, Claude Sonnet, and DeepSeek-V3 on sorting-code generation, measuring execution time, memory use, file size, and readability in a Linux Docker setup.
- [Capturing the context-aware code change via dynamic control flow graph for commit message generation](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10994-024-06671-3): Models commit-message generation with dynamic control-flow graphs so code-change summaries reflect context-aware behavior rather than raw diffs alone.
- [Empirical Analysis of Data Sampling-Based Decision Forest Classifiers for Software Defect Prediction](https://doi.org/10.3390/software4020007): Evaluates decision-forest defect predictors under different data-sampling strategies, focusing on imbalanced software-module risk classification.
- [Scalable RL-based data generation and multi-resolution architecture for code-switched speech recognition: a high-performance computing approach](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11227-026-08256-4): Proposes RL-generated synthetic data and multi-resolution architecture for code-switched speech recognition, which is off-axis for software-development coding models.
- [Performance analysis of AI-generated code: A case study of Copilot, Copilot Chat, CodeLlaMa, and DeepSeek-Coder models](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10664-025-10776-1): Compares code generated by Copilot, Copilot Chat, CodeLlama, and DeepSeek-Coder in an empirical software-engineering performance study.
- [The End of Programming as We Know It: Envisioning Radical Re-Conceptualizations of Co-Coding with AI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3737609.3747093): Workshop position piece on speculative and HCI futures for AI co-coding, emphasizing interaction design and values rather than a new model or verifier.
- [Enhancing the ability of LLMs for spaceborne equipment code generation via retrieval-augmented generation and contrastive learning](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10515-025-00545-1): Combines retrieval-augmented generation with contrastive learning to adapt LLM code generation to spaceborne-equipment programming knowledge.
- [Resilient Software Design Through Cognitive-Aware Antipattern Propagation in 4+1 Architectural Views](https://doi.org/10.3390/app15179526): Models antipattern propagation across 4+1 architectural views with propagation graphs, economic simulation, and cognitive-load framing for resilient software design.
- [Automated Extraction of Research Software Installation Instructions from README Files: An Initial Analysis](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-031-65794-8_8): Analyzes README installation-instruction extraction for research software, a documentation-mining task better routed to software-engineering datasets or tooling than to model training.
- [Exploring Neural Network Structure Code Reuse in the Open‐Source Community for Improving Maintenance](https://doi.org/10.1002/smr.70090): Studies how neural-network architecture code is reused in open-source projects, targeting maintenance risks and clone patterns rather than proposing a code-generation model.
- [ODEL: An Experience-Augmented Self-Evolving Framework for Efficient Python-to-C++ Code Translation](https://doi.org/10.3390/app16031506): Uses an experience-augmented self-evolution loop to improve Python-to-C++ translation, reusing past translation feedback to refine later code conversions.
- [Developing RAGs for robot code generation](https://doi.org/10.1088/1757-899x/1342/1/012064): Builds a retrieval-augmented code-generation setup for robot programs so the LLM grounds generated robot code in task-specific examples and documentation.
- [VulSCC: image-based vulnerability detection with SPP-CNN and code large language model](https://doi.org/10.1186/s42400-026-00589-0): Converts source code into image-like representations and combines SPP-CNN features with code-LLM signals for vulnerability detection.
- [Data-Driven Transferable Modeling for Cross-Project Software Vulnerability Detection via Dual-Feature Stacking Ensemble](https://doi.org/10.3390/math14050780): Uses a dual-feature stacking ensemble to transfer vulnerability detectors across projects, combining heterogeneous software metrics for cross-project prediction.
- [Knowledge distillation-driven commit-aware multimodal learning for software vulnerability detection](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10515-026-00595-z): Distills commit-aware multimodal vulnerability signals so code changes and commit context jointly supervise a lighter detector.
- [Advancing research software engineering with AI: a research framework](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10515-026-00621-0): Research-framework candidate; outlines AI-assisted research software engineering as an agenda, not a concrete model, verifier, or training recipe.
- [A code-based multiple watermarking scheme for the preservation of authenticity of digital video](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11042-026-21509-x): Uses code-based multiple watermarking to preserve digital-video authenticity, an off-axis multimedia-security method rather than software-development model work.
- [Toward Bridging the Tool Gap: Equipping Large Language Models with Tools to Answer Programmers’ Questions](https://doi.org/10.1145/3759534.3762682): Equips LLMs with programmer-facing tools so answers to development questions can incorporate external code or project evidence instead of text-only recall.
- [CoEdPilot: Interactively Recommending Project-Wise Code Edits](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11390-025-5139-z): Recommends project-wide code edits interactively, modeling edit propagation across a repository rather than isolated single-file completions.
- [Optimizing Copyright Subject and Originality Rules for AI-Generated Software](https://doi.org/10.1145/3783669.3783752): Legal-policy analysis of copyright subject and originality rules for AI-generated software, not a reusable code-model method.
- [Enhancing code generation accuracy using fine-tuning and task-adaptive pretraining with domain-specific data augmentation](https://doi.org/10.4314/swj.v19i4.13): Combines task-adaptive pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and domain-specific data augmentation to improve code-generation accuracy.
- [A comparative study of Kolmogorov-Arnold networks and multilayer perceptrons for software defect prediction with SHAP analysis](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10586-025-05691-5): Compares Kolmogorov-Arnold networks with multilayer perceptrons for defect prediction and uses SHAP to interpret software-metric effects.
- [A Study on Framework for Adaptive Code Generation and Performance Optimization Based on Qwen](https://doi.org/10.5220/0014021500004918): Adapts Qwen for code generation and performance optimization, framing generation as an iterative optimization workflow rather than plain prompting.
- [DB-GPT: Large Language Model Meets Database](https://doi.org/10.1007/s41019-023-00235-6): Presents a database-oriented LLM system for natural-language database interaction, SQL generation, and data workflow assistance.
- [GrammarT5: Grammar-Integrated Pretrained Encoder-Decoder Neural Model for Code](https://doi.org/10.1145/3597503.3639125): Integrates programming-language grammar into a T5-style encoder-decoder so code pretraining and generation respect syntactic structure.
- [PyDex: Repairing Bugs in Introductory Python Assignments using LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1145/3649850): Uses LLM-based repair for introductory Python assignments, targeting student bugs with assignment context and repair feedback.
- [AceCoder: An Effective Prompting Technique Specialized in Code Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3675395): Specializes prompting for code generation through an AceCoder strategy that structures the generation request around programming-task constraints.
- [Exploring Automated Assertion Generation via Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3699598): Evaluates LLMs for automated assertion generation, measuring whether generated assertions capture program behavior for tests.
- [HAFix: history-augmented large language models for bug fixing](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10664-026-10833-3): Augments LLM bug fixing with historical project context so prior fixes and change patterns inform patch generation.
- [Combining Small Language Models and Large Language Models for Zero-Shot NL2SQL](https://doi.org/10.14778/3681954.3681960): Combines small and large language models for zero-shot NL2SQL, using a lightweight model to assist or constrain SQL generation by the larger model.
- [You Don’t Have to Say Where to Edit! jLED—Joint Learning to Localize and Edit Source Code](https://doi.org/10.1145/3712187): Jointly learns bug localization and source-code editing, removing the need for callers to provide the exact edit location before repair.
- [Bug Detection and Localization using Pre-trained Code Language Models](https://doi.org/10.18420/inf2024_124): Fine-tunes pretrained code language models to detect bugs and localize suspicious code regions in software.
- [Code Summarization without Direct Access to Code - Towards Exploring Federated LLMs for Software Engineering](https://doi.org/10.1145/3661167.3661210): Explores federated LLM code summarization where project code remains local and only privacy-preserving model updates or outputs are shared.
- [STRUT: Structured Seed Case Guided Unit Test Generation for C Programs using LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1145/3728970): Guides LLM unit-test generation for C programs with structured seed cases that expose input formats and expected behavioral partitions.
- [DeciX: Explain Deep Learning Based Code Generation Applications](https://doi.org/10.1145/3660814): Explains deep-learning code-generation applications by tracing model behavior and generated-code decisions for developer inspection.
- [Spotting Code Mutation for Predictive Mutation Testing](https://doi.org/10.1145/3691620.3695491): Predicts likely code mutations before full mutation testing, reducing testing cost by selecting mutation targets from code-change signals.
- [Increasing the Reliability of Software Systems Using a Large-Language-Model-Based Solution for Onboarding](https://doi.org/10.3390/inventions9040079): Uses an LLM onboarding assistant to surface project knowledge and reliability practices for developers joining a software system.
- [The Sustainability Face of Automated Program Repair Tools](https://doi.org/10.1145/3744900): Studies sustainability implications of automated program-repair tools, a tooling-impact analysis rather than a new repair model.
- [Model-based software sustainability analysis and improvement](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10270-026-01374-w): Applies model-based analysis to software sustainability and improvement planning, closer to software-quality modeling than LLM code generation.
- [Graph neural networks for precise bug localization through structural program analysis](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10515-025-00556-y): Combines graph neural networks with structural program-analysis features to localize bugs more precisely in code.
- [Implementation of Semantic Search in an Academic Repository Using Sentence-BERT and FAISS](https://doi.org/10.33395/sinkron.v10i2.15940): Implements Sentence-BERT plus FAISS semantic search for academic repositories, an information-retrieval system that is off-axis for software-development models.
- [Inteligencia artificial generativa para la enseñanza de matemáticas y programación en educación superior: Revisión sistemática](https://doi.org/10.31876/rcs.v32i2.45578): Systematic review of generative AI in higher-education mathematics and programming instruction, better routed to education survey coverage.
- [Escaping the latency trap: a length-aware scheduling research for sequence parallelism](https://doi.org/10.1117/12.3113829): Studies length-aware scheduling for sequence-parallel model serving, an infrastructure optimization rather than software-development code modeling.
- [When Generative AI Mixes Languages: Multilingual Users' Code-Switching Behavior in Human-LLM Interaction](https://doi.org/10.1145/3772363.3798492): Examines multilingual users' code-switching behavior in human-LLM interaction, an HCI multilingual-use study rather than a code model.
- [SQL Statement Generation Enhanced Through the Fusion of Large Language Models and Knowledge Graphs](https://doi.org/10.3390/electronics15020278): Fuses LLMs with knowledge graphs to improve SQL statement generation from natural-language database requests.
- [Mitigating LLM hallucinations in Text-to-SQL parsing with a self-refinement feedback loop and memory augmentation](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10844-026-01050-x): Adds self-refinement feedback and memory augmentation to reduce hallucinations in Text-to-SQL parsing.
- [AI-Powered Code Autocompletion and Bug Detection for Developers](https://doi.org/10.21275/sr25027114413): Describes a developer assistant that combines code autocompletion with bug-detection signals for IDE-style support.
- [From Scenario to Code: Structured Prompting for LLM-Based Unit Test Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3779657.3779658): Uses structured scenario prompts to turn software requirements into LLM-generated unit tests.
- [Utilizing Large Language Model for Conversational Information Seeking via Dual-Query Generation and Joint-Encoding](https://doi.org/10.1145/3742423): Uses dual-query generation and joint encoding for conversational information seeking, an IR method with no clear software-development modeling target.
- [Structure-Preserving Discrete Diffusion Models for Verified Code Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3785706.3785940): Uses structure-preserving discrete diffusion for code generation and couples generation with verification constraints.
- [AutoRuleSQL: Hybrid Text-to-SQL via Rule-Driven Fast Paths and LLM Bootstrapping](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746252.3761438): Combines rule-driven fast paths with LLM bootstrapping so Text-to-SQL generation can fall back to learned parsing when rules are insufficient.
- [Program Code Generation with Generative AIs](https://doi.org/10.3390/a17020062): Empirically compares generative AI systems for program-code generation across correctness, usability, and generated-code quality.
- [An Empirical Study of the Code Generation of Safety-Critical Software Using LLMs](https://doi.org/10.3390/app14031046): Evaluates LLM-generated code for safety-critical software, focusing on correctness and assurance concerns in high-risk code.
- [Need a Programming Exercise Generated in Your Native Language? ChatGPT's Got Your Back: Automatic Generation of Non-English Programming Exercises Using OpenAI GPT-3.5](https://doi.org/10.1145/3626252.3630897): Uses GPT-3.5 to generate programming exercises in non-English languages, targeting localized CS education content rather than general code models.
- [MFTCoder: Boosting Code LLMs with Multitask Fine-Tuning](https://doi.org/10.1145/3637528.3671609): Improves code LLMs through multitask fine-tuning across multiple code-intelligence tasks.
- [Studying the Quality of Source Code Generated by Different AI Generative Engines: An Empirical Evaluation](https://doi.org/10.3390/fi16060188): Empirically evaluates source-code quality from multiple generative engines, measuring generated-code attributes rather than introducing a new model.
