- [LLM-Generated Invariants for Bounded Model Checking Without Loop Unrolling](https://doi.org/10.1145/3691620.3695512): Generates loop invariants with LLMs so bounded model checking can avoid explicit loop unrolling.
- [Improved Program Repair Methods using Refactoring with GPT Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3626252.3630875): Combines GPT-based program repair with refactoring transformations to improve patch generation.
- [Towards LLM-augmented multiagent systems for agile software engineering](https://doi.org/10.1145/3691620.3695336): Sketches LLM-augmented multi-agent support for agile software engineering, better routed to agent-harness or process-tooling coverage.
- [Enhancing Large Language Models-Based Code Generation by Leveraging Genetic Improvement](https://doi.org/10.1007/978-3-031-56957-9_7): Applies genetic improvement to LLM-generated code so candidate programs are evolved toward better correctness or performance.
- [Large Language Model-based Test Case Generation for GP Agents](https://doi.org/10.1145/3638529.3654056): Uses LLMs to generate test cases for genetic-programming agents, supplying evaluation inputs for generated programs.
- [Analyzing Developer-ChatGPT Conversations for Software Refactoring: An Exploratory Study](https://doi.org/10.1145/3643991.3645082): Analyzes developer-ChatGPT refactoring conversations to characterize refactoring intents, prompts, and interaction patterns rather than proposing a model.
- [Integrating Generative AI into Legal Education: From Casebooks to Code, Opportunities and Challenges](https://doi.org/10.5204/lthj.3640): Legal-education discussion of generative AI and coding examples, off-axis for software-development model methods.
- [DSL-Xpert: LLM-driven Generic DSL Code Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3652620.3687782): Drives domain-specific-language code generation with LLMs, targeting generic DSL synthesis from high-level task descriptions.
- [A Knowledge Enhanced Large Language Model for Bug Localization](https://doi.org/10.1145/3729356): Enhances an LLM bug localizer with external software knowledge so suspicious locations are ranked with project-aware context.
- [Generative AI in Introductory Programming Instruction: Examining the Assistance Dilemma with LLM-Based Code Generators](https://doi.org/10.1145/3649165.3690111): Studies the assistance dilemma of LLM code generators in introductory programming, an education/HCI evaluation rather than a model-side method.
- [KareCoder: A New Knowledge-Enriched Code Generation System](https://doi.org/10.1145/3639478.3643076): Adds knowledge enrichment to code generation so generated programs can use retrieved or structured domain knowledge.
- [Coeditor: Leveraging Repo-level Diffs for Code Auto-editing](https://openreview.net/forum?id=ALVwQjZRS8): Trains code auto-editing from repository-level diffs, teaching the model to apply coherent edits across project context.
- [Why Do Software Practitioners Use ChatGPT for Software Development Tasks?](https://doi.org/10.1145/3696630.3731667): Surveys practitioner motivations for using ChatGPT in software work, better routed to human-factors or survey coverage.
- [Imperfect Code Generation: Uncovering Weaknesses in Automatic Code Generation by Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3639478.3643081): Empirically exposes failure modes in LLM code generation, including correctness and quality weaknesses of generated programs.
- [Leveraging Human Insights for Enhanced LLM-based Code Repair](https://doi.org/10.1145/3696630.3731672): Incorporates human repair insights into LLM-based code repair to guide patch generation beyond raw model suggestions.
- [SDCC: software-defined collective communication for distributed training](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11432-023-3894-4): Optimizes distributed training with software-defined collective communication, an ML-systems infrastructure item rather than software-development code modeling.
- [On Calibration of Pretrained Code Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3597503.3639126): Measures and improves calibration of pretrained code models so confidence better matches correctness on code tasks.
- [Exploring Reproducibility of Human-Labelled Data for Code-Mixed Sentiment Analysis](https://doi.org/10.63317/4rk7cfdtiy7k): Studies label reproducibility for code-mixed sentiment analysis, off-axis for software-development and code-model capability.
- [Towards Interpreting the Behavior of Large Language Models on Software Engineering Tasks](https://doi.org/10.1145/3639478.3639798): Probes LLM behavior on software-engineering tasks to identify interpretable patterns behind successes and failures.
- [Towards automatically identifying the co‐change of production and test code](https://doi.org/10.1002/stvr.1870): Models co-change between production and test code to predict when tests should evolve with code modifications.
- [Generating Multi-Part Autogradable Faded Parsons Problems From Code-Writing Exercises](https://doi.org/10.1145/3626252.3630786): Generates autogradable faded Parsons problems from code-writing exercises, targeting programming-education content generation.
- [CQLLM: A Framework for Generating CodeQL Security Vulnerability Detection Code Based on Large Language Model](https://doi.org/10.3390/app16010517): Generates CodeQL vulnerability-detection queries with an LLM so security rules can be produced from natural-language vulnerability descriptions.
- [Enhancing queries for code generation with reinforcement learning](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-025-21271-4): Uses reinforcement learning to improve code-generation queries, optimizing prompts or query formulations against generated-code outcomes.
- [SynergyBug: A deep learning approach to autonomous debugging and code remediation](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-025-08226-5): Uses deep learning for autonomous debugging and remediation, coupling bug detection with candidate code fixes.
- [CQPES: A GPU-Aided Software Package for Developing Full-Dimensional Accurate Potential Energy Surfaces by Permutation-Invariant-Polynomial Neural Network](https://doi.org/10.3390/chemistry7060201): Chemistry simulation software for potential-energy surfaces, clearly off-axis for software-development model coverage.
- [Symmetry-Aware Code Generation: Distilling Pseudocode Reasoning for Lightweight Deployment of Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.3390/sym17081325): Distills pseudocode reasoning into a lightweight model and uses symmetry-aware signals to support deployable code generation.
- [The Modular Imperative: Rethinking LLMs for Maintainable Software](https://doi.org/10.1145/3759425.3763392): Argues for modularity-oriented LLM design for maintainable software, a position or design-principles item rather than a concrete training method.
- [Executable Science: Research Software Engineering Practices for Replicating Neuroscience Findings](https://doi.org/10.1145/3736731.3746147): Discusses research-software engineering practices for replicating neuroscience findings, useful for RSE process coverage but not a model method.
- [Software Performance Engineering for Foundation Model-Powered Software (FMware)](https://doi.org/10.1145/3676151.3719357): Frames performance engineering problems for foundation-model-powered software systems, closer to software-performance methodology than model training.
- [Empowering Diversity by Building Inclusive Software Engineering Projects with Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.34190/icair.4.1.3023): Studies inclusive software-engineering projects with LLM assistance, a human-centered process topic rather than a reusable code model.
- [Exploring Code-Switching Dynamics in Urdu-English Multilingual ChatGPT Models: Patterns, Challenges, and Implications](https://doi.org/10.61506/02.00203): Analyzes Urdu-English code-switching in ChatGPT interactions, off-axis for software-development because "code" refers to language mixing.
- [Vehicle Domain-Specific Language: Unifying Modeling and Code Generation for Low-Code Automotive Development](https://doi.org/10.1145/3691620.3695282): Defines a vehicle DSL that unifies automotive modeling and code generation for low-code development.
- [Dynamic Scoring Code Token Tree: A Novel Decoding Strategy for Generating High-Performance Code](https://doi.org/10.1145/3691620.3695505): Introduces a dynamic-scoring code-token-tree decoding strategy to steer generation toward higher-performance code.
- [ChronoLLM: customizing language models for physics-based simulation code generation](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11044-026-10152-x): Customizes LLMs for physics-based simulation code generation with domain-specific simulation knowledge.
- [An Empirical Study on the Characteristics of Reusable Code Clones](https://doi.org/10.1145/3793251): Empirically characterizes reusable code clones, informing clone-aware maintenance rather than adding a model-side method.
- [A transformer-based stacked ensemble framework for software defect prediction](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10115-026-02696-3): Uses a transformer-based stacked ensemble for software defect prediction over project defect features.
- [Generating highly customizable python code for data processing with large language models](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00778-025-00900-4): Uses LLMs to generate customizable Python data-processing code from user requirements and data-workflow constraints.
- [Designing and Optimizing Alignment Datasets for IoT Security: A Synergistic Approach with Static Analysis Insights](https://doi.org/10.1145/3727582.3728687): Designs IoT-security alignment datasets using static-analysis insights to supervise safer code-security behavior.
- [Generating Code Tours Using Locally-Runnable LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1145/3696630.3731462): Generates code tours with locally runnable LLMs so repository explanations can be produced without cloud inference.
- [The Future of Software Engineering Beyond the Hype of AI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3743095.3743102): Position article on software engineering's future with AI, not a concrete model, verifier, or dataset contribution.
- [Fus: Combining Semantic and Structural Graph Information for Binary Code Similarity Detection](https://doi.org/10.3390/electronics14193781): Combines semantic features with structural graph information to detect binary-code similarity.
- [Infrastructure as Code and Observability Automation for Payment Systems in Cloud-Native Environments](https://doi.org/10.71465/fair526): Describes infrastructure-as-code and observability automation for payment systems, a DevOps practice entry rather than model-side work.
- [Towards combining chain-of-thought and code static analysis for buffer overflow vulnerability detection](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11219-025-09733-4): Combines chain-of-thought prompting with static-analysis evidence for buffer-overflow vulnerability detection.
- [A cross-language and cross-binary type approach to binary-source software composition analysis using BM25](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10207-025-01148-3): Uses BM25 over cross-language and cross-binary type information for binary-source software composition analysis.
- [Using Pre-trained Transformers to Detect Malicious Source Code Within JavaScript Packages](https://doi.org/10.18420/inf2024_40): Applies pretrained transformers to detect malicious source code inside JavaScript packages.
- [A Brief Analysis of the Progress and Trends in Software Defect Prediction Methods](https://doi.org/10.54254/2755-2721/99/20251769): Short trend analysis of software-defect prediction methods, better treated as survey/background material.
- [Pay Attention! Human-Centric Improvements of LLM-based Interfaces for Assisting Software Test Case Development](https://doi.org/10.1145/3672539.3686341): Studies human-centered interface improvements for LLM-assisted test-case development rather than a new model-side method.
- [How Do Large Language Models Perform in Deep Learning Code Generation? An Empirical Study](https://doi.org/10.1145/3816024): Evaluates eight LLMs on DeepEval, a 100-task deep-learning code-generation benchmark, analyzing syntax, semantic, and executability failures such as hallucinated APIs and tensor-shape mismatches.
- [Extending OCL for semantic representation of software systems](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11334-026-00639-3): Extends Object Constraint Language into AgileUML OCL so UML/OCL models can represent software application semantics for analysis or re-engineering.
- [Refactoring Object‐Oriented Software With ChatGPT: An Empirical Study](https://doi.org/10.1049/sfw2/3348017): Tests ChatGPT on 200 Java refactoring cases across five scenarios, finding high compilation rates but much lower intended-refactoring correctness than NetBeans.
- [DIR-SMOTE: a density-influence resampling framework for imbalanced code smell detection](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10515-026-00624-x): Uses density-influence resampling to address class imbalance in code-smell detection datasets.
- [Cyber Security and Software Engineering](https://doi.org/10.3390/app16104625): Broad security-software-engineering background; retained as survey/editorial context rather than a specific code-model contribution.
- [Causal modeling in software defect prediction: bridging expert knowledge and novice insight](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10515-026-00625-w): Applies causal modeling to software defect prediction, connecting expert causal assumptions with novice-facing defect-analysis insight.
- [AI-powered DevOps: the Software Industry Is Re-wiring the Pipeline](https://doi.org/10.1145/3772008.3772011): DevOps workflow candidate; discusses AI across the software production pipeline rather than a model-training contribution.
- [Hybrid Modal Decoupled Fusion for Stable Multilingual Code Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3773365.3773431): Uses hybrid modal decoupled fusion to stabilize multilingual code generation across programming-language modalities.
- [Enhancing Security in Software Design Patterns and Antipatterns: A Framework for LLM-Based Detection](https://doi.org/10.3390/electronics14030586): Uses LLMs plus security heuristics to detect risks in design patterns and antipatterns, targeting context-aware security analysis beyond static-analysis rules.
- [No Code, No Cloud: On-Device Mockup-to-Code with Lightweight Vision-Language AI](https://doi.org/10.1145/3742413.3789144): Converts UI mockups to code with a lightweight on-device vision-language model, avoiding cloud inference for visual frontend generation.
- [BugPrioritizeAI for multimodal test case prioritisation using bug reports, code changes, and test metadata](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-025-31851-z): Prioritizes tests by fusing bug reports, code changes, and test metadata, making regression testing a multimodal ranking problem.
- [A Knowledge Graph-Based Approach for Assisted PLC Code Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3804601.3804649): Guides PLC code generation with a knowledge graph so industrial-control code synthesis can use structured domain relationships.
- [On-demand generation of high-quality software engineering datasets using large language models and ontologies](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10515-026-00617-w): Uses LLMs and ontologies to synthesize software-engineering datasets on demand, supplying task-specific training or evaluation data.
- [Enhancing Parameter-Efficient Code Representations with Retrieval and Structural Priors](https://doi.org/10.3390/app16021106): RS-Rep combines structure-semantic retrieval, graph relative bias, and span-discriminative contrastive learning to improve PEFT code representations with about 5% trainable parameters.
- [Chain-of-Verification for Secure Code Generation Using Iterative Self-Review](https://doi.org/10.71465/ajbd3634): Iteratively generates, vulnerability-checks, revises, and re-verifies code, using self-review cycles to improve secure-code generation over single-pass prompting.
- [ccPASTpqc: Post Quantum Cryptography Migration of Programs Using Code Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3799830.3799836): Uses code language models to migrate programs toward post-quantum cryptography, targeting cryptographic API modernization.
- [Beyond prompting: the role of phrasing tasks in vulnerability prediction for Java](https://doi.org/10.1186/s42400-025-00476-0): Studies how zero-shot, chain-of-thought, and inverted prompt phrasing affect GPT and Llama vulnerability prediction for Java methods.
- [ValExtractor: Reliable Automated Local Variable Extraction in Code Refactoring](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11390-025-5133-5): Automates local-variable extraction in refactoring, targeting reliability for a specific refactoring operator.
- [Human-AI Collaboration in Data-Driven Software Engineering: A Seq2Seq Model with Attention for Automated Code Debugging](https://doi.org/10.56536/ijset.v3i1.62): Trains a Seq2Seq LSTM with attention on noisy code from Stack Overflow and CodeSearchNet to correct syntax and semantic errors.
- [Software Reuse in the Generative AI Era: From Cargo Cult Towards Systematic Practices](https://doi.org/10.1145/3755881.3755981): Practice and methodology candidate; reframes generative-AI software reuse from ad hoc copying toward systematic reuse processes.
- [Automatic test suite generation using bat algorithm](https://doi.org/10.1177/1088467x251395503): Uses a search-based bat algorithm with mutants and mathematical constraints to reduce generated test-suite size against GA, PSO, and ABC baselines.
- [Enhancing GUI test case generation with multi-objective quasi-oppositional genetic sparrow](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-025-31221-9): Generates GUI test cases with a multi-objective quasi-oppositional genetic sparrow search algorithm optimizing coverage, redundancy, and fault detection.
- [Toward Inferring Structural Semantics from Binary Code Using Graph Neural Networks](https://doi.org/10.1145/3733822.3764673): Uses graph neural networks to infer structural semantics from binary code for reverse engineering and binary understanding.
- [Improving the Multi-Class Classification of Non-Functional Requirements in Spanish: A Study of Dataset Balancing and Performance](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10664-025-10736-9): Evaluates dataset balancing for Spanish non-functional-requirement classification, comparing shallow classifiers across translated and balanced PROMISE-derived datasets.
- [Structure-Aware Multi-Stage Adaptation of Qwen-72B for Code Generation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3777730.3777795): Adds a structure-aware multi-stage adaptation recipe for code generation models.
- [PIONEER: improving the robustness of student models when compressing pre-trained models of code](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10515-025-00560-2): Improves robustness when compressing pre-trained code models into student models.
- [Decomposition then watermarking: Enhancing code traceability with dual-channel code watermarking](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10515-025-00561-1): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [Towards Privacy-Preserving Code Generation: Understanding and Mitigating Memorization in Code Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3696630.3731473): FSE paper on memorization in code LLMs, pairing privacy-risk measurement with mitigation strategies for safer code generation.
- [Towards Personalized and Contextualized Code Explanations](https://doi.org/10.1145/3708319.3733681): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for software-engineering agents and code models. Core idea: focus on towards personalized and contextualized code explanations as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [LLM Compiler: Foundation Language Models for Compiler Optimization](https://doi.org/10.1145/3708493.3712691): Adapts foundation language models to compiler optimization tasks, using program text and optimization context as model inputs for predicting optimization decisions rather than treating compilation as hand-written heuristics only.
- [Experimental Evaluation of Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning for Software Engineering Tasks](https://doi.org/10.1145/3722107): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for software-engineering agents and code models. Core idea: focus on experimental evaluation of parameter-efficient fine-tuning for software engineering tasks as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [LLM-based Co-Evolution of Configurable Software Systems](https://doi.org/10.1145/3744915.3748460): Uses LLMs for co-evolution of configurable software systems.
- [Hallucination-Resilient LLM-Driven Sound and Tunable Static Analysis: A Case of Higher-Order Control-Flow Analysis](https://doi.org/10.1145/3759425.3763378): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [An Empirical Study on Language Models for Generating Log Statements in Test Code](https://doi.org/10.1145/3759915): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [Enhancing Domain-Specific Code Completion via Collaborative Inference with Large and Small Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3770748): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [Code-Enhanced Cross-Perspective Bug Question Retrieval](https://doi.org/10.1145/3789667): Improves cross-perspective bug-question retrieval with code enhancement.
- [CoCoder_Repair: MISRA Code Rule Repair Based on Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3789982.3790042): An LLM-based method for repairing MISRA code-rule violations. Core idea: apply code-model repair to standards-oriented embedded-software quality constraints.
- [Using large language models to generate and correct code written in low-resource and domain-specific programming languages](https://doi.org/10.20935/acadai8228): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for software-development capability. Core idea: Introduction: Large language models (LLMs) are being used increasingly by programmers to generate code.
- [AUTOMATIZAÇÃO INTELIGENTE DE DOCUMENTAÇÃO DE SOFTWARE COM LLMS](https://doi.org/10.31512/gesto.v14i1.2447): Builds an on-premise Ollama and LangChain chatbot pipeline for software technical-documentation management, using two-stage processing to classify intents and preserve organizational data control.
- [Harnessing Large Language Models for Automated Software Testing: A Leap Towards Scalable Test Case Generation](https://doi.org/10.3390/electronics14071463): Uses LLMs for automated software testing and scalable test-case generation.
- [AI-Driven Augmented Software Engineering: Leveraging Cognitive Models for Enhanced Code Generation](https://doi.org/10.47852/bonviewjcce52026123): Integrates cognitive models with AI-driven code generation, refactoring, and bug fixing, evaluating the framework on code quality, productivity, usability, and adaptability.
- [LLMs as Code Generators for Model-Driven Development](https://doi.org/10.5220/0013580300003964): ICSoft paper evaluating LLMs as code generators inside model-driven development, connecting model artifacts to generated implementation code.
- [Comparing LLM-based and MDE-based code generation for agile MDE](): Comparing LLM-based and MDE-based code generation for agile MDE: A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for software-development agents and code intelligence. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [Exploring the Effect of Genetic Improvement for Large Language Models-Generated Code](https://doi.org/10.1007/s42979-025-04281-x): Studies genetic-improvement techniques for refining LLM-generated programs beyond one-shot generation.
